### 2026-10-17T12:02:57Z [**since2006/shell-rs**](https://github.com/since2006/shell-rs)

```
### Added  - Import hosts from ~/.ssh/config and ~/.ssh/known_hosts: open it from the host list’s “…” menu or the context menu of its empty space, preview the hosts, tick the ones to import, and change each one’s name, group, username and authentication. - Broadcast input: click the megaphone at the left of the tab bar and check a few terminals; they’re shown side by side, and the command bar above them sends a command to all of them. Turn on Sync keys to have typing in one go to the others too. - Windows portable version: unzip and run, with all its data in the data folder next to the program; it updates itself too.  ### Improved  - After a remote terminal disconnects, Space closes the tab; Enter still reconnects. - Less processor use in virtual machines without a graphics card (where the processor draws the interface): fewer frames while a terminal scrolls, a cursor that doesn’t blink, and no animations. - In the Chinese interface, What’s new opens the changelog in Chinese.  ### Fixed  - Terminal text looked blurry at display scaling such as 150%. - A terminal shown in a split popped up a reminder when its bell rang. 
```

```
## [6.7.0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.6.0...rea-agents-6.7.0) (2026-10-18)  ### Highlights  - Client configuration edits follow the duplicate-member semantics used by MCP clients.  ### Changes  - Includes the [6.6.0 changes and migration notes](https://github.com/morluto/rea/releases/tag/rea-agents-6.6.0). That version was tagged but was not published to npm.  ### Fixes  - **Client configuration:** Setup edits the last repeated JSON/JSONC member, matching the value clients actually read. Setup and uninstall refuse a removal that would reactivate a shadowed duplicate, preserving the original configuration. ([#4957](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4957), [morluto/rea#4952](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4952)). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @xu91102.  Full contribution record: [4 commits and 1 merged PR](https://github.com/morluto/rea/blob/rea-agents-6.7.0/docs/releases/6.7.0.contributions.json).  ## [6.6.0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.5.0...rea-agents-6.6.0) (2026-10-18)  ### Highlights  - Add Kuna as a caller-supplied static-analysis provider. - Query selected JavaScript facts and multiple APK locale tables without repeating the underlying analysis. - Extend Ghidra analysis to Windows ARM PE targets and explicit raw m68k images. - Preserve completed Evidence, cancellation reasons and retryable cleanup across provider and CLI finalization.  ### Changes  - **Kuna analysis:** Use a caller-installed Kuna for procedure listings, pseudocode, assembly, call relationships, exact-address xrefs and cached string searches. Project exports retain source spans and digests; repeated requests reuse completed work. Recorded real-provider coverage uses Kuna 1.833 on Linux; Windows, PE and universal Mach-O are unsupported. See [the provider guide](docs/kuna-provider.md). ([#3832](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3832), [#3854](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3854), [#4071](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4071), [#4095](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4095)). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0. - **Retained JavaScript facts:** Query exact application paths, selected properties and filtered module references through `inspect_analysis_view`, with paging and complete row Evidence. These views reuse completed analysis and preserve source selection and unknown coverage. ([#4265](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4265), [morluto/rea#4190](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4190), [#4097](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4097), [morluto/rea#4092](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4092)). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0. - **APK locale tables:** Request several resource locales in one decode with repeatable CLI `--locales` or MCP `locales`. Each table reports its status and coverage; existing single-locale fields remain available. ([#4804](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4804), [morluto/rea#4777](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4777)). Thanks @abhay-2108, @N0zoM1z0. - **Windows ARM binaries:** Ghidra admits ARMNT/Thumb-2 and ARM64 PE applications and DLLs on the Windows x64 host lane. Recorded Ghidra 12.1.4 verification uses `WindowsResourceReference` disabled and raised startup budgets; default-analyzer completion, ARM64EC/ARM64X and Windows-on-Arm hosts remain unverified or unsupported. See [the exact scope](docs/windows-ghidra-p0.md#native-authority-and-target-identity). ([#3965](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3965), [morluto/rea#2186](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/2186)). Thanks @N0zoM1z0, @WestLabsAI. - **Raw m68k images:** Analyze explicitly selected big-endian m68k raw binaries using Ghidra’s fixed 68000 profile and a caller-supplied aligned entry. Real CLI/MCP verification used Ghidra 12.1.4 on macOS arm64; Linux is unverified and Windows P0 does not admit m68k. See [mapping requirements](docs/raw-binary.md). ([#4018](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4018)). Thanks @icedac. - **JADX deadlines:** Set `REA_JADX_OPERATION_TIMEOUT_MS` to extend APK loading and upstream operation deadlines together. Doctor reports the effective value and setup forwards an explicit selection. ([#4466](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4466), [morluto/rea#3918](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3918)). ([d38f099](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d38f099692394ec90c582f9f0a14b036b421b160), [63696d2](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/63696d277328f556c971d1fc9f81ae83128d468b), [cc038da](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/cc038da218cada68c87700477aba3db4739791ff)). Thanks @GovinduSasanka, @peter-research. - **Android method bodies:** Recover original bytecode bodies when Java source is omitted while preserving genuine native/abstract body absence and primary failures during retryable cleanup. ([63c65a2](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/63c65a22430a4bbc0c1807a0298edb867775001f), [742b6f5](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/742b6f587f3c4ef1b8212e7376d6f3a305b194dc), [9fa6d2b](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/9fa6d2b254cc390665974efb52463386a029e9fc)). Thanks @balkan16, @Chessing234. - **ADB observations and transfers:** Preserve display-scoped focus, distinguish missing declarations from absent focus, retain numeric physical density and mark unknown permission coverage partial. Transfers use consumed snapshot identity and refuse unknown destination existence; uninstall requests explicitly select user zero. ([7086768](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/7086768a27242a15daa93486aefbaacc3c891e78), [3d028db](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/3d028dbc74a465f6f8df1813a931fbb5362e7dc3), [#4074](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4074), [morluto/rea#3942](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3942), [#4663](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4663), [#4665](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4665), [1342127](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/1342127e91d4db8b4113a7a4e85be909bc96e1db), [61e6880](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/61e688070f48016b6d178cd04c02fcc2f33e19ad), [be0b2d1](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/be0b2d138c46bb951fc3c8ee57ef7b0d57fed312)). Thanks @Copilot, @coygeek, @gongfpp, @Tapraj-Pathak, @yankikucuk. - **Apple metadata:** Preserve exact plist/keyed-archive integers, negative zero, non-finite real identity and dictionary keys. Reject malformed XML before value decoding, retain undecodable dates as unknown and report unsupported OpenStep entries explicitly. ([#4324](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4324), [morluto/rea#4292](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4292), [2cf9c28](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/2cf9c28f27261b7e64af2ba7ecb4e23ea7714aee), [#4839](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4839), [morluto/rea#4834](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4834), [f2b7bbb](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/f2b7bbb31279a820f4cf32d439570f8e5381d010), [6c98708](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/6c98708793c23615de8053b2cd14d9f164b960c5), [78cf610](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/78cf6100f33ad2412c0a3d60b83f8a44a43fbbb5), [c7e9e09](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/c7e9e096d3e2444e4c251123b8e914e9c0941f4e), [#3793](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3793), [#4105](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4105), [morluto/rea#3892](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3892)). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @mertkayacs, @pablodiazjorge, @pollychen-lab, @xu91102. - **Apple application structure:** Derive bundle components and launchd domains from their owning directories, retain unavailable components, and select only exact `Assets.car` names as asset catalogs. Interface Builder keeps unresolved classes and counts hierarchy records beyond the returned object page. ([149a0fc](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/149a0fc06c3c67ee45624e6da14dfb0754473758), [5d6029e](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/5d6029ea62a65ec34fd739ee40223c77c6fdb63b), [c192c58](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/c192c58815ddeaf5cf30159e7082c742c0bc36b7), [#4094](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4094), [morluto/rea#3914](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3914), [#4118](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4118), [morluto/rea#3958](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3958), [#4162](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4162), [morluto/rea#3950](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3950)). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Native names and layout:** Keep valid Objective-C records beside malformed neighbors, preserve leading U+FEFF and repeated load-command fields, and select exact arm64e.x1 slices. PE resource ownership and NativeAOT reads stay within declared image/header ranges. ([#3833](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3833), [morluto/rea#3336](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3336), [#4549](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4549), [morluto/rea#4540](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4540), [morluto/rea#4541](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4541), [#4730](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4730), [#4048](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4048), [morluto/rea#4008](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4008), [#3500](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3500), [morluto/rea#3389](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3389), [#4463](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4463), [morluto/rea#4373](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4373), [morluto/rea#4455](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4455), [3fc0075](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/3fc007534bb2104d662b43cc712281f9d1d6a7d3), [4a076f3](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/4a076f368c62bdfb19415accad342e03a38d1005)). Thanks @coygeek, @Iyanuoluwa007, @jayinvers, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @kevinjosh10, @maxazure, @ShuaixinHuang. - **Native analysis providers:** Keep Ghidra byte matches within examined coverage, retain PE PDB/function-boundary limitations and preserve configured MIPS choices. Attached IDA refuses universal Mach-O rather than silently choosing a slice; request timeout identity and captured output survive adapter handling. ([#4531](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4531), [#2933](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/2933), [morluto/rea#2259](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/2259), [01a1b3f](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/01a1b3fda6a7dacd24a0b10619d41d12153080d4), [50dd9fc](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/50dd9fcad1efb47cb404192c30bdf190dcdaca58), [#4765](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4765), [morluto/rea#3871](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3871), [c0cfccb](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/c0cfccb45fe43e7eeff18e56c64caa5b121988f9)). Thanks @BeLit-Development-2, @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @ShuaixinHuang. - **Recorded crashes:** Prevent GDB from discovering an executable named by an untrusted core, reject core mapping paths that escape the isolated sysroot, and preserve pwntools worker ownership across launcher exec. Completed decode facts and cancellation reasons remain available through failed final cleanup. ([5bf4fff](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/5bf4fffc1b835130ddb16f96db9ce13b639591db), [b33c0df](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/b33c0df02e93fabca00a44f2de625b1a63637ee9), [#4435](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4435), [morluto/rea#4011](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4011)). Thanks @coygeek, @N0zoM1z0. - **Cutter and Rizin:** Read Cutter’s active document from core configuration and attribute its host version correctly. Keep interrupted Rizin frames and output whitespace before completion markers, with exact command and cleanup failure attribution. ([#4507](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4507), [morluto/rea#4497](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4497), [#4514](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4514), [morluto/rea#4498](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4498), [#4459](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4459), [morluto/rea#4015](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4015), [d5fc7e6](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d5fc7e6ebf9af050081fc4c6e18b9079cfaa5cdc), [199396f](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/199396f61c8f61da326d059606a992304a760147)). Thanks @coygeek, @N0zoM1z0, @Xander-AJ. - **Windows cleanup:** Retain unpublished runtimes after failed removal, handle read-only attributes and sharing conflicts, preserve primary snapshot failures and honor caller environment overrides. ([f78d137](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/f78d137b42741a0ed5fb8ac161286f9a50220c4d), [d69acc3](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d69acc3455c33f90a2fc4381db43d38b6af50622), [0679a79](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/0679a7972b4f2b02eee6a86a44f50f4c2967d3cb), [5f9ac2c](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/5f9ac2c98a6fe11988c41afb737df9b6d5c3a1b3), [1bbf4bd](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/1bbf4bd16ca134cdb2187eaf29291cae90d14a57)). Thanks @DeryFerd. - **Managed metadata:** Compare nominal signatures and declaring identities across builds, preserve empty names without display substitutions, verify ABI-prefixed Mach-O exports exactly, and charge cached metadata once. Metadata errors report artifact file offsets; mixed-mode assemblies retain their actual boundary classification. ([#3059](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3059), [#3079](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3079), [#3853](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3853), [morluto/rea#3352](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3352), [#3974](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3974), [morluto/rea#3961](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3961), [#3491](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3491), [morluto/rea#3449](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3449), [#4418](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4418), [morluto/rea#4372](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4372), [#3516](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3516), [morluto/rea#3366](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3366)). Thanks @8rulerstar, @coygeek, @duckhunt113, @Hahaa909, @JayOfTheKeyboard, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @keeponlight, @N0zoM1z0, @tomyrms. - **Archive and filesystem identity:** Reject colliding extraction paths before writing, classify ZIP offsets beyond EOF as malformed, normalize ZIP current-directory prefixes once, and preserve symlink and unreadable-path provenance. A directory named `*.map` is no longer mistaken for a source-map file. ([862a0b2](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/862a0b2631785492f8dc20c8acd396646c545fd1), [c209dcb](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/c209dcb209118069f123e789949eeba1ef78853e), [6b1c36e](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/6b1c36e336eeacbb7159b211e687b4b84f99b9f5), [25c2222](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/25c22222abe7a681491bdb4d7316250fdcf32973), [d6e4fe4](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d6e4fe4a4dd8914ec3db50d2a7fc3d0802649262), [#4131](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4131), [morluto/rea#3909](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3909)). Thanks @Adkid-Zephyr, @coygeek, @jackie-cqz, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @maxazure, @XY-code1. - **Evidence and reconstruction:** Authenticate all retained inventory substeps, preserve source-attributed caveats in composed workflows, and require comparable cited Evidence before declaring reconstruction coverage or readiness. Missing Evidence and residual replay uncertainty stay explicit. ([9ac91e0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/9ac91e0c1e13e91cd21341635ad5be89b0e8642d), [c4608a2](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/c4608a29620ea3e7bb8259b47357b942fe4e4709), [#4075](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4075), [morluto/rea#3357](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3357), [#3526](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3526), [morluto/rea#3363](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3363), [153017d](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/153017d750322188c1a112b2284c1b8065c67320)). Thanks @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @rudycelekli. - **Offline data:** Preserve exact UTC packet-capture instants, SQLite column identity and snapshot admission failures, and cooked WASM directive literals. Cleanup cancellation retains completed EVM/WASM observations rather than losing them. ([796faa0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/796faa0e4e6bf745bcdc33acef65af8ccfc3622d), [748ac7f](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/748ac7f0df340f0d0569eca2e803a59cd51ffdbd), [bc51d0e](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/bc51d0ea5df7dcee1fcc42dfae3ab0c62c9cca5e), [4f87f02](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/4f87f02bb19bf77349a683a5bcb2da2d1da427d0), [d694aef](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d694aef227e90164e6654ea9e5ce6a854d099404), [321cacb](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/321cacb84a020f447e1110df5c58fa71d99b7bf1)). Thanks @himamaheswarreddy, @mertkayacs, @rollroyces, @viniciusdsandrade. - **Kuna recovery:** Share concurrent single-function requests and cache repeats, validate warm exports once, stream string inventories beyond the former fixed buffer ceiling, and keep unproven processes in their run slots until close confirms release. Failure messages distinguish final buffer refusals from configurable deadlines. ([#4045](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4045), [#4169](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4169), [#4049](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4049), [#4318](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4318), [#4052](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4052), [#4098](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4098), [#4343](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4343)). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Flutter snapshots:** Check symbol-table ranges against loadable segments and retain completed inspection facts when cancellation occurs during snapshot cleanup. ([#3859](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3859), [morluto/rea#3346](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3346), [#4766](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4766), [morluto/rea#4717](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4717), [#4762](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4762), [morluto/rea#4640](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4640)). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Native metadata recovery:** Keep unknown Objective-C category classes, empty protocol names, undecoded Swift aliases and unfinished property types explicit instead of discarding neighboring valid records. Preserve unnamed segment identity and exclude a dylib’s own install name from dependencies. ([#4093](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4093), [morluto/rea#4012](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4012), [#4078](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4078), [morluto/rea#3873](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3873), [#4085](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4085), [morluto/rea#3934](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3934), [#3825](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3825), [morluto/rea#3345](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3345), [#4051](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4051), [morluto/rea#3875](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3875), [#4050](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4050), [morluto/rea#3872](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3872)). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Document selection:** Preserve explicitly selected plist paths and bundle defaults, and only use document fallback after confirming its applicability. Finalization keeps completed selection facts and cancellation outcomes. ([e6c4877](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/e6c487799bb64f559e66b69be1863e6ec5187548), [#3774](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3774), [9df559b](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/9df559b7e712298a761ff26e9caae8d659b7e54b)). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Capture and comparison accuracy:** Admit schema-valid HAR numeric values while retaining exact-publication limits, preserve empty URL path segments, and keep unrelated process traces and unnamed reference endpoints distinct or unknown. ([#3159](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3159), [morluto/rea#3145](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3145), [#3792](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3792), [morluto/rea#3307](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3307), [#4178](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4178), [morluto/rea#3931](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3931), [#3809](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3809), [morluto/rea#3388](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3388)). Thanks @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @pablodiazjorge, @xu91102. - **Evidence publication:** Recheck staged output identity after its final write, classify missing review Evidence and duplicate ledger entries as caller input, and preserve the selected freshness requirement in comparison parameters. ([#4764](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4764), [morluto/rea#4613](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4613), [#4452](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4452), [morluto/rea#4444](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4444), [#4065](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/4065), [morluto/rea#4000](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/4000)). Thanks @amyanger, @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @Rinkia, @Swatantra-66.  ### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES  - **Saved Evidence:** Evidence and analysis snapshots now require explicit nullable `relationship_coverage` in their identity commitments. Artifact inventories require scoped coverage and explicit `integrity_contradictions`; obsolete `coverage_status` and prose-based coverage inference are removed. Regenerate old Evidence/snapshots and inventory observations with this release: saved files are not migrated and omitted facts are not filled with guessed defaults. ([db8128d](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/db8128dc0d1c4f731f3d19877fa00d25cf17f657), [0405c9d](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/0405c9d361de6d904dfa16cc0b6d8350407f2c9b), [a9588be](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/a9588be4ebdd20eeda975cb63ffcdb1cd868b340)). - **Browser capture results:** Consumers must retain resolved capture limits, event families and omission facts. Set desired budgets through the supported capture options; omitted caller inputs still select defaults, but stored observations must report the resolved values. Admitted content reads queue behind bounded concurrency. ([57a23f0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/57a23f0a2da0fdc3835e203718ded8acea5a34e6)). - **Android version codes:** `inspect_adb_package.version_code` is a decimal string rather than a JSON number. Read it as a string, or use `BigInt` for numeric comparison. ([#3485](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3485), [morluto/rea#3368](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3368), [7666a48](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/7666a482b69dc7cabdbac3bbaf89a43e32300192)). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Native UI failures:** Failed or cancelled native UI scenarios now return typed errors with the collected report in `partial_observation`. Handle the error path and read that report instead of expecting a successful result containing failed steps. ([d909919](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d9099190ab3a819bd3a9fd62a8714cef398016d5)). - **Provider status:** `binary_session` candidate availability can be unknown during owner finalization. Preserve that state rather than treating it as unavailable; internal consumers must await the asynchronous session status API. ([150e996](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/150e996a86f2bec4982ba87f37bf502a8d5e8214)). - **Swift symbol locations:** `swift_symbols` entries may have a null `address` when the provider supplies no location. Handle unknown addresses; optional `evidence.reported_address` retains the original provider value. ([8116dc0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/8116dc06512b4609d8db63cebc9f2ff8cb964151)). - **Linux Hopper admission:** Older builds previously admitted by a fixed allowlist need retained publisher-signed package evidence or an approved update to the current public demo. Update explicit `HOPPER_LAUNCHER_PATH` selections when migrating; the live feed offers only its current release. The official RPM metadata mismatch remains unresolved and is refused. ([#3590](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/3590), [morluto/rea#3577](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/3577), [70cd4cb](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/70cd4cbfb849b61f05d97d2690372a9782831426)). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.  Full contribution record: [571 commits and 236 merged PRs](https://github.com/morluto/rea/blob/rea-agents-6.6.0/docs/releases/6.6.0.contributions.json).
```

```
- 设置导航里 OpenAI 只留一个入口（调度组）。去掉「白名单」页和 `persona_rules` 整套：网关不再按末轮 user 正则追加防泄漏 / 身份 / 无工具提示，park 与 overlay 只带 `persona_standing`，overlay 模板去掉 `{{rules}}`。保存协议时 `routing.json` 里的 `persona_rules`、`persona_leak_append` 一并删除。 - 设置 → 遥测重做：槽位分布条、每槽实时旁路状态（运行中的槽每 20 秒探测）、按槽开关，旁路重启失败单独提示；展开可看上传时间线（批次、GrowthBook、失败）和脱敏原始记录。「上传中」要求 `worker.json` 遥测生效且进程在跑；种子开但没有官方身份显示「开 · 无官方身份」，集群槽注明。全开 / 全关先确认并逐槽显示进度，GPT 槽不参与批量写入。附播种写入键对照、上传链路说明，以及「换票后同步遥测」「Codex analytics」的当前值和跳转。 - 遥测上传间隔可调：`routing.telemetry.upload_interval_seconds`，整分钟、1 分钟到 24 小时，默认 10 分钟（原固定 10 秒）。设置 → 遥测可按分钟或小时设置，保存后重写各 Claude 槽 `worker.json`，旁路热重载，不重启；集群槽改为排队推送到节点。GrowthBook 仍是 6 小时，10 分钟会话窗口不变。 - 遥测旁路读官方身份时不再要求 `machineID`。当前 CLI 初装只写 `userID`，这类槽原先一直卡在 `waiting_official_identity`，不上传也没有记录。`.claude.json` 都缺失时，改从槽记录的 `fingerprint.official_user_id` 取身份。GPT 槽不写 Claude 遥测身份。  已部署机升级：更新 Node 控制面、`web/dist` 与 `bin/kin-worker`，重启一次 Node。无迁移、无新依赖，不必 `wrap-cli/sync`。上传间隔要新的 `kin-worker` 才生效：本机槽需重启遥测旁路（fleet `action=telemetry`）；集群槽在下次槽位启动、重载或出口重建时推送新的 `kin-worker`。旧旁路忽略该设置，仍每 10 秒上传。卡在 `waiting_official_identity` 的本机槽，播种或保存一次种子后 `worker.json` 会写入身份。不要覆盖 `routing.json`、`vms/`、`data/`、`.env`。  完整变更记录：[CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/dofastted/vm2api/blob/v1.3.207/CHANGELOG.md) 
```

### 2026-10-21T02:40:09Z [**yetone/magpie**](https://github.com/yetone/magpie)

```
## Features - ZCode now installs plugin 0.1.20 and supports selecting a team plan alongside your account plan. - Providers that can search only some of their models are now offered as search providers limited to those models.  ## Improvements - Improved memory use when reading cached session calls. - Claude Code usage no longer performs unnecessary desktop-root path calculations. - Packed blocks now keep their actual compressed bytes instead of reserving a worst-case buffer. - Settings now labels the gateway row as an address and clarifies that only the port is typed there. - Allowance cards now appear in the same order as the Providers page. - Hiding accounts now applies throughout the window, including Providers and its editor.  ## Bug Fixes - Fixed the endpoint shown for providers that use their own decision models (e.g., OpenRouter, Ollama). - Fixed group naming in live view and Usage so found groups appear as `auto/<model>` instead of `group/auto-<model>`.  ### Install  Download from [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai), or `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`. An installed magpie updates itself.  - **macOS (Apple Silicon):** `magpie-darwin-arm64.dmg` - **macOS (Intel):** `magpie-darwin-amd64.dmg` - **Windows:** `magpie-windows-amd64.exe` (ARM: `magpie-windows-arm64.exe`) - **Linux:** `magpie-linux-amd64` / `magpie-linux-arm64` (needs GTK 3 and WebKitGTK 4.1) - **Terminal only:** `magpie-cli-<os>-<arch>`  <!-- lang:zh -->  ## 功能 - ZCode 现在会安装 0.1.20 版插件，并支持在选择账户套餐的同时选择团队套餐。 - 仅能搜索部分模型的提供商现在会作为限定于这些模型的搜索提供商提供。  ## 改进 - 改进了读取缓存会话调用时的内存占用。 - Claude Code 使用过程中不再执行不必要的桌面根路径计算。 - 打包块现在保留其实际压缩字节，而不是预留最坏情况缓冲区。 - 设置中现在将网关行标记为地址，并说明此处仅输入端口。 - 额度卡现在按与 Providers 页面相同的顺序显示。 - 隐藏账户现在会应用于整个窗口，包括 Providers 及其编辑器。  ## 错误修复 - 修复了使用自有决策模型的提供商（例如 OpenRouter、Ollama）所显示的端点。 - 修复了实时视图和 Usage 中的分组命名，使找到的组显示为 `auto/<model>` 而不是 `group/auto-<model>`。  ### 安装  从 [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai) 下载，或运行 `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`。已安装的 magpie 会自动更新。    --- The same release, and the feed magpie updates from: https://github.com/yetone/magpie-releases/releases/tag/v0.1.1307 
```

```
# Vetta 0.5.64  发布日期：2026-10-14  ## 改进  - 默认主题（mono）调整背景色、次级色与浅色边框色：深色模式背景色改为 `#0f0f0f`、次级色（secondary）改为 `#171717`；浅色模式背景色改为 `#faf9f9`、次级色（secondary）改为 `#f1f0f0`；同时适度加深浅色模式下的边框与输入框颜色（调整为 `rgb(228, 228, 228)`），避免在浅色画布底色上过白发淡，界面边界与色块层级更分明。 - 优化消息列表 Agent 回复完成后的步骤折叠交互与样式：折叠触发文案调整为「已完成」，进入会话时中间步骤默认保持合上，字号与正文对齐并采用粗体（font-bold）亮色显示，下方附有微透明的长虚线下划线且去除了底部分割线，并增加合理的底部留白；展开后过程步骤具有清晰左边距，左侧以垂直细线贯穿连接顶部的「已完成」与底部的「完成」指示节点，流程层级更规整连贯。 - 项目详情页全面采用去线留白设计：项目指令支持「预览」与「编辑」双视图自由切换，预览排版与标题左右端精确对齐，编辑区 textarea 高度由原来的 13rem 增加至 32rem（支持自由拉伸）；标签「项目指令」与文件名分行清晰排布；当项目下无会话时自动隐藏最近会话区块。 - Vetta 现在会同时读取同一目录中的 AGENTS.md 与 CLAUDE.md 项目规则；两者冲突时以 AGENTS.md 为准，项目目录规则优先于父目录。 - 回复过程中触发的自动上下文压缩不再用一条分割线把回复截成两段，而是作为这条回复里的一个步骤显示：压缩进行中显示转动的进度，完成后标注压缩前的上下文大小，点开可以查看压缩生成的摘要；回复完成后它和其他步骤一起折叠。手动执行的压缩仍以分割线显示在两轮对话之间。  ## 修复  - 修复 Agent 回复过程中排队的消息被自动发出后，离开再回到该会话时，这些消息全部堆到列表末尾、后续回复被并进第一条回复的问题；现在重新进入会话与一直停留时的显示一致。 - 修复回复过程中发生上下文压缩后，同一条回复下方同时出现两个「Vetta 正在回复」等待提示的问题。 
```

### 2026-10-17T09:09:24Z [**Kuddev/pebrel**](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel)

```
# Pebrel 2.2.2  ## English  This release updates Windows, macOS and Linux. Android Preview remains at [2.2.1](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/releases/tag/v2.2.1).  ### Added  - Added: The file tree keeps the active ancestor folder visible while scrolling. Its clickable breadcrumb shows the last three path levels and provides a menu for earlier ancestors. - Added: Git history shows author avatars when available, with initials while an image is unavailable or cannot load.  ### Improved  - Improved: The interface bundles the compact Geist variable font for English text. Chinese uses installed font fallbacks without adding a bundled Chinese font; terminal font preferences remain independent, and paths and other metadata retain their monospace styling. - Improved: File filtering uses a softer search field, a clearer Filter files placeholder and quieter case, whole-word and regular-expression controls. Breadcrumbs and terminal actions provide stronger text or icon color feedback on hover with transparent backgrounds. - Improved: Adjacent choices share a smooth sliding indicator across Settings, SSH controls, Shell and Quick Jump filters, Git views, theme previews and import/export choices. The indicator follows actual option sizes, continues from its visible position when reversed and respects reduced-motion preferences. - Improved: The Shell and SSH picker uses a shorter search header and more space above its filter choices. - Improved: Command Manager shows Copy, Edit and Delete on row hover or keyboard focus. The three-dot button is removed; moving commands between folders remains available from the row context menu. - Improved: Git added and deleted line totals of 1,000 or more use one-decimal k units while retaining their signs and colors. - Improved: Tab titles, filenames and settings labels use more consistent text weights and spacing. Tab text follows the existing interface-size preference, iconless rows avoid an empty icon gutter, and shell types use plain text labels. - Improved: Captured-answer reader controls, notices, image selection and image errors follow the interface language. Switching languages preserves the original answer and author-provided image descriptions, and Copy source retains the exact original content. ([#545](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/545))  ### Fixed  - Fixed: Git history lines join across adjacent rows and use smoother branch curves. - Fixed: Git-ignored file and folder names retain their italic styling, and ordinary interface labels avoid unintended bold Chinese text. - Fixed: A tab's custom color strip stays separate from its icon at small interface sizes. - Fixed: Windows recreates a missing temporary welcome script when opening a new tab or split, and retries failed writes instead of keeping a stale cached path. Addresses [#622](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/622). - Fixed: On macOS, default GPUI shortcuts release plain Control character combinations such as Control+K to the terminal while Command shortcuts perform the corresponding interface actions. Explicit user bindings and navigation combinations retain their established behavior. Addresses [#656](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/656). ([#658](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/658)) - Fixed: Local file links with trailing line-count annotations, including terminal-truncated annotations, resolve to an existing base file. Existing filenames containing parentheses still take precedence; this does not add line-number navigation. Addresses [#619](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/619). ([#663](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/663))  ## 中文  本版更新 Windows、macOS 和 Linux；Android Preview 保持为已发布的 [2.2.1](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/releases/tag/v2.2.1)。  ### 新增  - 新增：文件树滚动时将当前祖先目录保持在顶部；可点击的面包屑显示末尾三级路径，更早的祖先目录通过列表菜单访问。 - 新增：Git 历史在可用时显示作者头像，图片暂不可用或加载失败时显示姓名首字母。  ### 改进  - 改进：内置体积较小的 Geist 可变字体用于英文界面；中文使用本机字体回退，不增加内置中文字库。终端字体偏好保持独立，路径等辅助信息继续使用等宽字体。 - 改进：文件过滤框采用更柔和的颜色和清晰的“过滤文件...”占位提示，大小写、全词及正则选项的默认颜色更克制；路径和终端操作按钮在悬停时通过更明显的文字或图标颜色反馈，背景保持透明。 - 改进：设置、SSH、Shell 与 Quick Jump 筛选、Git 视图、主题预览及导入导出的紧邻选项共用平滑滑动指示器；浮块跟随真实选项尺寸，快速反向时从当前位置继续，并遵循减少动态效果设置。 - 改进：Shell 和 SSH 选择器调矮搜索栏，并增加筛选选项上方的留白。 - 改进：命令管理器中的复制、编辑、删除操作在整行悬停或键盘聚焦时显示；移除三个点按钮，命令分组移动继续通过行右键菜单访问。 - 改进：Git 增删行数达到 1,000 时使用保留一位小数的 k 单位，并保留正负号及原有颜色。 - 改进：统一 Tab 标题、文件名及设置标签的字重和间距；Tab 文字继续读取原有界面字号偏好，无图标行不保留空白图标槽，Shell 类型使用纯文字标签。 - 改进：完整回答阅读器的控件、提示、图片选择和图片错误跟随界面语言；切换语言保留回答原文及作者提供的图片说明，“复制原文”保留完整原始内容。 ([#545](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/545))  ### 修复  - 修复：Git 历史线路在相邻行之间连续连接，并采用更平滑的分支曲线。 - 修复：Git 忽略的文件和文件夹名称保留斜体，普通界面标签避免出现意外加粗的中文。 - 修复：较小界面字号下，Tab 的自定义色条与图标保持间距。 - 修复：Windows 新建标签或分屏时会重建丢失的临时欢迎脚本，写入失败后可重试，避免持续使用失效的缓存路径。对应 [#622](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/622)。 - 修复：macOS 的默认 GPUI 快捷键将 Control+K 等纯 Control 字符组合交给终端，使用 Command 快捷键执行对应界面操作；显式用户绑定及导航组合保留原有行为。对应 [#656](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/656)。 ([#658](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/658)) - 修复：带有尾随行数说明的本地文件链接可回退到实际存在的基础文件，也兼容终端截断后的说明；真实带括号的文件名仍优先匹配，本次不增加行号导航。对应 [#619](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/issues/619)。 ([#663](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/663))  ## Contributors  <p>   <a href="https://github.com/Aschenbath"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/184111764?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Aschenbath" title="Aschenbath" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/YinBuLiao"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/35324248?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="YinBuLiao" title="YinBuLiao" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/galact-byte"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/94279960?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="galact-byte" title="galact-byte" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/Kuddev"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/292709043?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Kuddev" title="Kuddev" /></a> </p>  Thanks to Aschenbath ([#545](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/545)), YinBuLiao ([#658](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/658)), galact-byte ([#663](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/663)) and Kuddev for the fixes, interface improvements and release integration.  感谢 Aschenbath（[#545](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/545)）、YinBuLiao（[#658](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/658)）、galact-byte（[#663](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/663)）和 Kuddev 对修复、界面改进及发布整合的贡献。  ## SHA256  - `Pebrel-v2.2.2-linux-x64-preview.AppImage`: `7ea76a93d49724eba09c5ba1a59b88e52047d937bca1107caa48b13b64248781` - `Pebrel-v2.2.2-linux-x64-preview.deb`: `47fbd6e31657dfab0621f4f48e3faee10488f3c5b9964382fcfda8b205bed765` - ### 2026-10-17T19:59:43Z [**anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop**](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop)
```

```
# 中文  **稳定版更新公告：从下个版本开始，稳定版将不再更新，并由 DSH NEXT 取代。**  本次更新新增桌面端开发者日志和编辑右键菜单，统一插件管理入口，完善市场来源配置，并修复任务通知、界面恢复、本地路径引用和 Windows 更新安装等问题。  DSH Desktop 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  **上游版本：** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.2](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.1-rc.2)。  ## 新增与改进  - **开发者日志:** 新增可选开启的桌面端开发者日志，覆盖 Host 与界面进程，关联记录模型、工具和会话活动、通信耗时、进程输出及界面错误；日志保留有容量限制，并纳入诊断导出，不记录聊天正文或工具输入／输出。 - **启动诊断:** 补充认证、页面加载、DOM 就绪及 preload 失败记录；未开启开发者日志时也保留关键启动诊断，界面启动健康检查等待时间延长至 120 秒。 - **编辑右键菜单:** 可编辑区域提供撤销、重做、剪切、复制、粘贴和全选的原生右键菜单；选中的只读文本提供复制。Windows 菜单文字随应用语言切换。 - **插件控制入口:** 插件页集中提供插件市场和手机远控的管理入口，市场选择会被记住；桌面设置中的原有入口改为引导至插件页。 - **插件启停:** 改进插件市场和手机远控的启用／停用流程，支持无需重启整个应用即可生效。 - **市场来源配置:** 新增来源配置入口，可启用实验性的“自定义插件市场”、配置或移除插件源；当前预置可配置源为 dsh-market。配置操作按需预加载索引。 - **首次设置:** 插件市场步骤新增“自定义插件市场”选项，选择后自动启用对应插件并配置 dsh-market 源。  ## 修复  - **Windows 沙箱命令:** 改善执行沙箱命令时弹出控制台窗口、抢占焦点的问题。 - **插件信息:** 修复插件组件名称、说明和图标未正确显示的问题，完善桌面 Profile 的插件包信息查询。 - **旧版目录清理:** 修复清理旧版 Profile 模块链接时可能误删旧安装目录文件的问题。 - **后台任务通知:** 前台命令完成或失败、关闭会话时取消的任务不再误报“后台任务完成／失败”；真正转到后台运行的任务仍可正常通知。 - **界面恢复:** 修复兼容模式下页面已销毁、自动恢复耗尽后，显示或关闭窗口触发异常并导致整个应用退出的问题。 - **本地路径引用:** 修复拖入文件夹无法使用的问题；拖入、粘贴或选择的本地文件及文件夹可正确解析为输入框中的 @ 路径引用。 - **侧栏浏览器:** 让其他 Profile 默认启用右侧栏浏览器，同时保留用户在 Profile 中显式设置的开关。 - **深色模式下拉菜单:** 修复 Windows 和 Linux 深色模式下，下拉列表白底白字、选项难以辨认的问题。 - **Windows 更新安装:** 兼容旧版更新器传入的静默与隐藏参数，让更新安装进度窗口正常显示。 - **插件页状态:** 修复返回已安装插件页时开关重复进入加载、短暂禁用或闪动的问题，保留已读取的状态并在后台刷新。  ## 下载  从本页 **Assets** 下载对应平台的安装包：  | 平台 | 文件 | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-x64-Setup.exe` | | Windows x64 · 便携版 | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-x64-Portable.zip` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-linux-x64.tar.gz` |  可使用本页 `SHA256SUMS.txt` 核验全部安装包。  ## 使用提示  - 请先退出旧版应用并备份 `~/.dsh`，安装后验证常用插件和工作流程。 - 如需通过应用内终端管理 `desktop` Profile 的插件，请先启动 Desktop 完成该 Profile 的初始化。 - Windows 使用不透明窗口；Linux 仅提供兼容模式。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.17...v2.0.18) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  ---  # English  **Stable edition update notice: Starting with the next release, the Stable edition will no longer receive updates and will be replaced by DSH NEXT.**  This release adds Desktop developer logging and a native editing menu, unifies plugin controls, introduces market source configuration, and fixes task notifications, renderer recovery, local path references, and Windows update installation.  DSH Desktop is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  **Upstream version:** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.2](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.1-rc.2).  ## New and improved  - **Developer logging:** Added opt-in Desktop developer logging across Host and renderer processes for correlated model, tool, and session activity, transport timing, process output, and renderer errors. Logs have bounded retention and are included in diagnostic exports without recording chat text or tool inputs/outputs. - **Startup diagnostics:** Added authentication, page-load, DOM-readiness, and preload-failure records, retaining key startup diagnostics even when developer logging is disabled. The renderer boot health deadline is now 120 seconds. - **Editing context menu:** Added a native editing menu with Undo, Redo, Cut, Copy, Paste, and Select All, plus Copy for selected read-only text. Windows labels follow the application language. - **Plugin controls:** The Plugins page brings together market and mobile remote-control management, remembers the selected market, and replaces the old Desktop Settings controls with a link to Plugins. - **Live plugin toggles:** Improved market and mobile remote-control toggles so changes can take effect without restarting the entire application. - **Market source configuration:** Added source configuration for enabling experimental Custom Plugin Markets and configuring or removing sources. The current built-in configurable source is dsh-market; indexes are preloaded on demand during configuration. - **First-run setup:** Added a Custom Plugin Markets option to onboarding that enables the corresponding plugin and configures the dsh-market source.  ## Fixes  - **Windows sandbox commands:** Reduced console-window popups and focus stealing when running sandboxed commands. - **Plugin information:** Fixed missing component names, descriptions, and icons, and improved package information lookup in Desktop Profiles. - **Legacy directory cleanup:** Fixed cleanup of legacy Profile module links potentially deleting files from an older installation directory. - **Background task notifications:** Foreground commands and tasks canceled during session teardown no longer trigger misleading background-task completion or failure notifications. Tasks that actually continue in the background still notify. - **Renderer recovery:** Fixed the entire app exiting when showing or closing a Compatibility-mode window after its page was destroyed and automatic recovery was exhausted. - **Local path references:** Fixed dropped folders not working. Dropped, pasted, or picked local files and folders now resolve correctly to @ path references in the composer. - **Sidebar browser:** Enabled the right-sidebar browser by default in other Profiles while respecting explicit Profile-level overrides. - **Dark-mode select menus:** Fixed unreadable white-on-white select popups in dark mode on Windows and Linux. - **Windows update installation:** Handled silent and hidden flags passed by older updaters so the installation progress window remains visible. - **Plugin page state:** Fixed switches repeatedly loading, briefly disabling, or flickering when returning to Installed Plugins. Previously loaded state is retained during background refresh.  ## Downloads  Choose the matching installer from **Assets** below:  | Platform | File | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-x64-Setup.exe` | | Windows x64 · Portable | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-x64-Portable.zip` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-Desktop-2.0.18-linux-x64.tar.gz` |  Use `SHA256SUMS.txt` from this release to verify all installers.  ## Usage notes  - Quit the previous version and back up `~/.dsh` before installing. Verify your usual plugins and workflows afterward. - Before managing plugins for the `desktop` Profile from the bundled terminal, launch Desktop to initialize that Profile. - Windows uses opaque windows. Linux supports Compatibility mode only.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.17...v2.0.18) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn) 
```

```
这是 v0.16.0 的补丁发版，修两个会影响启动和更新体积的问题。  ## 修复  - **独立入口在装有第三方 bundle 的 profile 上启动失败** — #1577 把后端装配放到了延迟根守卫之前。profile 的 `dsh.profile.bundles` 里有 `@deepseek-ai/*` 和 dsh-tui 之外的包时，装配会提前解析组合根，根能力守卫立刻装上且撤不掉；DSH 0.2.0-rc.2 的基础行在 `ctx.root.accessor('invocation')` 处被拒，`plugin-manager`、`llm`、`commands` 等 9 个必需插件无法激活，`agent-loop` 等不到 `llm`。现在延迟紧跟在入口绑定根之后，排在装配和内核选择之前，恢复 0.15.0 的守卫时序。([#1598](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1598) by @MomentDerek；维护者修复 by @ccch1mneyyy) - **更新时不再把其他平台的 Claude SDK 包拉进 profile** — pnpm 更新会物化所有平台的 optional dependency；0.15 → 0.16 曾把七个 `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk-*` 包（约 1.6GB）装进 profile store，而实际只会链接当前平台的那一个。现在更新和按需安装内核都会按本机 platform/arch/libc 过滤；Claude SDK 仍是按需安装的可选后端，普通更新不会自动加。([#1600](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1600) by @ccch1mneyyy)  ## 兼容与升级  - 从 v0.16.0 升级：程序内 `/update`，或   `npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.16.1`。 - 已在 0.16.0 遇到启动失败：升级到 0.16.1 即可，不必再钉回 0.15.0、移除第三方 bundle 或先升 DSH。 - 一键安装包 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`、各平台 `dsh-tui-standalone-*` 便携包与 `SHA256SUMS` 仍在 Release 资产里。  ## 安装  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.16.1   偏好一键安装的用户可下载 Release 中的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`，解压后按平台运行 `install.sh` 或 `install.ps1`；便携包解压即用。   <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.16.1 -->  ## What's Changed * fix(update): install only this platform's Claude SDK bundle by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1600 * fix(host): defer the root guard before the entry's backend assembly by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1598   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/compare/v0.16.0...v0.16.1 
```

```
This is a hotfix release for v0.9.4. See the v0.9.4 notes for the full feature release: https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr/releases/tag/v0.9.4  ### Fixed - Prefix shortcuts work again when the terminal sends the next key as plain text. This affected Tern, Chinese, Japanese and Korean input methods on macOS, and Ghostty `text:` keybinds that send the prefix and a key together. Workspace navigation had the same problem. (#5255) - Codex sessions no longer start with a "clamping Interrupt hook timeout" warning. Herdr no longer installs Codex turn hooks, and Codex status comes from screen detection again. Run `herdr integration install codex` to update. (#5246, #5278) - Claude Code questions show as blocked in named sessions. (#4573, #5192) - On Windows, plugin panes start even when a folder on `PATH` has the same name as the plugin's program. (#5267, #5269, thanks @JJLiebig) - On Windows, Claude Code session tracking works when the user folder name contains non-ASCII characters, such as Korean. (#5253, #5270, thanks @JJLiebig) - Adding a Windows machine no longer prints raw PowerShell progress output. (#4639, #5282, thanks @JJLiebig)
```

```
本次更新重点优化了长任务执行、上下文恢复和用量统计的可靠性：修复摘要达到输出上限、历史工具参数格式异常导致的对话中断；解决上下文压缩后证据难以追溯、恢复任务时遗漏已有脚本和测试结论的问题，减少重复探索；修复缓存写入 Token 在流式响应、持久化和汇总过程中丢失、DeepSeek 缓存命中用量未正确识别等统计问题，并完善去重及总量缺失时的计算逻辑，新增用量明细与趋势展示。同时修复移动端项目页面遮挡、滚动受限和表格重叠，以及并发创建资产时的数据库锁冲突。  ---  This update improves the reliability of long-running tasks, context recovery, and usage tracking. It fixes conversation interruptions caused by summary output limits and malformed historical tool arguments, preserves retrievable evidence after context compaction, and restores existing scripts and test outcomes when resuming tasks to reduce repeated work. It also fixes missing cache-write token counts across streaming, persistence, and aggregation, corrects DeepSeek cache-hit reporting, and improves usage deduplication and total-token fallback calculations, alongside detailed usage views and trends. Additional fixes address overlapping mobile layouts, inaccessible content and cramped tables, as well as database lock conflicts during concurrent asset creation.
```

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.2.4 为 OxideSens 加入图片输入、工具返回图片、小米 MiMo 提供商，以及独立配置的 DeepSeek 搜索与网页读取；同时完善 SSH 提权凭据、命令历史、文件导航和离线端口转发管理，整理设置与首次使用引导，并修复云同步初始化、主机监控和 SSH 重连问题。  #### 🖼️ AI 图片输入与工具图片  - 对话支持从剪贴板粘贴图片或选择图片文件，输入区以可移除的缩略图展示附件；发送后的图片随对话保存，重新打开历史对话时仍可查看。 - 图片输入适配 OpenAI Chat Completions、Responses、Anthropic 和 Gemini 接口。图片会经过格式、尺寸及请求大小检查，并在发送前缩放；上下文预算纳入图片 token 估算，未确定的计费规则按暂估处理，实际用量以服务商返回值为准。 - 修正模型图片能力判断：明确不支持图片的模型会在添加附件时提示；能力未知的自定义模型不再被直接当作纯文本模型。模型图片能力仍可在设置中指定。 - 切换到不支持图片的模型时，如果历史上下文包含图片，会先确认是否保留对话中的图片、仅以文字继续，不会直接删除历史附件。 - 工具返回的图片贯通工具消息、模型请求和对话存储，包括 MCP 图片结果与 ACP 宿主工具回复；图片附件与普通工具文字输出分开处理。 - 新增小米 MiMo 提供商配置，并补充对应模型的图片能力与推理设置适配。  #### 🌐 独立联网工具与全局子 Agent 设置  - 新增 DeepSeek 搜索和网页读取工具，搜索账号独立于聊天模型配置。可在全局 AI 设置中选择已保存的官方 DeepSeek 配置，让其他聊天模型通过工具使用搜索服务。 - 搜索与网页读取结果显示来源链接，并区分认证、余额不足、限流、页面不可用及请求失败等情况。网页读取限于受支持的公开 HTTP/HTTPS 地址。 - 子 Agent 的启用状态、默认模型和并发限制统一为全局设置；所有对话共享并发上限，等待中的任务释放运行名额。关闭后不再启动新子任务，已运行任务可继续完成。 - 工具设置明确区分“允许使用工具”和“自动批准操作”。自动批准列表中未勾选的操作仍可在确认后运行；停用工具则不能运行。子 Agent 沿用父任务允许的工具范围与审批规则。  #### 🔑 SSH 提权凭据与动态验证码  - 新增 SSH 通用提权凭据范围，可复用 sudo、su 和自定义密码提示凭据；连接专属匹配优先，本地终端保持独立范围。 - 密码提示只有一个匹配凭据时，可按 Enter 填入；存在多个匹配时，先显示名称、类型和来源供选择，支持方向键、Enter、鼠标和 Esc。开始手动输入或切换终端会关闭选择器。 - 启用敏感凭据同步且同步全部连接时，SSH 通用凭据随连接同步；只同步部分连接时不改动通用范围。包含密码的加密连接导出包会保存按连接限定的副本，导入后不会自动变成全局默认凭据。 - 共享 TOTP 凭据支持在设置中编辑连接绑定，保存时应用替换关系；命令行创建和编辑连接支持按名称或 ID 选择已有 TOTP 凭据，也可清除绑定。  #### ⌨️ 命令历史与快捷命令  - 已保存的 SSH、Telnet、Mosh 和串口连接可选择在本机保存命令历史，重启后继续使用；补齐 Telnet、Mosh 及串口文本控制台的历史建议。 - 历史列表支持当前连接与全部连接范围，可暂停当前连接记录、删除单条记录或清空历史；保留天数可配置，所有已保存连接合计最多保留 10,000 条。 - 快捷命令匹配规则支持选择主机地址、连接名称或全部字段，结合通配符与逗号分隔规则定位目标。 - 简化命令历史范围切换控件，协调终端输入栏、快捷命令面板与命令发送器在背景下的表面样式。  #### 📁 文件导航与端口转发  - SFTP 新增“前往终端目录”，按同一服务器的当前终端或最近使用终端定位目录，也适用于独立 SFTP 窗口。默认快捷键为 macOS `⌘⇧G`、Windows/Linux `Ctrl+Shift+G`，支持自定义；终端目录变化不会自动移动文件浏览器。 - 本地文件管理器支持拖动收藏项排序、调整收藏侧栏宽度及双击恢复默认宽度，顺序和宽度会保存；系统目录与驱动器保持原有位置。 - 已保存的端口转发规则在连接断开时仍可查看、编辑、保存和删除。“保存并启动”可通过现有连接流程建立 SSH 连接，无须额外打开终端。（Issue [#694](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/694)） - 停止转发后保留规则并关闭自动启动，手动停止的规则不会因重连再次启动；断线恢复保留规则身份，避免旧连接清理影响替换后的运行实例。  #### 🎨 设置、技能管理与首次使用引导  - 统一设置行的名称、说明、控件对齐和窄窗口换行；路径输入、代理表单、带单位数字框和背景预览继续使用适合各自内容的布局。 - 技能管理新增名称与说明搜索、范围筛选、启用数量、可展开详情和格式、读取、同名覆盖诊断，并提供打开所在目录的入口；停用技能保留原文件。 - 重新组织 AI 提供商与上下文页面，明确当前聊天配置、搜索服务配置和高级上下文容量设置；整理工具审批与子 Agent 的作用范围说明。 - 外部 MCP 控制页分别展示服务端点、监听端口、待审批操作和客户端权限；细粒度权限按功能分组，允许使用的工具与审批模式分别设置，保留原有权限含义。 - 协调壁纸下的标签、卡片、输入控件和终端辅助面板底色，限制嵌套表面的不透明度累加，并减弱侧栏分隔边缘；文字、强调色和媒体保持各自的绘制规则。 - 继续整理五页首次使用引导：统一内容宽度与间距，把连接流程与功能介绍分栏，更多功能按需展开；AI 开关前置，背景素材区限制高度，开始使用页采用更紧凑的操作入口并移除重复快捷键。  #### 🛠️ 连接、监控与同步修复  - SSH 连接失败时只重置仍在等待连接的窗格，保留已经进入 Shell 启动阶段的重连，防止重复发起。（PR [#682](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/682)） - 修复同一节点同时存在重连和新终端打开请求时，关闭重连窗格会丢失临时节点清理标记的问题。（PR [#685](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/685)） - X11 转发按终端报告协商成功、缺少本地显示或授权、服务端拒绝和超时等状态，并补充 Windows 本地 X Server 的要求；远端 `DISPLAY` 仍由 SSH 服务端分配。 - 远端平台检测显式使用 POSIX Shell，并支持仅接受交互式 Shell、不接受 exec 请求的 SSH 服务端；不支持的 Shell 不再反复触发自动集成检查提示。 - 主机监控打开后立即采样，刷新时保留现有数据直到新数据到达；区分环境检测中、无法识别系统和采样失败，并提供重试入口。修复列表行提示状态互相影响的问题。 - VNC 查看器断开或失败后保持停止，等待用户明确重连，不因页面重新绘制而重复启动连接。 - 修复部分 WebDAV 服务在首次同步目录尚不存在时返回 409 导致初始化失败的问题；首次同步失败或取消后，密码仍可重新设置，已有加密数据则继续走密码更换流程。 - 修正工作区启动和生命周期定时任务的调度，改善通知、后台状态和界面更新的时序一致性。  #### 📦 构建环境  - Nix 开发环境补齐图形运行库搜索路径，以及 `libdrm` 和 Mesa，使通过 `nix develop` 后执行 `cargo run` 的程序能够找到相关运行库；此项针对开发环境，发行包无法启动的反馈仍需单独定位。（PR [#688](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/688)） - macOS 构建通过 Xcode 查找已安装的 Metal 工具链，避免仅依赖默认工具路径；本机仍需安装 Metal Toolchain。  #### 👥 贡献者  - @m00nLi：修复 SSH 重连状态与临时节点清理问题（PR [#682](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/682)、[#685](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/685)）。 - @sincerely-hello-world：修复 Nix 开发环境的图形运行库配置（PR [#688](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/688)）。  #### 📌 使用与升级说明  - 本机命令历史保存默认关闭；开启后完整命令会写入未加密的本地文件，参数中的密码或令牌也可能被记录。仅记录开启后的命令，默认保留 90 天，设为 0 可永久保留。 - 联网工具需启用工具调用并配置官方 DeepSeek 账号；搜索词和网页地址会发送给 DeepSeek，搜索及网页读取按请求单独计费。 - 图片输入能力取决于实际模型和接入端点，图片 token 估算不代替服务商账单。 - 本版更新日志范围：[v2.2.3…v2.2.4](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.3...v2.2.4)。  ### English  OxideTerm 2.2.4 adds image input, tool-result images, Xiaomi MiMo, and independently configured DeepSeek web search and page reading to OxideSens. It also improves SSH privilege credentials, command history, file navigation, and offline forwarding management, reorganizes settings and onboarding, and fixes cloud-sync initialization, host monitoring, and SSH reconnection.  #### 🖼️ AI image input and tool-result images  - Conversations accept pasted images and image files, with removable thumbnails in the composer. Sent attachments are saved with the conversation and remain available when reopening history. - Image input supports OpenAI Chat Completions, Responses, Anthropic, and Gemini interfaces. Images undergo format, size, and request-budget checks and are resized before sending. Context budgeting includes estimated image tokens; unspecified pricing rules remain provisional, and provider-reported usage is authoritative. - Corrected model capability detection: models known to lack image support show an error when adding attachments, while unknown custom models are no longer treated as text-only. Image capability can still be specified in settings. - Switching to a text-only model with images in the conversation context asks whether to retain the images in history and continue with text only, rather than deleting the attachments. - Tool-result images now flow through tool messages, model requests, and conversation storage, including MCP image results and ACP host-tool replies. Attachments remain separate from ordinary tool text output. - Added Xiaomi MiMo provider configuration, with image-capability and reasoning-setting support for its models.  #### 🌐 Independent web tools and global subagent settings  - Added DeepSeek web search and page-reading tools with a search account configured separately from the chat model. Global AI settings can use a saved official DeepSeek configuration so other chat models can access the search service through tools. - Search and page-reading results show source links and distinguish authentication errors, insufficient balance, rate limits, unavailable pages, and request failures. Page reading accepts supported public HTTP/HTTPS URLs. - Subagent enablement, default model, and concurrency limits are now global. Conversations share the concurrency limit, and waiting tasks release their running slots. Disabling subagents prevents new subtasks while allowing running tasks to finish. - Tool settings distinguish tool availability from automatic approval. Unchecked entries in the automatic-approval list can still run after confirmation; disabled tools cannot run. Subagents retain their parent task's allowed tools and approval rules.  #### 🔑 SSH privilege credentials and one-time passwords  - Added a common SSH privilege-credential scope for sudo, su, and custom password prompts. Connection-specific matches take priority, and local terminals retain a separate scope. - At a password prompt, Enter fills a single matching credential. Multiple matches open a chooser showing name, type, and source, with arrow-key, Enter, mouse, and Esc support. Manual input or switching terminals closes the chooser. - Full connection sync includes common SSH credentials when sensitive-credential sync is enabled; syncing selected connections leaves the common scope unchanged. Encrypted connection exports that include passwords carry connection-scoped copies, which do not become global defaults on import. - Shared TOTP credentials now support editing connection bindings in settings, with replacements applied on save. CLI connection creation and editing can select an existing TOTP credential by name or ID, or remove its binding.  #### ⌨️ Command history and quick commands  - Saved SSH, Telnet, Mosh, and serial connections can optionally retain command history on this device across restarts. Restored history suggestions for Telnet, Mosh, and serial text consoles. - History supports current-connection and all-connection scopes, pausing recording for a connection, deleting individual entries, and clearing history. Retention is configurable, with up to 10,000 entries across saved connections. - Quick-command rules can match host addresses, connection names, or all fields, using wildcards and comma-separated patterns. - Simplified the history scope selector and coordinated wallpaper-backed surfaces in the terminal input bar, quick-command panel, and command sender.  #### 📁 File navigation and port forwarding  - Added Go to Terminal Directory in SFTP, using the current or most recently used terminal on the same server, including detached SFTP windows. The customizable default shortcut is `⌘⇧G` on macOS and `Ctrl+Shift+G` on Windows/Linux. Terminal navigation does not automatically move the file browser. - Local File Manager favorites can be reordered by dragging, and the favorites sidebar can be resized or reset by double-clicking its edge. Order and width are saved; system folders and drives keep their positions. - Saved forwarding rules remain visible and editable while disconnected and can be saved or deleted offline. Save and Start uses the existing SSH connection flow without opening an extra terminal. (Issue [#694](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/694)) - Stopping a forward retains its rule and disables automatic startup. Manually stopped rules remain stopped after reconnection; interrupted forwards retain their identity, and cleanup from an old connection does not affect its replacement.  #### 🎨 Settings, skills, and onboarding  - Unified setting labels, descriptions, control alignment, and narrow-window wrapping, while retaining suitable layouts for paths, proxy forms, numeric fields with units, and background previews. - Skill management now offers name and description search, scope filtering, enabled counts, expandable details, and diagnostics for invalid, unreadable, or overridden skills, plus an Open Containing Folder action. Disabling a skill preserves its files. - Reorganized AI provider and context pages to distinguish the current chat configuration, search-service configuration, and advanced context-capacity settings, with clearer tool-approval and subagent scope descriptions. - External MCP controls separate the endpoint, listening port, pending approvals, and client permissions. Fine-grained permissions are grouped by function, with tool access and approval mode configured separately and existing permission semantics preserved. - Coordinated wallpaper-backed tabs, cards, input controls, and terminal panels, limited accumulated opacity in nested surfaces, and softened sidebar separators. Text, accents, and media retain their own rendering rules. - Refined the five-page onboarding flow with consistent widths and spacing, side-by-side connection steps and feature introductions, and expandable additional features. AI switches appear earlier, the background gallery has a bounded height, and the final page uses compact actions without duplicate shortcuts.  #### 🛠️ Connections, monitoring, and sync fixes  - SSH connection failures now reset only panes still waiting for a connection, preserving reconnects already starting a shell and preventing duplicate attempts. (PR [#682](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/682)) - Fixed loss of temporary-node cleanup state when a reconnect and a new terminal open share a node and the reconnect pane is closed. (PR [#685](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/685)) - X11 forwarding reports per-terminal negotiation success, missing local display or authorization, server rejection, and timeouts. Windows guidance now explains the local X server requirement; the SSH server assigns the remote `DISPLAY`. - Remote platform detection explicitly uses a POSIX shell and supports SSH servers that accept interactive shell requests but reject exec requests. Unsupported shells no longer trigger repeated automatic integration-check notices. - Host monitoring samples immediately on opening and retains existing data during refresh until replacements arrive. It distinguishes environment detection, unknown systems, and sampling failures, with a retry action. Fixed tooltip state leaking between list rows. - Disconnected or failed VNC viewers remain stopped until an explicit reconnect instead of restarting during a redraw. - Fixed first-sync initialization against WebDAV servers that return 409 when a sync directory's ancestor is missing. Failed or canceled initial sync attempts allow password setup to be retried, while existing encrypted data continues to use the password-rotation flow. - Corrected scheduling of workspace startup and lifecycle timers for more consistent notification, background-state, and UI updates.  #### 📦 Build environments  - The Nix development shell now exposes graphics runtime-library paths and includes `libdrm` and Mesa, allowing `cargo run` inside `nix develop` to locate the required libraries. This fixes the development environment; reported packaged-app startup failures still require separate investigation. (PR [#688](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/688)) - macOS builds locate the installed Metal toolchain through Xcode instead of relying solely on the default tool path. Metal Toolchain must still be installed locally.  #### 👥 Contributors  - @m00nLi: SSH reconnect-state and temporary-node cleanup fixes (PR [#682](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/682), [#685](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/685)). - @sincerely-hello-world: Nix development-shell graphics runtime configuration (PR [#688](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/688)).  #### 📌 Usage and upgrade notes  - Saving command history locally is off by default. When enabled, complete commands are written to an unencrypted local file, including any passwords or tokens in arguments. Recording begins when enabled; the default retention is 90 days, and 0 retains history indefinitely. - Web tools require tool calling and an official DeepSeek account. Search queries and page URLs are sent to DeepSeek, and search and page-reading requests are billed separately. - Image support depends on the actual model and endpoint. Estimated image tokens do not replace the provider's billing data. - Full changelog: [v2.2.3…v2.2.4](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.3...v2.2.4).  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_windows_x64-setup.exe) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_windows_arm64-setup.exe) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.4/OxideTerm_2.2.4_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  ## 插件市场兼容说明  插件市场 v1 已冻结，原地址、索引内容和对应安装包持续保留。旧客户端仍可使用冻结目录中的插件，但不会看到后续的新插件和更新；请升级到支持 v2 目录的主程序版本。后续插件、新版本和版本历史仅发布到 v2。  ## Plugin marketplace compatibility  The v1 plugin marketplace is frozen. Its original URL, exact contents, and referenced packages remain available. Existing clients can keep using the frozen catalog but will not see subsequent plugins or updates; upgrade to a host version supporting v2 to receive them. All subsequent plugins, releases, and version histories are published only to v2.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper. Run in Terminal:  bash xattr -cr ~/Downloads/OxideTerm_*.dmg # or after install / 或安装后 xattr -cr /Applications/OxideTerm.app   ### Windows  If SmartScreen warns, click **More info** -> **Run anyway**.  若 SmartScreen 弹出警告，点击 **更多信息** -> **仍要运行**。  ### Linux  bash # AppImage chmod +x OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage && ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage  # Debian/Ubuntu sudo dpkg -i OxideTerm_*_linux_*.deb && sudo apt-get install -f  # Fedora/RHEL-compatible systems sudo dnf install ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.rpm   </details>  ## 🔗 Links  - Documentation: https://oxideterm.app - GitHub Issues: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues - Changelog: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/.github/release-notes/stable-changelog.md 
```

by turn ownership by @GGMGG in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1915 * fix(chat): accept the highlighted slash command on Enter by @toxeh in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1911 * fix(history): invalidate indexes after equal-metadata replacements by @GGMGG in ### 2026-10-20T17:56:37Z [**t8y2/dbx**](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx)

```
### 新功能  - openGauss 开启表数据导入能力，数据页工具栏、对象浏览器与侧边栏的导入入口现在可用 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12895](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12895)) - 数据网格手写 WHERE 输入框在列名后支持补全运算符，并按列类型提供日期、布尔等类型化取值候选 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#12879](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12879)) - Oracle 与 OceanBase Oracle 支持无效对象筛选与批量编译，以及会话与锁管理 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12773](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12773)) - Oracle 与 OceanBase Oracle 的过程、函数和程序包支持查看双向依赖明细 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12770](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12770)) - 数据网格高级复制新增 DbUnit XML、PHP 数组、HTML 双 MIME 等格式，并支持配置列元数据复制与对象全限定名 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12777](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12777)) - 表详情列面板支持复制所选列名及带表头的元数据 TSV (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#12771](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12771)) - MySQL 连接支持 Navicat HTTP 隧道（ntunnel_mysql.php），适配无 PHP-FPM 的 CGI/FastCGI 主机 (contributed by @303562915) (PR [#12494](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12494)) - AI 助手新增选项，可选择是否在上下文中附带查询结果预览，默认关闭 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12776](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12776)) - SQL 编辑器新增上一条、下一条语句及跳转到语句起始位置的导航快捷键 (contributed by @PanSHIgg) (PR [#12754](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12754)) - 侧边栏支持 F5 快捷键（可配置）刷新连接列表与数据库 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12780](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12780)) - 表与对象容器右键菜单支持直接新建表分组，无需先选中表 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12781](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12781)) - 本地值筛选支持按出现数量筛选，例如 count>1 筛重复、=1 筛唯一 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12628](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12628)) - 连接分组右键菜单支持一键打开组内全部连接 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12844](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12844)) - SQL 文件面板支持名称搜索过滤与 A-Z/Z-A 排序 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12840](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12840)) - AI 助手选中的技能标签支持一键折叠与展开，不再占用对话区域 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12864](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12864))  ### 改进  - 新增 MiniMax 配置默认使用 M3.1 Flash 模型，并支持 low/medium/high/xhigh/max 推理力度 (contributed by @octo-patch) (PR [#12885](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12885)) - 设置搜索补齐约 90 项遗漏配置与关键词，展示全部匹配结果，并支持嵌套子页导航与定位 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#12743](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12743)) - H2 连接 URL 输入框显示 jdbc:h2 格式占位符，不再误显 postgresql 示例 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12866](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12866)) - MQTT 控制台消息轮询默认间隔调整为 1s，并支持 1/2/3/5s 刷新间隔选择 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12851](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12851)) - 片段密语已在本机安全保存时隐藏密码显示按钮，避免出现无效果的控件 (contributed by @thanadon-dev) (PR [#12847](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12847)) - 查询执行期间切换结果视图时，忙碌遮罩中的已耗时显示保持连续 (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#12842](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12842)) - 查询出错或取消后重新执行时正常显示执行时长 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12767](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12767)) - 分组右键菜单中「关闭数据库连接」默认置于数据库一级菜单首位 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12875](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12875)) - 页签栏重命名从 SQL 库打开的查询时，新名称同步到已保存的 SQL 文件 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12831](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12831)) - 分组标签栏中当前激活的标签页增加主色调上缘高亮，多分组下更易定位 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12540](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12540)) - Windows 7 离线包为 WebView2 109 启用现代颜色支持，主题、背景与高亮颜色更接近现有样式，可用环境变量回退 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12761](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12761)) - Kingbase 兼容模式探测不再依赖单一 catalog，按 SYS settings、PG settings 与 SHOW 顺序多路探测 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12822](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12822)) - Kingbase 探测到的兼容模式完整传递到连接元数据，前端可稳定读取 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12823](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12823)) - Kingbase 连接与重连在就绪前刷新兼容模式，避免沿用过期模式 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12827](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12827)) - Kingbase 编辑器按连接兼容模式配置语法、补全、诊断与格式化 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12834](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12834))  ### 修复  - 达梦原生导出/导入（dexp/dimp）规整参数文件中的 Windows 路径与引号，并在失败时展示工具真实输出，不再只报 did not report success (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#12872](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12872)) - 虚谷数据传输在 Agent RPC 超时后丢弃受影响会话并可恢复继续，不再中断失败 (contributed by @mapan0424) (PR [#12832](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12832)) - Kingbase 查询与 SQL 文件处理使用当前连接的兼容模式分句，不再拆开存储过程内部语句 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12825](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12825)) - Kingbase 结构化索引保留表达式、INCLUDE 列、排序与过滤条件，重建索引不再丢失语义 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12806](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12806)) - Kingbase 生成列与 identity 列元数据完整保留，不再误导出为普通 DEFAULT (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12805](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12805)) - 数据传输到 DuckDB 目标保留源端 decimal 精度，修复宽数字 Conversion Error (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#12882](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12882)) - SQL Server 仅在显式选择 Schema 时才限定未限定表名，连接默认、恢复页签或保存 SQL 带入的 Schema 不再引发 Invalid object name (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#12819](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12819)) - Kingbase 识别 jdbc:kingbase8/83/86 等 release JDBC URL，不再当作原生 DSN 透传导致连接失败 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12815](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12815)) - 导入含空白工作表的 Excel 工作簿时保留工作表选择并禁用空表任务，不再因预览空表直接失败 (contributed by @ericbyz) (PR [#12738](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12738)) - MongoDB 集合表格保存编辑后保持滚动位置与选中，不再跳回第一行 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#12810](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12810)) - Kingbase 变量替换、参数抽取与批量执行使用当前兼容模式，避免误识别参数或拆分多库事务 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12833](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12833)) - Kingbase 语句边界保留过程块、临时表与 PG #>> 等 hash 操作符的执行范围 (contributed by @HandSonic) (PR [#12828](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12828)) - 内联 EXPLAIN 之后的查询不再被并入 EXPLAIN 语句，修复执行报语法错误 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12824](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12824)) - TDengine 超级表折叠重开后保留已加载子表列表与计数，并优化大表树加载与重开性能 (contributed by @CN-Scars) (PR [#12759](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12759)) - 无主键表行定位器被引擎拒绝时自动降级为普通查询，修复 Hologres 单击无主键表报错 (contributed by @ericbyz) (PR [#12687](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12687)) - 复制行粘贴到新行时空字符串保持为空字符串，不再被写成 NULL (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#12748](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12748)) - SQL 文件执行全程固定同一会话，修复含 BEGIN/COMMIT 的文件跨连接执行导致的锁等待与卡死 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#12725](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12725)) - Redis 数据库置顶在修改别名与重连后保持有效 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12765](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12765)) - 侧边栏搜索结果在折叠再展开后不再被清空 (contributed by @zhukj125) (PR [#12766](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12766)) - Oracle TNS 连接明文导出保留 TNS_ADMIN 目录，重新导入后无需重新配置 (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#12723](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12723)) - 数据库连接「显示全部」后系统数据库保持可见 - 在数据库中查找首次执行时自动继续扫描剩余未扫描的表，不再提前停止 - Phoenix 手动 classpath 接受旧版直接客户端 jar，不再误报驱动未安装 - MySQL 存储过程源码页签执行 CREATE 时先删除再重建例程，修复修改 MySQL 8 存储过程失败  --- <!-- dbx-cnb-mirror --> > 国内下载：如果 GitHub 下载较慢，可从 [CNB 镜像](https://cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx/-/releases/tag/v0.6.50) 下载桌面端安装包，Docker 镜像从 `docker.cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx:v0.6.50` 拉取。 
```

```
<div align="center">   <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Kaku/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Kaku Logo" width="120" height="120" />   <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Kaku V0.26.0</h1>   <p><em>A fast, out-of-the-box terminal built for AI coding.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog 1. **Remote Page**: The web page draws with your Mac's fonts, shows the agent title, previews an image path when you hover it, and picks up a new version on its own after reconnecting. 2. **Remote Typing**: Typing stays ahead of file transfers, emoji take two cells as they do in Kaku, splits keep the Mac's layout, and the page redraws itself if it falls out of step. 3. **Remote Access**: Only one Kaku serves remote access at a time, so a second copy no longer takes the page over, and notices say what to do when the Mac is offline. 4. **Lower CPU**: Programs that keep updating the title, such as Claude Code and Codex, no longer redraw the whole window each time. 5. **Dead Keys**: A dead key such as `~` followed by a key it cannot combine with now types both characters, as Terminal.app does. 6. **Character Picker**: The emoji and Nerd Font picker is back in the command palette, found by searching emoji. 7. **Fixes**: Frame lines are quieter on dark themes, SSH tabs show their number when tab numbers are on, and AI summaries keep file names whole.  ### 更新日志 1. **远程网页**：网页使用 Mac 上的字体，显示 Agent 标题，悬停图片路径即可预览，重连后自动换上新版本。 2. **远程输入**：传文件时输入依然优先，emoji 与 Kaku 一样占两格，分屏保持 Mac 上的布局，画面错位时会自动重绘。 3. **远程访问**：同一时间只有一个 Kaku 提供远程访问，另开的副本不再接管网页，Mac 离线时提示会说明该怎么做。 4. **更低的 CPU 占用**：Claude Code、Codex 这类持续更新标题的程序不再每次重绘整个窗口。 5. **死键**：`~` 这类死键后接无法组合的按键时，两个字符都会输入，与 Terminal.app 一致。 6. **字符选择器**：emoji 和 Nerd Font 选择器回到命令面板，搜索 emoji 即可打开。 7. **修复**：深色主题下的边框线更柔和，开启标签编号后 SSH 标签也显示编号，AI 摘要里的文件名保持完整。  Special thanks to @SimonRen for their contribution to this release.  > https://github.com/tw93/Kaku 
```

### 2026-10-17T07:34:13Z [**rvben/rumdl**](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl)

```
 ### Changed  - **2.4–4.7× faster `rumdl fmt`** than 0.2.79 on frozen documentation   snapshots: Cargo (99 files) 4.7×, MkDocs Material (85 files) 2.6×, and   Ruff (16 files) 2.4×. Reusing parsed Markdown and shared source analysis   reduces repeated work. Speedups compare median elapsed times from 31 paired   runs after five warmups on Apple M6: ordinary Rust 1.96.0 release builds,   default threads, warm filesystem cache, and `--no-config --no-cache --silent`   (`--flavor=standard` for Cargo/Ruff, `--flavor=mkdocs` for MkDocs Material).   Output includes the correctness fixes listed below and can differ from 0.2.79. - Code block tools are supported and remain opt-in. Missing tags, missing   active-mode tools and invocations with no tools checked are ignored by default.   An omitted unknown-tag policy inherits `on-missing-language-definition`,   preserving existing strict configurations. Valid internal cached checks count   as checked; applicable external tools rerun to reflect changes in their own   configuration and environment. - `on-missing-language-definition = "warn"` now reports uncovered languages;   previous releases treated it as `ignore`. - Executable lookup remains system-only by default. Per-binary preferences can   opt into nearest-first project lookup bounded by repository/configuration   boundaries, including hoisted workspace installations. - MD037 still reports spaces inside emphasis delimiters but no longer provides   automatic fixes, including editor fixes. Repair the reported emphasis manually;   inserting escapes or removing whitespace can change literal Markdown meaning.  ### Added  - `fmt --preflight` and `check --fix --preflight` validate and plan the complete   selected file batch before applying any writes, with atomic replacement per   file after successful planning. - Configurable coverage policies with block diagnostics and invocation-wide   fail-fast behavior. Invalid tool definitions receive central configuration   warnings, made fatal by `--deny-config-warnings`. - Fail-fast tool policies report an incomplete invocation (exit 2), including   when document finding failures are disabled with `--fail-on never`. - Shared full-table language recognition and flavor-aware fence coverage.   No-tools invocation errors preserve JSON's findings array and use SARIF   invocation notifications rather than assigning errors to a document. - `--stdin-batch-targets FILE` supplies a NUL-delimited file/directory inventory   for cross-file existence checks without requiring placeholder documents. - **MD013**: add reflow-break-code-spans to keep code spans whole while emphasis wraps ([bd41ee8](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/bd41ee8fb9c3ddac017b6a62430c8dec396ed58c)) - **fmt**: add Bun code-block formatting and reduce redundant checks ([2605ead](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/2605ead28dbf75ff219af9404d1790c7efa3234e)) - **stdin**: accept separate batch link target inventories ([56c492b](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/56c492b541c1c78a212d01a11bce1d9983d45d4a))  ### Fixed  - Headings inside blockquotes and list items are checked and fixed relative to   their container. Heading spacing, indentation, capitalization, and reflow   preserve list markers and avoid rewriting literal text or template code. - Tables nested under multiple containers are recognized. Blank-line checks   respect MkDocs admonition openers and Markdown-enabled HTML boundaries;   HTML markers inside comments and indented code remain literal content. - **MD035**: leave a first-line thematic break unchanged when fixing it would   turn the following content into front matter. - File discovery skips source-code files matched by broad include patterns,   while explicitly supplied unsupported source files still receive an error. - Windows path matching handles device-prefixed paths and directory separators   consistently across discovery and per-file configuration. - Reject invalid UTF-8 in external tool stdout instead of replacing invalid   bytes and allowing that output to become formatted document content. - **deps**: update rustls to address security advisories ([7acb91a](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/7acb91ad797bf792baad33de3eb356f017a8544e)) - **MD058**: follow the parser on whether a bare marker opens a list item, and limit the list tab check to table rows ([0075568](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/007556897ade7e6d23590b4f965ad90e4252b16d)) - **MD056**: confirm tables longer than the parser slice limit
```
