input 完整保留多轮对话角色**: 解决 `/v1/chat/completions` 接收仅带 `instructions` / `input` 而无 `messages` 的 Responses 格式请求时，原实现强行将数组 `input` 字符串化导致丢失角色边界的问题。改为无缝复用 `convert_codex_to_openai_request` 结构化转换器，按条目精确还原各轮次角色。(PR #3672, Fixes #3671, Thanks to @blackdm666)     -   **根除 0ms 乐观重置风暴与物理异步冷却**: 彻底移除 `clear_for_optimistic_reset()` 机制；当号池最短限流等待时间 `<= 5s` 时执行真实物理异步冷却，`> 5s` 时主动返回明确冷却需求；引入两轮自适应匀速轮换（150ms 延迟）与线性阶梯退避重试（1.5s ~ 3.5s），高并发 429 失败率从 37.4% 彻底降低至 0.0%。(PR #3685, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **全账号限流重试秒数解析大小写双向兼顾**: 解决 `extract_retry_after_seconds` 仅检索大写 `"Wait "` 导致无法解析冷却秒数的缺陷，支持 `"Wait "` 与 `"wait "` 双向匹配，确保 503 错误响应正常生成 `Retry-After: N` 标准头部。(PR #3687, Fixes #3506, Thanks to @EricZhou05) -   **[流量监控报文详情重构：统一单 Tab 视窗、正文结构化审查与双链路 HAR 导出]**:     -   **统一 Postman 风格单 Tab 视窗架构**: 将报文详情重塑为「请求 (Request)」、「转出 (Forwarded)」、「响应 (Response)」三大主选项卡，告别上下分屏挤压，大幅提升查阅空间。     -   **正文「结构化拆分」与「原始代码」双视图**: 智能拆解展示系统提示词、多轮对话气泡、工具函数声明、思考推理块与调用回执，复杂 Agent 报文清晰直观。     -   **键值对表格与敏感 Header 脱敏保护**: 请求头与 Query 参数升级为两列键值对表格，支持关键词搜索、悬浮复制及 `Bearer sk-***` 掩码脱敏与明文切换。     -   **标准双链路 HAR 1.2 导出 (3 报文齐全)**: 导出包含「客户端->网关」与「网关->上游真实端点」双链路 Entry，确保抓包工具导入时客户端请求、网关转出及响应 3 个报文 100% 齐全；支持一键复制 cURL 命令与全量 JSON。  ## What's Changed * fix(process): 隔离各 IDE 目标 state.vscdb 并通过 proc_pidpath 精准识别进程二进制 (Fixes #3598) by @cubelikeplayDaniel in https://github.com/lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager/pull/3600 * fix(gemini): 穿透非流式收集器中的流错误响应并防止空块污染思考缓存 by @cubelikeplayDaniel in https://github.com/lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager/pull/3601 * fix(proxy): 释放 invalid_grant 分片锁避免自死锁，隔离 project_id 探测并引入 5 分钟负缓存 by @cubelikeplayDaniel in https://github.com/lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager/pull/3602 * fix(patch): macOS 注入重签名失败时自动回滚备份以防 AMFI 闪退，并在还原时保留官方证书 by 下载与选型指南  * **标准版 (Base)**：[下载方案](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-base.zip)   * *适用人群*：纯全拼、纯双拼用户。  * **轻量版 (Lite)(t9)**：[下载方案](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-lite.zip)   * *主要区别*：无声调词库，减少部分 Lua 功能。t9与lite一起打包，使用无声调词库  * **精简版 (Pure)**：[下载方案](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-pure.zip)   * *适用人群*：追求最小词库、仅需基础功能的用户。   * *适用场景*：Win7,fcitx4等低版本，不能使用lua插件的系统。  * **双拼辅助码增强版 (Pro)**：均为独立完整配置包，含词库，支持任意双拼挂载，下载包等于选辅助码类型。   * *适用人群*：双拼+辅助码用户。   * *版本选择*：[自然码辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-zrm-fuzhu.zip) | [小鹤辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-flypy-fuzhu.zip) | [万象辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-wx-fuzhu.zip) | [首右辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-shouyou-fuzhu.zip) | [首右+辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-shyplus-fuzhu.zip) | [虎码首末辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-tiger-fuzhu.zip) | [墨奇辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-moqi-fuzhu.zip) | [五笔前2辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-wubi-fuzhu.zip) | [汉心辅助](https://github.com/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang/releases/download/v18.1.7/rime-wanxiang-hanxin-fuzhu.zip)  * **大模型语法包 (必装组件)**：[点击下载语法模型](https://github.com/amzxyz/RIME-LMDG/releases/download/LTS/wanxiang-lts-zh-hans.gram)   * *使用说明*：**所有版本（Base/Lite/Pure/Pro）用户均必须下载此文件**。下载后直接放入输入法用户目录根目录（与方案文件放一起），无需任何额外配置。  ---  ## 分发渠道与周边生态  * **Linux 仓库**：   * *Arch Linux*：启用 [Arch Linux CN 仓库](https://www.archlinuxcn.org/archlinux-cn-repo-and-mirror/) 安装。基础版：`rime-wanxiang-[方案名]`；增强版：`rime-wanxiang-pro-[方案名]`   * *deepin 25*：已并入官方系统仓库，支持 `apt install` 部署 * **周边生态**：   * [仓输入法皮肤推荐](https://github.com/BlackCCCat/ResourceforHamster/tree/main/Skin_Keyboard/) | [高级版本管理更新脚本](https://github.com/rimeinn/rime-wanxiang-update-tools) | [万象 CNB 国内镜像源](https://cnb.cool/amzxyz/rime-wanxiang)

### 2026-10-19T14:31:41Z [**kunkundi/crossdesk**](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)

```
**新增功能与改进：**  1. iOS 和 Android 新增系统键盘输入，支持使用系统输入法的候选词、纠错和听写功能，确认文字后直接输入远端。重新设计电脑键盘，提供“字母数字 / 符号功能”两页布局，完善组合键、横竖屏适配和按键触感反馈。 2. 改善移动端画面操作，支持从画面外的黑边区域开始双指缩放；相对鼠标移动至画面上、下边缘后继续移动，可自动上下平移画面，方便操作被遮挡区域。 3. 统一桌面端文件收发与桌面墙文件分发通知，按文件展示传输进度、速度和状态；传输结束或连接关闭后，结果保留至手动关闭。 4. 新增文件发送取消操作，支持取消排队中或正在发送的文件，包括桌面墙分发任务；取消单个文件不会断开远程连接。 5. 重复启动桌面应用时，自动唤起已有窗口，方便找回已最小化或隐藏的 CrossDesk。 6. 完善 Web 客户端视频参数协商，支持被控端处理浏览器端的画面设置，优化网络变化时的带宽与视频流量控制。 7. 优化近期连接卡片、设备信息提示、远程更新提醒及 macOS 权限弹窗，统一界面样式与操作反馈。 8. 在关于与更新弹窗中新增赞助入口，并加入可关闭的一次性赞助提示。 9. 新增 HarmonyOS PC 与手机、平板客户端的开发版适配代码，补充构建、权限和使用说明。  **问题修复：**  1. 修复 Windows 在 RDP 会话断开后无法正常恢复远程控制的问题，完善会话切换和画面采集恢复。 2. 改善 Web 连接的 ICE 候选收集与 TCP 连接处理，修复部分网络环境下的连接问题。 3. 修复 Web 会话画质异常下降的问题，改善带宽受限时的视频传输表现。 4. 修复被控端在文件传输期间无法继续添加待发送文件的问题。 5. 修复远程设备 ID 输入时的闪烁和超长输入问题，将输入限制为九位数字。 6. 修复检查更新时关于弹窗布局跳动、更新弹窗高度约束冲突等问题，统一各桌面平台的弹窗尺寸与布局。  **使用提示：**  - 移动端直接输入中文、表情等文字，需要被控端支持 Unicode 文本输入。目前支持该能力的 macOS 被控端可直接接收；Windows、Linux 及不支持该能力的旧版被控端使用 ASCII 按键回退，其他文字可通过会话中的剪贴板功能发送并粘贴。 - 文件取消操作位于发送端。关闭传输通知只隐藏通知，不会取消传输；需要停止发送时，请点击“取消”。 - HarmonyOS PC 与手机、平板适配仍处于开发阶段，尚需进一步完成真机兼容性与功能验收。  ---  **New Features and Improvements:**  1. Added system keyboard input on iOS and Android, with support for native input candidates, autocorrection, and dictation. Confirmed text is sent directly to the remote computer. Redesigned the computer keyboard with alphanumeric and symbol/function pages, improved modifier keys, orientation handling, and haptic feedback. 2. Improved mobile viewport controls. Pinch-to-zoom can now start in the black margins outside the remote image. In relative mouse mode, continuing to move beyond the top or bottom edge pans the image vertically to reach obscured areas. 3. Unified desktop file transfer notifications across sending, receiving, and DeskHub distribution. Each file shows progress, transfer speed, and status. Results remain visible until dismissed, including after the connection closes. 4. Added cancellation for queued and active outgoing files, including DeskHub distribution tasks. Cancelling an individual file keeps the remote connection active. 5. Launching the desktop application again now brings its existing window forward, making minimized or hidden CrossDesk windows easier to find. 6. Improved video settings negotiation for Web clients, allowing hosts to process browser-requested settings. Refined bandwidth and video flow control as network conditions change. 7. Refined recent-connection cards, device information tooltips, remote update notices, and the macOS permissions dialog, with more consistent styling and interaction feedback. 8. Added sponsorship links to the About and update dialogs, along with a dismissible, one-time sponsorship prompt. 9. Added development support for HarmonyOS PCs, phones, and tablets, with build instructions, permission guidance, and usage documentation.  **Bug Fixes:**  1. Fixed Windows remote control failing to recover after an RDP session disconnects. Improved session handoff and screen capture recovery. 2. Improved ICE candidate gathering and TCP connectivity for Web sessions, addressing connection issues in some network environments. 3. Fixed unexpected video quality degradation in Web sessions and improved video delivery under limited bandwidth. 4. Fixed controlled devices being unable to queue additional outgoing files while a transfer was active. 5. Fixed flickering and excessive input length in the remote device ID field, limiting input to nine digits. 6. Fixed About dialog layout shifts during update checks and conflicting height constraints in update dialogs. Aligned dialog sizing and layout across desktop platforms.  **Usage Notes:**  - Direct input of Chinese text, emoji, and other Unicode characters from mobile devices requires host support. Supported macOS hosts accept this input directly. Windows, Linux, and older hosts without this capability fall back to ASCII key input; use the session clipboard feature and paste on the remote computer for other text. - File cancellation is available on the sending side. Dismissing a transfer notification only hides it; click Cancel to stop sending. - HarmonyOS PC, phone, and tablet support remains in development. Further physical-device compatibility and feature validation is still required.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog：** [[v1.7.0…v1.7.1-20261019](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.0...v1.7.1-20261019)] 
```

### 2026-10-17T01:52:12Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.14.3  This release removes a dependency that kept failing security audits, fixes a validate hang in pre-commit hooks, and stops refusing some symlinked spec files.  ### Improved  - **No more `braces` audit warning** - Installing OpenSpec no longer pulls in the unpatched `braces` package, so `npm audit` in your project stops reporting GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm through OpenSpec. - **Glob artifacts stay inside the change** - The propose, ff, continue and explore workflows now choose and check a glob artifact's file path before anything writes it, including a skill or command the schema delegates creation to. A path that would leave the change directory, directly or through a symlink, is refused.  ### Fixed  - **Symlinked spec files no longer refused** - `list --specs`, `show`, `validate` and `archive` no longer error out when a capability's `spec.md` is a symlink inside the project. Symlinked layouts are not officially supported. A link that resolves outside the project is still refused. - **`validate --all` hanging in hooks** - On a terminal that reports zero columns, as some pre-commit runners provide, the spinner no longer loops forever writing cursor escapes. `OPEN_SPEC_INTERACTIVE=0` and `CI` now turn the spinner off too. - **Very wide brace patterns** - A `generates` pattern whose braces expand to more than 1,024 paths is rejected with a clear error instead of running `openspec status` out of memory.  ## New Contributors  * @CRYPTONIKAV made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2104 * @surlymochan made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2103  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.14.2...v1.14.3 
```

### 2026-10-17T18:32:49Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.10.1 is a big one for a point release. You can now write a real reply to an agent, with line breaks, long pastes and images, and see every agent you run in one list. tuios also got much easier to make your own: a theme editor inside tuios, light and dark theme pairs, styles for the borders, the titles, the rail and the dock, and `tuios look` to apply a setup that someone shared. If you come from tmux, screen or zellij, a keybind preset gives you the keys you know. And an idle tuios now does almost nothing: no timer runs while nothing changes.  Read [Upgrading](#upgrading) before you update. The clock, the prefix badge and the pane environment changed.  The full list is at [tuios.dev/releases](https://tuios.dev/releases).  ## Watch  | | | |---|---| | [![Show your setup](https://tuios.dev/releases/v0.10.1/show-your-setup.jpg)](https://tuios.dev/releases/v0.10.1/show-your-setup.mp4)<br>**Twelve setups, one tuios** | [![Coming from tmux](https://tuios.dev/releases/v0.10.1/coming-from-tmux.jpg)](https://tuios.dev/releases/v0.10.1/coming-from-tmux.mp4)<br>**Coming from tmux** |  Show me your setup in [discussion #849](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/849), and tell me what you still miss from tmux in [discussion #850](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/850).  ## Install  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.10.1 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.10.1   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`.  ## Highlights  ### The reply editor  [![The reply editor](https://tuios.dev/shots/composer-800.webp)](https://tuios.dev/shots/composer.webp)  The Inbox reply is now a full editor ([#769](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/769), [#770](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/770), [#771](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/771)).  - It has more than one line. `enter` sends. `ctrl+j`, `shift+enter` or `alt+enter` adds a line. The word keys, `ctrl+u`, `ctrl+k`, undo (`ctrl+z`) and redo work as in a shell. - A long paste becomes one block, such as `[Pasted text #1 · 42 lines]`. `ctrl+o` opens the block, `ctrl+e` makes it editable text, and `backspace` removes it. - A reply can carry a paste of up to 8 MB. When the message is too large to type, tuios writes each block to a file and gives the agent the path ([#812](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/812)). - `up` brings back what you sent to this agent before, and `ctrl+r` searches it. `ctrl+g` opens the draft in your `$EDITOR` ([#832](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/832)). - `@` names a pane, an agent or a file of the pane's folder ([#826](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/826)). - `esc` keeps the draft. `r` on the same pane opens it again. - You can reply to an agent on a linked machine. Before, the Inbox said "Attach there to reply" ([#835](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/835)). - The mailbox and the Inbox's other text fields use the same editor ([#828](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/828), [#830](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/830)).  ### Image paste  - `ctrl+v` in the reply editor attaches the image on the clipboard. A dropped image file works too ([#814](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/814)). - The draft shows each image as a small preview above the text, and `ctrl+o` shows it larger ([#817](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/817)). - tuios gives the image to Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Kilo, Crush and pi in the way each one reads it ([#814](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/814), [#833](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/833)). - Limits: 8 images a message, and 8 MB for the whole message.  ### The agents view  [![The agents view](https://tuios.dev/shots/agents-view-800.webp)](https://tuios.dev/shots/agents-view.webp)  `tab` in the Inbox shows every agent in one list: the agents of this session, of your other sessions, and of linked machines ([#819](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/819)).  - Each row shows the state, the session, what the agent does, how long it has been in that state, and what waits for it. - `f` filters, `s` sorts and `/` searches. - On a wide screen, the agent's thread shows beside the list: what you asked, what it said, and the tools it ran ([#822](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/822)). - `ctrl+]` shows the agent's pane. `ctrl+b i` brings you back to the same agent, with your drafts ([#823](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/823)). - `space` marks agents and `*` marks all. Then reply, allow, deny, dismiss or snooze all of them at once ([#825](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/825)).  ### The theme editor  The theme editor is a side sheet that changes the colours of a theme while the whole screen shows the result ([#827](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/827), [#829](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/829)). Open it from the command palette with "Edit theme", or with `ctrl+o` in the theme picker for a new theme.  - The Quick tab makes a whole theme from two colours: an accent and a surface. - The Roles and Pane colours tabs set each colour. Each row shows its contrast, and `f` fixes a colour that is too hard to read. - When you move to a role, every part of the screen that uses it flashes. - `u` undoes, `x` resets and `ctrl+s` saves to your themes folder.  ### Ricing  Every part of the chrome now has a style you can set. All of these are in the settings page, and `set-config` changes them live.  - **Light and dark pairs.** `appearance.theme_light` is the theme for a light terminal. tuios follows your terminal when it switches between light and dark ([#818](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/818)). - **Borders.** The focused border takes a colour for copy mode and for an agent that waits for you ([#820](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/820)). `appearance.border_focused_gradient` draws a gradient on it ([#821](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/821)). - **Titles.** `appearance.window_title_style` is `pill`, `tab` or `plain`. `window_title_align` puts the title on the left, in the centre or on the right ([#824](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/824)). - **The rail.** `appearance.sidebar.row_style` (`bar`, `marker`, `underline`, `pill`), `header_style` with section icons, and `density = "roomy"` ([#831](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/831), [#834](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/834), [#836](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/836)). - **The dock.** Workspace tabs show a mark for a bell, for new output, and for an agent that waits ([#837](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/837)). The mode pill shows when the leader key waits ([#811](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/811)). - **Theme roles.** A theme can colour each border state, the rule, the status colours, the dots and the grounds ([#815](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/815)). - **Your terminal's colours.** With `theme = "none"`, the dialogs, the rail and the dock now use greys made from your terminal's own background ([#816](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/816)). - **Themes from other themes.** A theme file can name a `base` and change only some colours ([#789](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/789)). tuios reloads a theme or glyph file when you save it. `tuios theme reload` is a hook for pywal and matugen ([#791](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/791)). - **Share a theme.** `tuios theme export` prints a theme file, also as base16. `tuios theme share` prints the theme as one line you can paste ([#813](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/813)). - **The theme picker.** `ctrl+f` shows dark or light themes only, `ctrl+r` picks one at random, `ctrl+y` copies the id, and `ctrl+d` deletes a theme of yours ([#792](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/792)).  ### tuios look  `tuios look` applies a setup that someone shared, on top of your config ([#859](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/859), [#861](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/861)).  bash tuios look apply tuios.dev/r/<id>   # or an id, an https URL or a file tuios look show tuios look remove   - A look sets the theme, the glyphs, the borders, the dock, the rail, the layout and other parts of the look. It cannot set keys, hooks, commands, hosts or anything else that runs a program. - tuios shows each change and asks first. - The look goes in `config.d/look.toml`. Your `config.toml` does not change, and `tuios look remove` puts everything back.  The [gallery on tuios.dev](https://tuios.dev/gallery) has looks to try in your browser, with the command to apply each one.  ### Keybind presets  `keybindings.preset` gives you the keys of another multiplexer ([#800](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/800), [#801](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/801), [#802](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/802)).  - `tmux`: `ctrl+b`, then `%` and `"` to split, `z` to zoom, `c`, `n` and `p` for workspaces, and the rest of the tmux keys. - `screen`: `ctrl+a`, with `ctrl+a a` to send `ctrl+a` to the pane. - `zellij`: `ctrl+g`, then a mode key. - The tuios features that a preset moves are under the leader, then `ctrl+t`. - `U` in window mode opens the preset picker. It shows the keys that your shell loses with each preset. - On first start, tuios finds your `tmux.conf`, `.screenrc` or zellij folder and offers the preset. It never switches by itself. - Your own bindings win over the preset. `tuios keybinds doctor` lists the preset keys that your bindings take ([#806](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/806)). - `keybindings.repeat` sets which actions stay armed after the leader, as tmux `bind -r` does ([#796](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/796)). - New actions with no default key: `last_workspace`, `break_pane`, `last_session` and `send_prefix` ([#795](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/795)).  ### Images in screenshots  A screenshot that you take in tuios now shows the images in a pane: kitty graphics, kitty images in Unicode placeholder cells, and sixel. Before, the screenshot showed blank cells ([#855](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/855), fixes [#678](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/678)). The image previews of the reply editor and the launcher icons show too ([#863](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/863)). `tuios screenshot` from the command line still draws no images, because the daemon does not keep them.  ### tuios.dev/shot  [![tuios.dev/shot](https://tuios.dev/shots/shot-library.png)](https://tuios.dev/docs/libraries)  The screenshot renderer of tuios is now a Go library, `tuios.dev/shot` ([#846](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/846)). It turns a grid of terminal cells into SVG, PNG, HTML, ANSI or text. It has fallback fonts for glyphs that the main font does not have, and v0.2.0 draws images on the grid ([#862](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/862)). It reads the screen of a `tuios.dev/vt` emulator directly.  ## Features  - **Wait for a program.** `tuios new-window NAME --keep-open --wait -- CMD` runs a program in a pane, waits for it, and exits with its status. `--keep-open` keeps the pane after the program exits, with its exit status under the output ([#764](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/764)). - **Cleaner captures.** `tuios capture-pane --unwrap` joins the rows that the terminal wrapped, and `--trim` drops the blank lines at the end ([#766](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/766)). - **Pause focus follows mouse.** `appearance.focus_follows_mouse_pause = "ctrl"` or `"alt"` keeps the focus where it is while you hold that key ([#768](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/768), fixes [#750](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/750)). - **Spaces in the rail.** The rail's sessions section has a heading for each space ([#760](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/760)). `tuios ls` with no daemon and `tuios ls --all-hosts` group sessions by space too ([#761](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/761)). Thanks to @abhijit-s. - **Close a workspace from its tab.** A right-click on a workspace tab in the dock has a Close row ([#786](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/786)). Thanks to @abhijit-s. - **A skill for your agent.** `tuios integration install` now also installs a short tuios skill for Claude Code, Codex, opencode and Pi ([#765](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/765)). At the start of a session, Claude Code and Codex learn which tuios pane they run in ([#767](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/767)). - **Tapes without the player.** `[tape] show_status = false` hides the tape's RUNNING pill, for screen recordings ([#841](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/841)). - **The browser build.** A page that runs tuios in the browser can set the look and input options with `set`, and load a look's theme with `themeJSON` ([#843](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/843), [#860](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/860)). - **Live demos that fit.** In the browser, the practice shell lays its output out again when a pane changes width. The logo of `fastfetch` no longer breaks apart after a split ([#864](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/864)).  ## Fixes  - **The agent's environment.** When an agent's tool call started the daemon, every pane got the agent's `NO_COLOR` and session variables. Programs drew without colour, and Codex in a pane never reported its state. Panes now leave these variables out ([#799](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/799), fixes [#798](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/798)). Thanks to @syz51. - **Kitty images that ask for one side.** An image placed with only a width or only a height now keeps its aspect. Before, a cover in go-musicfox showed as a thin strip ([#853](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/853), fixes [#851](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/851)). Thanks to @ryan4yin. - **Colours at 256 and 16 colours.** Theme colours now step down to the right palette entry. Nord's background was teal at 256 colours, and Latte's red was grey ([#858](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/858), fixes [#510](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/510)). - **Panels over a full-screen pane.** Over one pane that filled the screen, the close dialog, the Copies panel, the glyph picker, the dock and section editors and the copy dialog took the keys and drew nothing. They show now ([#786](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/786), [#838](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/838)). Thanks to @abhijit-s. - **Theme reload on macOS.** A themes folder that is a symlink, as home-manager and stow make, and `tuios theme reload` now reach a client on macOS and the BSDs ([#839](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/839)). Thanks to @abhijit-s. - **Repeat after the leader.** The resize and scroll actions now repeat after the leader as they should. Before, a second press went to the pane ([#773](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/773)). - **Picker previews on served clients.** Over `tuios ssh` and `tuios-web`, an arrow key in the theme or glyph picker changed the look for every other guest. The pickers now preview only for a local client ([#788](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/788)). - **Settings saves.** Two saves in a row could land in the wrong order and put the old value back. They now land in order ([#840](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/840)). - **The ZOOM notice** goes away when the pane leaves zoom ([#856](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/856)). - **Smooth animations.** The theme transition and the other animations now keep a steady 60 frames a second ([#774](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/774)). - **Tapes.** The which-key menu shows after a tape's leader key, and a tape that ends on `Sleep` waits for it ([#854](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/854)). - **`--pprof` with start-server** now reaches the daemon ([#775](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/775)). Thanks to @abhijit-s.  ## Performance  An idle tuios now sleeps. No timer runs in the client or the daemon while nothing changes.  - **The client.** The frame ticker stops at idle, and the maintenance tick runs only when a job is due ([#781](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/781), [#782](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/782), [#787](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/787)). With one idle client, CPU went from 0.20% to 0.05%, and context switches from about 130 a second to 9. With the clock on, from 0.5% to 0.17%. - **The clock** draws minutes by default, so it wakes the client once a minute, not every second ([#790](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/790)). - **The daemon.** The state saver, the agent monitor, the stall monitor and the cleanup loop sleep until something happens ([#776](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/776), [#777](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/777), [#778](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/778), [#779](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/779)). The daemon tells the client when the session list changes, so the client no longer asks every 3 seconds ([#794](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/794)). Daemon context switches with a client attached went from about 25 a second to under 2. - **Memory.** The daemon's queue for each pane and client starts small and grows with the backlog. With 50 idle panes, the live heap went from 30 MB to 15 MB ([#785](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/785)). The client frees the spare buffers of its panes, as the daemon does since v0.10.0 ([#762](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/762)). Thanks to @abhijit-s. - **Output.** The daemon no longer allocates for each chunk of pane output. A coloured flood scans about twice as fast ([#783](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/783)). - **Binary size.** The size budget is now 37,988,000 bytes on linux/amd64 and 35,719,000 on darwin/arm64. In v0.10.0 it was 36,620,000 and 34,465,000. The reply editor, the agents view, the theme editor, the styles, the presets, `tuios look` and images in screenshots take the difference. The which-key menus got 24 KB smaller to pay for part of it ([#805](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/805)). The ceiling stays at 40 MB.  ## Upgrading  ### The daemon  A v0.10.0 daemon works with a v0.10.1 client, and the reverse. The new fields are optional in both directions. Images in a reply, replies to another machine, the session list push and the idle changes need the new daemon. To get them, restart the daemon:  bash tuios kill-server   This closes every pane in every session, so save your work first.  ### The clock  The dock clock now shows `15:04`, with no seconds. To get the seconds back:  toml [appearance] clock_format = "15:04:05"   ### The prefix badge  While the leader waits for a key, the dock's mode pill shows it. The floating `PREFIX` badge no longer shows. To get the badge back:  toml [appearance] dock_prefix_indicator = "badge"   The badge still shows when the dock is hidden.  ### The pane environment  Panes no longer get `NO_COLOR`, `CLICOLOR`, `CLICOLOR_FORCE` and `FORCE_COLOR`, or the session variables of Claude Code and Codex, from the command that started the daemon. If you want `NO_COLOR` in your panes, set it in your shell's startup file.  ### Colours at 16 colours  At 16 colours, a pane's ANSI colours now go to your terminal as slots 0 to 15, and your terminal paints them. Before, tuios sent the theme's colours stepped down, and some came out wrong. A theme ground paints nothing at 16 colours.  ### New config keys  The defaults keep the old behaviour, except for the clock and the prefix badge above.  toml [keybindings] preset = ""                         # tmux, screen, zellij # repeat = ["..."]                  # the actions that repeat after the leader. [] turns repeat off  [appearance] theme_light = ""                    # a theme for a light terminal border_focused_gradient = ""        # "accent accent_bright" border_gradient_angle = 45 window_title_style = "pill"         # pill, tab, plain window_title_align = "auto"         # auto, left, center, right dock_prefix_indicator = "pill"      # pill, badge focus_follows_mouse_pause = "off"   # ctrl, alt, off  [appearance.sidebar] row_style = "bar"                   # bar, marker, underline, pill header_style = "auto"               # auto, plain, rule, pill, icon density = "normal"                  # normal, roomy  [dock.tabs] attention_fg = "" bell_marker = "" activity_marker = ""  [tape] show_status = true   See [CONFIGURATION.md](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/blob/main/docs/CONFIGURATION.md) and [THEMES.md](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/blob/main/docs/THEMES.md).  ### For developers  - The screenshot renderer moved from `internal/shot` to `shot/`, the `tuios.dev/shot` module, and `internal/mosaic` to `tuios.dev/shot/mosaic`. `go.work` now holds `shot` too. - The reply editor is in `internal/composer`, with no import of a tuios package. I plan to tag it as `tuios.dev/composer` after this release ([#848](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/848)). - The binary size budgets went up in commits of their own. See `docs/perf.md`.  ## Contributors  Thank you to everyone who sent a pull request for this release:  - @abhijit-s: space headings in the rail ([#760](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/760)), spaces in every `tuios ls` ([#761](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/761)), the client's spare buffers ([#762](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/762)), `--pprof` with start-server ([#775](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/775)), closing a workspace from its tab ([#786](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/786)), panels over a full-screen pane ([#838](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/838)) and theme reload on macOS ([#839](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/839)) - @syz51: the agent's environment in panes ([#799](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/799)) - @ryan4yin: kitty images that ask for one side ([#853](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/853))  And thanks to everyone who reported bugs and asked for features: @kdnk for the focus follows mouse pause ([#750](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/750)), @syz51 for the agent's environment ([#798](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/798)), @iTerminate for images in screenshots ([#678](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/678)) and @ryan4yin for the go-musicfox cover ([#851](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/851)).  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.10.0...v0.10.1  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.10.0...v0.10.1 
```

### 2026-10-19T12:52:27Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.8.0  GPUI Kit 0.8.0 adds an installable JavaScript Shell runtime, Linux WebView support, and a public rendering boundary for external editors. It also adds lighter syntax highlighting with syntect, native-style scroll bounce, richer completion highlighting, and improvements to text selection, tables, focus and accessibility.  ## Runtime and platforms  - **Shell runtime**: run and scaffold JavaScript applications with `gpui-shell`; package them with `bundle`, and use `dev` for reloading during development. ([#3464](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3464), [#3487](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3487)) - **WebView**: use `gpui-webview` on macOS, Windows and Linux X11/XWayland. GPUI Fast enables overlays above native pages on macOS and Linux; bounds and clipping updates preserve the page viewport. ([#3395](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3395), [#3499](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3499), [#3591](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3591))  This release updates the optional GPUI Fast backend to **0.1.10**. The default backend remains pinned to **gpui-pre 0.3.9**, the latest published snapshot. The WebAssembly example uses wasm-bindgen **0.2.127** to match the updated backend dependency graph.  ## Editor and text input  - **External editors**: `gpui_kit::base::text::editor_host` lets an editor render a `BlockNode` tree through TextView and collect keyed leaf geometry while retaining its own document model, selection, commands and undo. ([#3580](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3580)) - **Syntax highlighting**: enable the `syntect` feature for a lighter highlighter with bat’s syntax collection; tree-sitter takes precedence when both are enabled. ([#3512](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3512)) - **Completion highlighting**: configure `Lsp::with_completion_highlighter` or `set_completion_highlighter` with `highlight_prefix`, `highlight_contains`, `highlight_characters`, or a host callback returning byte ranges. ([#3496](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3496)) - **Wrapping**: `display_map()` and `prewrap(...)` are available on InputState and TextareaState as well as EditorState. `Editor::size` supports the shared control sizes. ([#3483](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3483), [#3495](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3495), [#3564](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3564), [#3480](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3480)) - **Software keyboards**: configure `input_action`, `autocorrect` and `autocapitalize`; masked fields are identified as passwords, and clear/mask controls have accessible names. ([#3557](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3557), [#3555](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3555), [#3554](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3554), [#3562](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3562)) - Fixed caret clipping in one-row viewports, caret visibility during reflow and height changes, vertical scroll clamping, scrollbar reach in auto-growing textareas, and caret crispness on click. ([#3583](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3583), [#3477](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3477), [#3527](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3527), [#3515](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3515)) - Custom Find queries and case changes start near the viewport; an unchanged query retains its active occurrence. Android’s Back button keeps entered text when `clean_on_escape` is enabled. ([#3584](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3584), [#3556](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3556))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - **Selection format**: `SelectionFormat::SourceAcrossStyles` copies plain text within a single style and Markdown source across styles. ([#3517](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3517)) - Copy uses the current gesture before repaint, including virtualized rich content. Unmounting a selection endpoint retires the old selection, so retaining and remounting a TextView cannot revive it. ([#3589](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3589), [#3588](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3588)) - HTML `<br>` creates a line break, and `<pre>` / `<pre><code>` preserve their text and whitespace through HTML minification. ([#3569](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3569), [#3570](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3570), [#3581](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3581))  ## Scrolling and motion  - **Scroll bounce**: precise scrolling uses a shared boundary-bounce engine, enabled by default on macOS, iOS and Android. Desktop motion follows NSScrollView; ordinary line-wheel input does not bounce. Nested areas retain their own gestures. ([#3500](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3500), [#3484](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3484)) - **Horizontal strips**: `ScrollableMask::vertical_wheel` maps a plain mouse wheel to horizontal scrolling and chains to the parent at the edge. ([#3518](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3518)) - Cubic-bezier easing preserves anticipation and overshoot, and transitions and presence retain duration precision. ([#3432](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3432), [#3590](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3590))  ## Components  - **DataTable**: fit a column by double-clicking its resize handle or calling `fit_col`, with widths supplied by `TableDelegate::fit_column`; inspect the result with `col_width`. Configure `fixed_cols_border`, `select_on_modifier_click` and `focus_ring`. ([#3489](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3489), [#3482](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3482), [#3523](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3523), [#3565](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3565)) - **DataTable**: Home/End select the first/last row in row mode; row selection preserves event propagation, and default column resizing is capped at 1200px while preserving explicitly assigned initial widths. ([#3471](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3471), [#3469](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3469), [#3472](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3472)) - **Resizable**: use the standalone `resize_handle` for application-owned handles and `ResizablePanel::fixed` to preserve a panel’s size when its container changes. ([#3302](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3302), [#3478](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3478)) - **Dock**: subscribe to `DockToggleEvent` to distinguish user-driven dock toggles. ([#3546](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3546)) - **MessageScroller**: read the current reading position through `MessageScrollerState::logical_scroll_top`. ([#3485](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3485)) - **Select**: keyboard confirmation and reconciliation of changed items preserve the intended selection. ([#3446](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3446)) - **Focus and accessibility**: focus traps preserve containment and accessibility; disabled controls report their disabled state; sliders can be named with `accessibility_label`; settings fields inherit their setting’s title. ([#3443](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3443), [#3531](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3531), [#3494](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3494), [#3524](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3524), [#3582](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3582)) - **Tooltip**: switching between triggers is direction-independent; a pressed trigger remains dismissed until the pointer leaves, and removing a trigger dismisses its tooltip. ([#3501](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3501), [#3492](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3492), [#3528](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3528)) - **Popover**: keyboard activation inside content remains available. ([#3586](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3586)) - **TabBar**: overflow-menu actions call the tab’s own handler, the sliding indicator appears after the first frame, and `TabVariant::Folder` adds a folder presentation. ([#3568](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3568), [#3574](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3574), [#3415](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3415)) - **Tree**: selection and toggling happen on click rather than mouse-down. ([#3567](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3567))  ## Shell APIs  - **Extensible catalog**: `gpui_shell::components::registry()` returns the unfrozen component catalog for host additions. Registered state objects support `release()` so applications can promptly dispose of retained resources. ([#3542](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3542), [#3544](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3544)) - **Lists and transcripts**: script lists support `with_follow_mode("tail")` and `on_visible_range_change`; item-count changes preserve the reader’s position. MessageScrollerState exposes append/prepend/splice, measurement and scrolling operations. ([#3548](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3548), [#3549](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3549), [#3547](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3547), [#3541](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3541)) - **Script components**: the catalog includes WebView/WebViewState, and Base TextareaState exposes `set_submit_on_enter` for message composers. ([#3532](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3532), [#3550](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3550)) - **Runtime compatibility**: scripts gain gradient backgrounds and custom shadows, more browser/Node-compatible runtime APIs, and corrected filesystem link handling. Script errors report their cause, and launching a window on Wayland no longer crashes during activation. ([#3534](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3534), [#3505](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3505), [#3510](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3510), [#3561](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3561), [#3509](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3509), [#3508](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3508))  ## Breaking Changes  ### Support Linux on X11 and rename the crate to gpui-webview ([#3395](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3395))  The crate is renamed from `gpui-wry` to `gpui-webview`.  diff  [dependencies] -gpui-wry = "0.7.1" +gpui-webview = "0.7.1"   diff -use gpui_wry::WebView; +use gpui_webview::WebView;   On Linux, applications using the WebView must start on X11:  diff -gpui_kit::application().run(|cx| { /* ... */ }); +#[cfg(target_os = "linux")] +let app = gpui_kit::platform::linux(WindowingModes::X11); +#[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))] +let app = gpui_kit::application(); +app.run(|cx| { /* ... */ });   ### Support gpui-pre and make `gpui-fast` optional again ([#3591](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3591))  Applications that relied on `gpui-webview` switching them to GPUI Fast must now select GPUI Fast on `gpui-kit` and enable the WebView feature with it, to keep overlays above the page on macOS and Linux:  diff  [dependencies] -gpui-kit = "0.7" -gpui-webview = "0.7" +gpui-kit = { version = "0.7", features = ["gpui-fast"] } +gpui-webview = { version = "0.7", features = ["gpui-fast"] }   Without both they build on gpui-pre; with only one of them, `gpui-webview` fails to compile and names the missing feature.  ### Add SelectionFormat::SourceAcrossStyles ([#3517](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3517))  `SelectionFormat` is not `#[non_exhaustive]`, so code that matches it exhaustively needs an arm for the new variant.  diff  match format {      SelectionFormat::Plain => ...,      SelectionFormat::Source => ..., +    SelectionFormat::SourceAcrossStyles => ...,  }   ### Let an application build its own resize handle ([#3302](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3302))  `ResizeHandle::on_drag` was `#[doc(hidden)]`; its constructor now takes `&T` instead of `Rc<T>`:  diff - .on_drag(DragPanel, |drag, _, _, cx| cx.new(|_| drag.deref().clone())) + .on_drag(DragPanel, |drag, _, _, cx| cx.new(|_| drag.clone()))   ### Bounce scrollable content like NSScrollView, by default on macOS ([#3500](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3500))  No existing signature changes. Every existing call still compiles. Four defaults change behavior, compared with `main`:  **1. `ScrollBounce` is enabled by default on macOS.** On `main` it was enabled only on iOS and Android. A `ScrollBounce` created without `.enabled(..)` now bounces on macOS too. To keep it still:  diff - ScrollBounce::new(id, &handle, child) + ScrollBounce::new(id, &handle, child).enabled(false)   **2. `ScrollBounce` owns vertical wheel scrolling by default.** On `main`, the child scrolled itself and the wrapper only stretched the deltas the child left unused. Now the wrapper scrolls the handle itself, through the shared engine. Code that relied on the child-owned mode, such as touch emulation or synthesized drags, must ask for it:  diff - ScrollBounce::new(id, &handle, child).enabled(true) + ScrollBounce::new(id, &handle, child).enabled(true).scroll_wheel(false)   **3. `ScrollBounceMotion::default()` changes on macOS, Windows and Linux.** The defaults are now tuned to match NSScrollView, replacing the UIScrollView tuning: - `tracking` goes from 0.55 to 0.075. - `response` goes from 524 ms to ≈503 ms. - The stretch is capped at 36 px.  iOS and Android are unchanged. To keep the previous feel:  diff - ScrollBounce::new(id, &handle, child) + ScrollBounce::new(id, &handle, child).motion( +     ScrollBounceMotion::default() +         .with_tracking(0.55) +         .with_response(Duration::from_millis(524)), + )   **4. Scrollable components scroll differently.** - **They bounce on macOS.** Scrollable (`overflow_y_scrollbar()`), List, DataTable, Tree, Select, Command, Textarea, Editor, Sidebar, Diff, MessageScroller, the settings page and scrollable TextView now bounce on precise scrolling. On `main` none of them did. Each one that has a builder can turn it off:    diff   - List::new(&state)   + List::new(&state).scroll_bounce(false)     - **Nested areas no longer scroll together, on every platform.** The innermost area under the pointer takes the whole gesture, including at its ends. On `main`:   - Nested GPUI scroll divs scrolled together.   - MessageScroller handed the wheel to an ancestor scroller once it reached an end.    Neither happens now. Sidebar, Diff, MessageScroller, the settings page and TextView have no `scroll_bounce` builder; they always follow the platform default.  Everything else listed under Public API is new in this PR, and so additive.  ### Keep a scroll over text that can scroll, even at its end ([#3484](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3484))  Scrolling over a textarea or editor whose text can scroll no longer reaches the page around it once the text is at its end: the rest of that swipe, its momentum, and later wheel flicks over the text stay in the input. Text that fits, and vertical scrolling over a single-line input, still scroll the page. No setting restores the old behavior; to scroll the page, move the pointer off the input.  ### Add `Folder` variant ([#3415](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3415))  `TabVariant` is not `#[non_exhaustive]`, so exhaustive matches need the new arm:  diff  match variant {      ... +    TabVariant::Folder => ...,  }   ### Keep cubic-bezier anticipation and overshoot in Easing ([#3432](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3432))  - Code that relied on `Easing::sample` staying within `[0, 1]` for an out-of-range cubic-bezier curve now sees overshoot:  diff - let alpha = easing.sample(delta); + let alpha = easing.sample(delta).clamp(0., 1.);   ### Select the first or last row on Home and End in row mode ([#3471](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3471))  In row mode, Home and End select the first or last row instead of the first or last column. No setting restores the old behavior: Left and Right still move to a column, and a host that wants Home and End to pick a column can select one with `set_selected_col`.  ### Don't stop event propagation in `set_selected_row` ([#3469](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3469))  `TableState::set_selected_row` no longer stops event propagation. Custom handlers that require it must call `cx.stop_propagation()` explicitly.  ### Skip the notify in `set_line_number` when nothing changes ([#3475](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3475))  In a code editor, `set_line_number` no longer notifies when the line numbers are already as asked. A caller that relied on that call to re-render the editor notifies it itself:  diff   state.set_line_number(true, window, cx); + cx.notify();   ### Notify from set_searchable only when it changes ([#3576](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3576))  `ListState::set_searchable` no longer notifies when the list is already searchable or not as asked. A caller that relied on that call to re-render the list notifies it itself:  diff   list.set_searchable(true, cx); + cx.notify();   ### Add `bundle` to package an application, and a `dev` command ([#3487](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3487))  `--dev` no longer implies `--watch` or a debug log. `dev` is the command that reloads.  diff - gpui-shell run -d app --dev + gpui-shell dev -d app          # reload + debug log + gpui-shell dev -d app --dev    # … and the relaxed sandbox, as before   `InvocationKind` gains a variant, so an exhaustive `match` over it needs a `Bundle` arm.  diff   match gpui_shell::host::parse_invocation(arguments)? {       InvocationKind::Run => …, +     InvocationKind::Bundle => …,       …   }   ### Fix the runtime gaps found by the gpui-shell benchmark apps ([#3505](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3505))  `gpui-shell run -d <dir>` with a manifest now stores data under the manifest `id`, the same place the installed copy uses, instead of `<dir>-<hash>`. Data written by earlier development runs stays in the old directory.  `process.env` was absent before; scripts that feature-detected it with `typeof process.env === "undefined"` now see an object.  `Capabilities`' read and write roots for a local run now include the manifest's roots rather than only the app and data directories, so `gpui-shell list` reports a wider grant for manifests that ask for one.  ### Extend UI testing with semantic queries and Linux rendering ([#3402](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3402))  Existing pointer helpers now retain held modifiers. Callers that deliberately require an unmodified click while modifiers are held can request it explicitly; ordinary calls and signatures remain valid.  diff - window.click("save", cx); // previously reset held modifiers implicitly + window.click_with_options("save", ClickOptions::new(), cx); // explicitly unmodified, then restores held state   Nested context-menu callers keep the same construction. The corrected interaction is:  diff - window.right_click("inner-trigger", cx); // both inner and ancestor menus opened + window.right_click("inner-trigger", cx); // only the innermost menu opens   ### Add find_all and configurable clicks to the UI test harness ([#3384](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3384))  `TestWindowExt` gains two required methods: `find_all` and `click_with_options`. Downstream implementations must implement both. `click_with_modifiers` has a default implementation through `click_with_options`. Call sites continue to import only `TestWindowExt` for all window queries and interactions.  diff  impl TestWindowExt for MyTestWindow {      // Existing methods remain. +    fn find_all(&self, id: impl Into<ElementId>) -> Vec<ElementSnapshot> { +        self.window.find_all(id) +    } +    fn click_with_options(&mut self, id: impl Into<ElementId>, options: ClickOptions, cx: &mut App) { +        self.window.click_with_options(id, options, cx); +    }  }   Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!  ## What's Changed * kit: Add optional gpui-fast backend ### 2026-10-20T10:10:45Z [**Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP**](https://github.com/Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP)
```

```
Since 0.9.7:  - Fix headless `open_database` hang/kill on Windows venv interpreters (#20): worker readiness is matched by the reserved bridge port (redirector launches register a different pid), managed workers are keyed by `instance_id`, and forced kills take down the whole process tree (`taskkill /T /F`) so venv redirectors no longer orphan workers. - hcli install scripts resolve the IDA user dir per platform (%APPDATA%/Hex-Rays/IDA Pro on Windows, ~/.idapro elsewhere). - Add pytest CI (ubuntu + windows); lifecycle matching unit tests. 
```

### 2026-10-16T00:38:48Z [**komari-monitor/komari**](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari)

```
## 重大更改 BREAKING CHANGES - 移除剪贴板功能，`/admin/clipboard` 路由及对应 RPC 不再可用。 - 移除 xterm.js 全局终端设置，`/admin/settings/xtermjs` 路由及 `admin:getXtermjsSettings`、`admin:setXtermjsSettings` 不再可用。 - 移除负载告警规则，`/admin/notification/load` 路由及对应 RPC 不再可用。 - 通知配置改为插件式通知渠道。旧消息发送器配置接口（`/admin/settings/message-sender`、`admin:getMessageSenderProvider`、`admin:setMessageSenderProvider`）以及内置 Bark、Email、JavaScript、Server酱³、Server酱 Turbo、Telegram 渠道已移除；Webhook 渠道保留。请安装并启用所需的通知渠道插件，通过 `/admin/notification/channels` 和 `/admin/notification/channel/configuration` 重新配置。 - 移除访客审计 RPC `public:recordVisitorEvent`。   ## 主要更新内容  - **通知系统全面重写**：移除旧的 `messageSender` 工厂模式（bark、email、Server酱³、Server酱Turbo、Telegram 等内置通道），替换为统一的 `NotificationChannel` 注册表。内置通道保留 Webhook 和 JavaScript，新增插件可通过 `server.registerNotificationChannel()` 注册自定义通知通道。JavaScript 通道现支持 `sendEvent(event)` 函数（回退到 `sendMessage`）。 - **Agent 协议 v2 扩展**：新增 `agent.startupConfig` / `agent.startupConfig.result` 方法，管理员可在线获取 Agent 完整启动参数（含凭据）；新增 `agent.switchVersion` 方法，支持远程请求 Agent 切换版本。对应新增 `admin:getAgentStartupConfig` 和 `admin:switchAgentVersion` RPC 接口。 - **WebSocket 压缩与心跳**：所有 WebSocket 连接（终端、客户端 v2、公共推送）现与浏览器协商压缩算法；v2 客户端连接引入 75 秒读超时 + Ping/Pong 心跳机制，替代原先 11 秒的固定读等待。 - **JS 运行时增强**：`URL` 和 `URLSearchParams` 注入为全局变量；新增 `tls` 模块（`tls.connect` / `tls.createConnection`），支持 STARTTLS 升级；`net` 模块新增 `AttachSocket` / `DetachSocket` 以配合 TLS 握手。 - **前端静态资源改为磁盘缓存**：启动时将内嵌 dist 解压至 `./cache/dist` 目录，不再全量驻留内存。  ## Bug修复  - 修复流量上报重试时可能将历史累计值误计为增量流量的问题（引入 `maxTrafficReportGap` 15 分钟上限及写锁串行化基线读写）。 - 优化长时间断报的识别逻辑，减少误判。  ## 其他  - 移除剪贴板功能（数据库表、API 路由、RPC 方法全部删除）。 - 移除负载通知（LoadNotification）功能及相关定时任务。 - 移除未落地的访客审计（Visitor Audit）配置项。 - 移除 xtermjs 独立设置接口。 - Linux 构建添加 `-extldflags=-Wl,--strip-all` 减小二进制体积；zstd 编码器降低并发与内存占用；上报批处理队列从 4096 缩减至 512。 - Docker 及 systemd 服务新增 `GODEBUG=disablethp=1` 环境变量。 - 新增 `admin:getLastRunVersion` 接口，返回本次启动前记录的版本号。 - `/admin` 路径现 302 重定向至 `/admin/dashboard`。  ### Commits  - 重定向旧版本的主题接口？ ([4ec22ed](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/4ec22edf1f7de4374356c8b886ec73a25b619e17)) @Akizon77 - 重写1下通知 ([d15f051](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/d15f0512c753c7dd62ce9b873a7661726ccabe4d)) @Akizon77 - 裁剪1下 ([f57461f](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/f57461f87f6d14d09df89f927c30ad8e676a5c4e)) @Akizon77 - 优化内存的分配喵，再压1点！ ([7e91794](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/7e91794f246869991d566a9f75a777e4322c1664)) @Akizon77 - 1点点小参数优化内存 ([2c3f228](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/2c3f2281fad28299f01d91c6fb9ecdcd43976522)) @Akizon77 - 添加1个小参数优化内存 ([154d2b8](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/154d2b8af2bc177ad313194b794861c38ada52ce)) @Akizon77 - 没有落地的访客审计功能先删掉吧 ([14bc87d](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/14bc87d1e9ca39205102b5f8bdea8c86bcc6ab9c)) @Akizon77 - xterm设置适用面有1点太少了 ([68c284d](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/68c284d043de436ce2da70674d67a92b79fa209c)) @Akizon77 - 剪切板也是1个用的比较少的功能，移除了喵 ([7d692d2](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/7d692d2faf276a7c12520faaa8c41c38584bb484)) @Akizon77 - 支持拿到Agent的运行参数了 ([29d309f](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/29d309f4a11af20e11463b9a8d512ad077a7baec)) @Akizon77 - 对于#675，有一个更简洁的方案可以试1下 ([70df320](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/70df32096f5dcd3247c6ca6463b829f52b402c1c)) @Akizon77 - Agent支持切换版本协议 ([9812acf](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/9812acfd1106f29fa2f6d193207b005396bc78e0)) @Akizon77 - 识别长时间断报优化一下 ([39cb3f5](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/39cb3f59b2837e8d052203bfbbf3187888e5fa4c)) @Akizon77 - js通知先加回来 ([a68704a](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/a68704a42560b5297343a4f99d0143c34add3de5)) @Akizon77 - 加1个通知升级提醒 ([e48aa1e](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/e48aa1e76931132fb5dc93ce53763dd59df83d9f)) @Akizon77 - 和浏览器1起协商ws压缩算法 ([13cbdca](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/13cbdca545e3427f720cfe55a0db787048a0a3be)) @Akizon77 - js通知沿用旧版本的逻辑 ([1b76b7a](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/commit/1b76b7ad5bc3de6529bb7f0577069b4ac241fec3)) @Akizon77  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/compare/1.5.1...1.6.0
```

```
# Chatless 0.7.4 发布  ## 下载 ### Windows - [Chatless Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless_0.7.4_x64-setup.exe) - [Chatless MSI (.msi)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless_0.7.4_x64_en-US.msi)            ### macOS - [Apple Silicon](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless_0.7.4_aarch64.dmg) - [Intel 处理器（通过 Rosetta 2 运行）](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless_0.7.4_aarch64.dmg)  > **打不开、提示「已损坏」？** 拖进 Applications 之后，终端运行下面这一行就好（本构建是 ad-hoc 签名，没有做 Apple 公证）： > `sudo xattr -r -d com.apple.quarantine /Applications/Chatless.app`  ### Linux - [Red Hat/CentOS/Fedora (.rpm)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless-0.7.4-1.x86_64.rpm) - [Ubuntu/Debian (.deb)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless_0.7.4_amd64.deb) - [通用格式 (.AppImage)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.4/Chatless_0.7.4_amd64.AppImage)            ---  ## 安装说明  ### Windows 1. 下载 .exe 文件（推荐）或 .msi 文件 2. 双击运行安装程序 3. 按提示完成安装  ### macOS 1. 根据处理器选择版本：    - Apple Silicon (M1/M2/M3): 下载 aarch64.dmg    - Intel 处理器: 下载 aarch64.dmg 通过 Rosetta 2 运行 2. 双击打开，拖拽到 Applications 文件夹 3. 从 Applications 启动应用  **注意**: 首次启动可能提示"已损坏"，请： 1. 点击"取消" 2. 打开终端，运行:   3. 重新启动应用  ### Linux 1. 根据发行版选择格式：    - Red Hat/CentOS/Fedora: 下载 .rpm 文件    - Ubuntu/Debian: 下载 .deb 文件    - 其他发行版: 下载 .AppImage 文件 2. 使用包管理器安装或直接运行  ---  ## 常见问题  **Windows 闪退**: 安装 [Visual C++ 运行库](https://aka.ms/vs/17/release/vc_redist.x64.exe)  **macOS 无法打开**: 使用终端命令移除隔离属性（见上方说明）  **Linux 权限问题**: 确保给予执行权限 `chmod +x`  ---  ## 文档  - [安装指南](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/blob/main/INSTALLATION_INSTRUCTIONS.md) - [项目主页](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless) - [问题反馈](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/issues)  ---  ## 更新内容  ### 修复 - 请求不再自行附带 `stop` 列表（四个对话模板边界 token）：用户没有设置过的采样参数不再替他发出去；模板泄漏仍由客户端在流里截断，行为不变 - 时间上下文只精确到日期：同一会话连续两轮请求的 system 前缀逐字节一致，服务端的前缀缓存才有机会命中（此前每分钟变一次，等于整段重新 prefill） - 改了会话参数后，输入框下方不再多出一条「参数」标签占用一行高度；会话参数入口仍在右上角菜单 - 托盘图标点击恢复窗口：Linux 上点击图标打开菜单，菜单第一项是「显示窗口」；Windows/macOS 保留点击切换。菜单里任何一项执行前都会先把窗口带回来，并移除了多余的「最小化」 - 托盘菜单不再因为一个已最小化的窗口而看起来「点了没反应」  ### 优化 - 玻璃主题重做：窗口只有一层玻璃，全应用只有一处实时模糊；侧栏变成同一张玻璃上的平面区（用分隔线代替第二张卡片），输入框是唯一抬起来的面 - 没选壁纸时，背景收敛为单一色相的柔雾（此前青/蓝/琥珀三层渐变压在每个强调色后面） - 选了壁纸时按「氛围」处理：一次降采样并烘焙模糊（长边 1600），旧壁纸自动补生成，再压一层暗色或白纱，把任何照片压进可读范围。代价是壁纸更接近氛围而不是相册——这是正文与次要文字对比度下限的必然结果 - 弹窗、菜单、通知与提示改为近不透明，长文字不再压在照片上 - 「减少动态效果」现在是关闭实时模糊并改用更实的填充，而不是把模糊半径调小（成本一样，观感更差） 
```

### 2026-10-17T14:14:50Z [**devld/go-drive**](https://github.com/devld/go-drive)

```
## Highlights  * Connect MEGA cloud storage, including two-factor sign-in, encrypted transfers, and reusable sessions. * Browse ZIP, 7z, and RAR archives, preview their contents, and download selected files or folders as ZIP packages. * Edit Markdown visually with Mermaid diagram rendering, and enjoy improved video controls on desktop and mobile. * Stored Drive credentials and OAuth data are now encrypted at rest.  ## Breaking Changes and Upgrade Notes  * **Encryption key.** Existing stored secrets are encrypted automatically on startup. Preserve `data-dir/encryption.key`, or keep using the same `GO_DRIVE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` if configured. Database backups require the same key to restore. If both sources exist, they must match. * **Unfinished uploads.** The temporary storage format for chunked uploads has changed. Uploads left unfinished before upgrading must be restarted. * **Custom Script Drives.** Update scripts to the current template and declarations. The Drive environment uses `env` / `DriveEnv` in place of `utils` / `DriveUtils`; reader methods now receive a `ReaderRange` instead of separate start and size arguments. Host `Entry.name` has been removed; derive names from `Entry.path`. Thumbnail metadata now uses `hasThumbnail` in place of `selfThumbnail` and `thumbnailUrl`. * **Custom integrations.** The old `POST /archive` endpoint is replaced by artifact-based ZIP preparation and download. Go extensions must also adapt to the updated entry and reader interfaces, consolidated error types, and `NewClassSet` / `With` in place of the exported `BuiltinClasses` catalog. * **Local filesystem paths.** Paths must use the exact names stored on disk. Alternate casing and other filesystem aliases are rejected to prevent permission bypasses. Parent directories must be readable so names can be verified.  ## New Features and Improvements  * MEGA supports configurable root folders and content caching. Deleted or replaced files go to the MEGA rubbish bin by default, with an option for permanent deletion. * Archive previews show directory contents and symbolic-link targets. Selected archive members and ordinary Drive files can be packaged for download. * Markdown files open in a visual editor with Mermaid diagrams. Diagram source can be shown or hidden while editing, and Mermaid loads only when needed. * Video previews offer mouse and touch controls, automatic control hiding, double-tap playback, and swipe-down dismissal on touch devices. * WebP is included in the default image previews. * Drive configuration reloads run in the background with progress feedback. Admin Drive controls work better on mobile and include contextual documentation links. * File menus can start downloads directly, and rename dialogs select the filename without its extension. * Chunked uploads report progress more consistently and reject simultaneous writes to the same chunk. * Shared file-content caches expire after five minutes by default, reducing stale reads after external changes. Archive work has configurable concurrency limits. * Editor loading is lighter, and frontend dependencies have been updated.  ## Bug Fixes  * Fixed a local filesystem permission bypass involving alternate path spellings on filesystems that treat different names as equivalent. * Pasting images into the Markdown editor no longer embeds them as base64 data. Image uploads and text pasting remain available. * Interrupted WebDAV uploads no longer replace existing files with incomplete data. * Remote storage authentication failures no longer appear as an expired go-drive login; Drive initialization errors are shown in the admin form. * Background task errors display translated messages, and streaming responses stop safely when requests end. * Partial file caches are reused correctly, and writes through the shared file layer are saved back to the Drive. * Nested custom request-header fields retain their form types when saved. * Entry menus behave more reliably on iOS, and the upload-task button stays hidden when there are no tasks.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/devld/go-drive/compare/v0.17.0...v0.18.0 
```

### 2026-10-19T10:37:11Z [**std-microblock/breeze-shell**](https://github.com/std-microblock/breeze-shell)

```
## 中文  ### 功能特性 - **(更新)** 自更新重写为后台流程：启动后定时检查，自动下载并校验文件大小、SHA-256、文件版本与 x64 结构，完成后提示重启资源管理器生效 - **(更新)** 新版本注入后反复崩溃时自动回滚到上一版 `shell.dll`，并把该版本记为坏版本、不再自动升级 - **(更新)** 注入器纳入更新链路：旧的注册表与计划任务启动项自动迁移到规范安装位置，由它接管后续注入 - **(配置)** 设置页按 Fluent 设计重写，主题与动画可直接预览，数值项改用滑杆编辑 - **(诊断)** 用日志页和问题页取代调试控制台，可查看分级日志、配置覆盖项与脚本 / 运行时问题 - **(脚本)** 新增文件与文件夹占用查询，可列出占用进程、结束进程，并支持一次性批量判断 - **(脚本)** 自绘控件可参与菜单键盘导航：获得焦点、接收按键、拿到焦点变化回调，选中行自动滚入视野 - **(脚本)** 控件能力扩展：暴露绝对坐标与鼠标指针样式，新增位图图标控件，菜单尺寸可统计原生菜单项，菜单项支持显隐 - **(脚本)** `flex` 支持 `on_key_down`、换行、最小尺寸、`align-self`、滚动条样式与文本省略号 - **(菜单)** 输入框聚焦时由菜单窗口接管键盘以支持输入法，关闭后归还焦点；回车直接激活首个可见项，嵌入控件首帧即可聚焦 - **(渲染)** 菜单、任务栏、注入器与 JS 控件迁移到事件驱动的 breeze-ui，空闲时不再持续重绘 - **(配置)** 字体改为按字体族名配置并支持回退链与彩色 emoji，新增 `font.locale`（默认优先中文） - **(配置)** 支持用 `BREEZE_DATA_DIR` 环境变量重定向数据目录  ### 问题修复 - **(菜单)** 修复「发送到」子菜单空白，以及菜单里多出用户目录项 - **(菜单)** 修复滚轮与触控板消息被投给焦点窗口后菜单无法滚动 - **(菜单)** 修复掉帧时悬停子菜单瞬间弹出，以及自绘菜单定位时跳到屏幕顶端 - **(菜单)** 修复自绘菜单项的位图图标丢失与背景半透明 - **(菜单)** 菜单文本清理零宽字符，不再出现缺字形方块，非法 UTF-16 也不再抛异常 - **(兼容)** 修复 OneCommander 新建文件夹被重复触发 - **(兼容)** ExplorerFrame 补丁改为只走经典菜单判定并跳过不可读引用，失败时给出告警 - **(兼容)** 不支持 host backdrop 的系统回退到 DWM 亚克力，不再出现空白背景 - **(稳定性)** 菜单 hook 的异常不再逃逸到资源管理器，键盘 hook 异常时正确释放，卸载时还原所有打过的补丁 - **(稳定性)** 修复异步回调与定时器跨线程拷贝引起的崩溃，重复定时器不再丢失回调 - **(稳定性)** 恢复崩溃上报，并由 `shell.dll` 自己记录崩溃归因 - **(配置)** 首次运行不再强制打开调试控制台，配置重载出错不再拖垮文件监视线程，用户目录变量缺失时自动回退 - **(渲染)** 渲染目标初始化失败时跳过本次显示，不再崩溃  ### 代码重构 - **(脚本)** JS 控件回调改为脱离渲染循环派发，同时跳过全透明的填充  ### 构建系统 - **(构建)** 固定 reflect-cpp 版本以规避 clang-cl 22 的编译 ICE，并让 sentry-native 不再把新增警告当成错误 - **(依赖)** 升级 breeze-js 与 breeze-ui 到最新版本  ### 杂项 - **(版本)** 版本号更新  ---  ## English  ### Features - **(update)** Self-update is now a background flow: it checks periodically after startup, downloads and verifies every build (size, SHA-256, file version, x64 image), and offers to restart Explorer once it is in place - **(update)** If a freshly installed build keeps crashing, the previous `shell.dll` is restored automatically and that version is never auto-installed again - **(update)** The injector is part of the update too: legacy Run-key and scheduled-task entries are migrated to the canonical install location, which takes over injection from then on - **(settings)** Settings pages rebuilt on a Fluent design system: themes and animations preview live, and numeric fields use sliders - **(diagnostics)** The debug console is replaced by a log page and a problems page covering leveled logs, config overrides, and script/runtime issues - **(script)** New file and folder in-use query: list the processes holding a path, terminate them, or ask once for a whole batch - **(script)** Custom widgets can join menu keyboard navigation: they take focus, receive keys, get focus/blur callbacks, and the selected row scrolls into view - **(script)** More for widgets: absolute coordinates and cursor styles, a bitmap icon widget, native item counts in menu sizing, and per-item visibility - **(script)** `flex` supports `on_key_down`, wrapping, min sizes, `align-self`, scrollbar styling, and text ellipsis - **(menu)** When an input field is focused the menu window takes over the keyboard so IME works, and hands focus back on close; Enter activates the first visible item, and embedded widgets are focusable from the first frame - **(render)** Menus, taskbar, injector and JS widgets moved to the event-driven breeze-ui and no longer redraw while idle - **(config)** Fonts are configured by family name with a fallback chain and color emoji; new `font.locale`, defaulting to Chinese-first glyphs - **(config)** `BREEZE_DATA_DIR` can redirect the data directory  ### Fixes - **(menu)** Fixed the empty "Send to" submenu and stray user-folder entries in the menu - **(menu)** Fixed menus ignoring scrolling when wheel and touchpad messages go to the focused window - **(menu)** Fixed submenus popping open instantly when frames drop, and self-drawn menus jumping to the top of the screen - **(menu)** Fixed bitmap icons disappearing from custom menu items and rendering semi-transparent - **(menu)** Menu text is stripped of zero-width characters so no more missing-glyph boxes appear, and malformed UTF-16 no longer throws - **(compat)** Fixed OneCommander creating a new folder twice - **(compat)** The ExplorerFrame patch now only takes the classic-menu path and skips unreadable references, warning instead of failing silently - **(compat)** Systems without host backdrop support fall back to DWM acrylic instead of showing an empty background - **(stability)** Exceptions from menu hooks no longer escape into Explorer, keyboard hooks are released on error, and every patched call site is restored on unload - **(stability)** Fixed crashes caused by async callbacks and timers being copied across threads, and repeating timers keep their callback - **(stability)** Crash reporting is back, and `shell.dll` now records its own crash attribution - **(config)** First run no longer forces the debug console open, config reload errors no longer take down the file watcher, and a missing user-profile variable falls back gracefully - **(render)** A failed render target init now skips that draw instead of crashing  ### Code Refactoring - **(script)** Widget callbacks are dispatched off the render loop, and fully transparent fills are skipped  ### Build System - **(build)** Pinned reflect-cpp to dodge a clang-cl 22 ICE, and stopped sentry-native from treating its new warnings as errors - **(deps)** Bumped breeze-js and breeze-ui to their latest revisions  ### Chores - **(version)** Version bump 
```

```
<!-- [INPUT]: GitHub #66 深色模式、#67 局域网 HTTP 模型地址，与 2.0.2 版本契约。 [OUTPUT]: 发布工作流使用的本次更新说明、玩家操作步骤与现场验收边界。 [POS]: 当前版本交付说明；操作入口交给 README，完整需求与历史验收交给 docs/issues.md。 [PROTOCOL]: 变更时更新此头部，然后检查 CLAUDE.md --> # DeepRant 2.0.2  游戏内嘴臭交流，自动翻译多语言。  - 处理 [#66：深色模式](https://github.com/liseami/DeepRant/issues/66)。侧栏底部新增外观切换：系统 / 浅色 / 深色。默认跟随系统。主窗口、游戏输入浮窗与原生标题栏同时切换，选择保存在本机设置中。 - 处理 [#67：局域网 http 模型地址](https://github.com/liseami/DeepRant/issues/67)。AI 模型页的 Base URL 现在接受本机与私有网络的 http 地址，例如 `http://192.168.1.20:3000`，也接受 10.x、172.16–31.x、Tailscale 100.64–127.x、IPv6 ULA 与 `*.local`。局域网服务的 Key 按需填写。 - 公网服务仍然只接受 https；http 不加密，请只在可信网络中使用局域网中转。不跟随重定向，Key 依旧只保存在系统凭据库。 - 三平台安装包与签名更新归档重新生成，沿用 CCBaga 的发布验收链；macOS 使用同组织 Developer ID 签名、公证与装订，全部平台校验后公开。  原有设置、个人 Key、自定义提示词、快捷键与原版游戏录屏保留。首次使用请填写自己的模型 Key；macOS 需允许辅助功能输入。深色视觉与局域网服务现场由玩家验收。 
```

### 2026-10-19T23:40:07Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Fixes  - Update single-file-core to 1.6.40: a stylesheet modified by the page's scripts is read back through its CSSOM serialization, and Firefox serializes a media or container feature value above the largest float as `(max-width: infinity * 1px)`, which no browser parses back, so every rule under such a query was dead in the saved page. styled-components writes its open-ended last breakpoint that way, and on liberation.fr the saved page lost the column gaps of the main grid at wide viewports, so the columns and their photos widened. The value is now written as the largest finite length, which every browser reads back. Firefox-only release: the Chromium and Edge builds are not affected, since Chrome serializes the value as a finite length already. 
```

legacy positional-only parameters in `__new__` methods (`PYI063`) ([#28502](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28502)) - \[`flake8-pyi`\] Fix false positives on quoted annotations (`PYI036`) ([#29274](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29274)) - \[`perflint`\] Avoid invalid comprehension suggestions (`PERF401`, `PERF403`) ([#29366](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29366))  ### Rule changes  - Treat more `collections` and `weakref` containers as mutable ([#29213](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29213))  ### Documentation  - \[`flake8-import-conventions`\] Update default and example `aliases` ([#29333](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29333))  ### Contributors  - [@fixfon](https://github.com/fixfon) - [@mmaxjr](https://github.com/mmaxjr) - ### 2026-10-15T13:46:58Z [**usememos/telegram-integration**](https://github.com/usememos/telegram-integration)

```
Memogram now uses calendar versioning, starting with **26.10.0**. This release restores compatibility with current Memos APIs and improves attachment feedback and Docker deployment.  ## Highlights  - Support the latest Memos development build and the 0.30 and 0.31 stable series through the HTTP REST API. - Report attachment upload failures accurately, with a link to the saved memo instead of a false success message. - Run Docker deployments as a non-root user and retain Telegram bindings across container recreation with a persistent data volume. - Add `--version` and include the exact release version and commit in build metadata and startup logs. - Expand compatibility tests for ordinary users, text saving and search, images and other attachments, albums, visibility and pin buttons, user isolation, and invalid or revoked tokens.  ## Upgrading  - Set `SERVER_ADDR` to your Memos HTTP or HTTPS base URL, for example `https://memos.example.com`. Use a Memos Personal Access Token when binding with `/start <token>`. - Back up your existing `DATA` file before replacing a deployment. When moving to the bundled Docker Compose setup, preserve or migrate the binding file to `/data/data.txt` and make it accessible to UID/GID `65532:65532`. - Build Docker and Compose deployments with `sh scripts/build.sh docker -t memogram` or `sh scripts/build.sh compose up -d --build`. The build script supplies the required version and commit metadata. This release provides downloadable binaries; it does not publish a Docker image. - New release tags have no `v` prefix. Future releases use `YY.MM.N`, such as `26.10.1`.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/usememos/telegram-integration/compare/v0.5.0...26.10.0 
```
