### 2026-10-19T07:59:40Z [**ryan4yin/nix-config**](https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config)

```
> **A two-GPU inference box, speech recognition on the NPU, and a proxy config that lives in this repository.** 114 commits across 51 PRs, 249 files, +10.4k/−1.6k lines in 12 days — mostly authored by agents: **DeepSeek Harness (deepseek-flash)**, with a few host fixes from GPT-6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra. These notes were drafted by the same agent run.  ## Highlights  - **Two-GPU inference host returns**: `aquamarine` is back as the inference box on the Colorful CVN Z890 ARK FROZEN V20 board it inherited from `ai`, with two R9700s capped at 250 W each, while `ai` returns to its MSI MAG B760M MORTAR with the RTX 4090 (#439, #453, #467). - **Speech recognition on the NPU**: an OpenAI-compatible `/v1/audio/transcriptions` service runs whisper-large-v3-turbo int8 on OpenVINO's VPU, after Qwen3-ASR and SenseVoice failed to compile (#472). - **Generated mihomo config**: the proxy config is generated from this repository instead of a copy in the live directory (#420, #438), and the IPv6 fake-IP design is reworked across gateway, desktops, and VM guests (#419, #420, #442, #443). - **Native CPU scaling**: TuneD is dropped; hosts scale their CPUs natively, with the power profile pinned per host (#458, #460, #461). - **Desktop hardware control**: OpenRGB for `ai`'s board ARGB and DDC/CI monitor control (#452), SuperIO fan and temperature sensors on both boards (#456), a 90% charge cap on `shoukei` (#457), and EasyEffects state that survives the tmpfs root. - **Agent tooling**: `dsh-nu` moves to its own flake (#475, #476), and dsh becomes Nushell-only with steerable turns and programmatic tool calling (#473).  ## Features  ### Hosts, inference & hardware  - Revive `aquamarine` on the Colorful CVN Z890 ARK FROZEN V20 board with both R9700s, and return `ai` to its MSI MAG B760M MORTAR with the RTX 4090 (#439). - Build `ai` and `shoukei` on `aquamarine` through remote builders, authenticating with passphrase-free host keys (#467). - Serve Whisper transcription from `aquamarine`'s NPU as an OpenAI-compatible endpoint for voice input (#472). - Make `aquamarine`'s iGPU usable for VA-API/QSV transcoding and `openvino-gpu`, keeping both R9700s for inference (#474). - Cap each R9700 at 250 W (#453), expose the boards' fans and temperatures through SuperIO (nct6683 on `ai`, nct6775 on `aquamarine`) (#456), add OpenRGB control for `ai`'s board ARGB and control its external monitors over DDC/CI (#452). - Cap `shoukei` battery charging at 90% (#457).  ### Network & proxy  - Generate the mihomo config from this repository, drop the redacted gateway template, and sniff on the gateway instead of the desktop (#420, #438). - Rework IPv6 fake-IP handling: answer AAAA on a public fake-ip pool, keep the fake pool out of `fc00::/7`, force the TUN v6 up so a cold boot keeps IPv6, and keep the tailnet and unproxiable ranges off the TUN (#419, #420, #442, #443). - Keep Steam downloads off the proxy so Steam keeps a CN CDN, and restore the Steam community and store PROXY rules (#408, #412). - Sync the mihomo policy to Clash Verge on macOS (#424). - Pin the MicroVM guests to a cluster ULA IPv6 identity (#420).  ### Power  - Replace TuneD with native CPU scaling, after pinning the profile per host and capping the VM hosts (#458), and mask the packaged `tuned-ppd` unit when `ppdSupport` is off (#460, #461).  ### Desktop & terminal  - Keep EasyEffects state across reboots and declare its excluded apps in the output preset, leaving local media and meeting apps unprocessed. - Enlarge yazi image previews and add four plugins (#469). - Keep tuios sessions across reboots, create them with a cwd before attaching, and handle a stopped daemon at shell startup (#416, #418); upgrade tuios to v0.10.1. - Add `nh` for NixOS and nix-darwin operations, and start terminals fullscreen on macOS. - Keep the catppuccin starship port build across `nix-collect-garbage` (#468), and stop Noctalia from double-counting mihomo's network speed (#414). - Keep named podman images when pruning (#435).  ### Gaming & streaming  - Add a generic umu game installer with a skill and desktop entries (#411). - Add WiVRn CLI streaming for Quest 3 (#392).  ### Agents & skills  - Build `dsh-nu` from its own flake instead of NUR (#475, #476), and bump it to 0.5.0. - Make Nushell the only shell for dsh, with steerable turns and programmatic tool calling (#473). - Extract a global `git-delivery` skill and cap delivery text (#426, #429), pin the global skills, and trim the always-loaded context (#427). - Add `review-comments-and-docs` and two evaluation suites, then close the rule gaps they found (#434). - Rewrite `find-docs` onto the nixpkgs ctx7 CLI (#429), and link the global rules into dsh too.  ### Secrets  - Move `nushell-secrets.nu` out of agenix (#423). - Manage the vinput config here and read its provider keys from an EnvironmentFile (#445).  ### Docs  - Add the NixOS modernization options and decision record (#409), and record the `idols-ai` mixed-memory freeze and voltage tests (#433).  ## Fixes  ### Monitoring  - Use per-component temperature thresholds, alert on the GPU at 90 °C and board sensors at 70 °C (#471), repair the alert source link and template fallback, correct the PostgreSQL configuration-change query, and ignore floating nct6775 inputs (#470). - Restore `youko` metrics, alerts, and VM-host deploys (#431).  ### Networking & DNS  - Route `shoukei` DNS through the mihomo TUN and pick up the Asahi U-Boot DTC fix (#440). - Disable gateway IPv6 DNS answers (#443) and use fake-IP for gateway IPv6 (#442). - Keep connections to idle LAN peers fast (#463), and prevent a GitHub IPv6 regression. - Name the Singapore nodes instead of pattern-matching their name (#420).  ### Deployment & hosts  - Build Colmena deploys on the target host (#439), and restart the MicroVM guest unit on the physical host to deploy it (#425). - Drop Home Manager from the k3s test workers (#432). - Match `ai`'s wired NIC by prefix, not by name (#439). - Run the macOS rollback as root (#425). - Shrink the `ai` and `aquamarine` swapfiles to 32G (#465).  ### Desktop  - Reclaim the page cache before the desktop's anonymous pages, and bound niri's NVIDIA free-buffer pool (#477). - Persist the user's cache and unit directories on `aquamarine` (#466, #464), and enable user lingering there (#444). - Keep wluma's `als` key (#425), and drop the legacy ctx7 preservation entry.  ### Docs & agents  - Review and correct comments and documentation (#436), and fix stale guidance in the skills (#425) plus skill frontmatter and commit message caps.  ## Other Changes  - **Refactor**: drop conda from the Linux and macOS profiles (#465), drop the pinned SSH keys from the LAN block (#446), and configure dsh from its profile patch (#415). - **Chore**: update the vinput key words, and pin tuios to v0.9.0 before the v0.10.1 upgrade.  ## Deployment notes  - Switch `ai` to this release before pulling the Z890 board and its second NVMe, or its initrd waits for the missing LUKS volume. - The 32G swapfiles replace disko-created files, so shrink the live ones with `btrfs filesystem mkswapfile`; a rebuild alone leaves them at 96G. - MicroVM guests deploy by restarting their unit on the physical host (WA-026).  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/compare/v0.18.0...v0.19.0 
```

### 2026-10-18T13:28:32Z [**dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email**](https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email)

```
## What's Changed  > 从 v1.12.0 升级无需新增数据库迁移。  ### Features  - feat: |Webhook| 新增邮件过滤规则，统一匹配条件/与/或/非四种节点，支持嵌套、字符串和原生正则匹配、可视化/JSON 编辑及规则检查；移除附件时保留全部原 From 头，缺失或无效时仍移除附件（issue #1155） - feat: |用户系统| 邮箱地址管理支持按地址关键字搜索已绑定邮箱，并保持分页结果准确（issue #1156） - feat: |AI 识别| 新增 `AI_EXTRACT_MODE`，可显式选择仅用本地规则（`local`）或优先用 Workers AI（`ai`）识别邮件；不填默认使用本地规则，邮件内容不会发送给 AI。**升级注意**：原先依赖 Workers AI 绑定自动启用 AI 识别的部署需设置 `AI_EXTRACT_MODE = "ai"`  ### Bug Fixes  - fix: |收件| 黑名单同时检查 SMTP 信封发件人与邮件头 From 的全部邮箱地址，任意一个命中即拒收；独立解析地址，避免正文解析失败或多个发件地址导致漏拦截；地址解析异常时保留原有信封检查 - fix: |AI 识别| `ai` 模式下地址未命中 AI 提取白名单时只跳过 Workers AI 调用，仍回退到本地规则提取验证码 - fix: |Telegram| 当邮件没有纯文本正文（如纯 HTML 邮件）时回退到 HTML 转纯文本，避免 Telegram 通知显示“解析失败”  ### Improvements  - feat: |AI 识别| 本地验证码规则增强：同时识别邮件标题，支持验证码在关键词前（如 `116352（动态验证码）`、`ABC123 is your code`）、`G-123456` 前缀、分组 / 空格 / 零宽字符 / 全角数字，新增俄西葡法德意土希伯来语等关键词；排除超过 8 位数字、小数金额、时间、URL 与邮箱地址中的数字、tracking / order / voucher code 及纯字母单词，收紧无关键词时的数字识别，并限制分析长度、消除正则回溯风险 - feat: |Frontend| 统一桌面和移动端邮件摘要，突出主题、保留完整发件人并支持正文预览；阅读区使用主题配色按钮和附件数量徽标，更多菜单支持键盘操作，详情弹窗集中展示邮件信息  <details> <summary>English</summary>  > No additional database migrations are required when upgrading from v1.12.0.  ### Features  - feat: |Webhook| Add email filters with condition/AND/OR/NOT nodes, nesting, string and native regex matching, visual/JSON editing and rule checking; preserve all original From headers while still removing attachments when From is missing or invalid (issue #1155) - feat: |User| Search bound mailboxes by email address in address management with accurate pagination (issue #1156) - feat: |AI Extract| Add `AI_EXTRACT_MODE` to explicitly choose local rules only (`local`) or prefer Workers AI (`ai`); defaults to local rules when unset so mail content is never sent to AI. **Upgrade note**: deployments that relied on the Workers AI binding to enable AI extraction automatically must set `AI_EXTRACT_MODE = "ai"`  ### Bug Fixes  - fix: |Mail| Check the SMTP envelope sender and every From email address against sender blacklists; parse addresses independently so body parsing failures or multiple From addresses cannot skip matching; preserve envelope checks on address parsing errors - fix: |AI Extract| In `ai` mode, an address allowlist miss now skips only the Workers AI call and still falls back to local verification-code extraction - fix: |Telegram| Fall back to HTML-to-text conversion when plain text body is empty, preventing "parse failed" messages in Telegram notifications  ### Improvements  - feat: |AI Extract| Improve local verification-code rules: also read the mail subject; support codes before keywords (e.g. `116352（动态验证码）`, `ABC123 is your code`), `G-123456` prefixes, grouped / spaced / zero-width-split / full-width codes, and Russian, Spanish, Portuguese, French, German, Italian, Turkish and Hebrew keywords; reject numbers longer than 8 digits, decimals and amounts, times, digits in URLs and email addresses, tracking / order / voucher codes and letters-only words; only accept keyword-less numbers in stricter positions; bound input length and remove regex backtracking risks - feat: |Frontend| Unify desktop and mobile mail summaries with prominent subjects, full sender identities and body previews; use theme-aware reader buttons and attachment-count badges, add keyboard-accessible actions and a mail-details dialog  </details>  ### [更新或者部署网页不生效请如图勾选清理缓存](https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/discussions/487)  <details> <summary>PRs</summary>  * feat: upgrade version to v1.13.0 by @dreamhunter2333 in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1148 * chore: upgrade dependencies by @dreamhunter2333 in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1149 * docs: clarify database migration notes in release skill by @dreamhunter2333 in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1150 * feat: 优化邮件列表与阅读体验 by @dreamhunter2333 in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1152 * feat: add AI_EXTRACT_MODE to choose local rules or Workers AI for email extraction by @solarhell in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1153 * fix: fallback to local extract on AI allowlist miss by @dreamhunter2333 in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1154 * feat: 用户邮箱地址管理支持搜索 by @dreamhunter2333 in https://github.com/dreamhunter2333/cloudflare_temp_email/pull/1157 * fix: check both ### 2026-10-17T15:42:41Z [**QuantumNous/new-api**](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api)
```

```
> [!IMPORTANT] > v1 正式版即将发布，现在是最后几个 RC 版本。新增 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash、Search 和 Fetch，通知分开开关，以及拖动排列渠道优先级的功能。 > > 敏感词过滤默认关着。以前填过敏感词、但没保存过这个开关的，升级后到「请求检查」里打开。 > > v1 is coming soon. These are the last few release candidates. Added DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, Search and Fetch, separate notification switches, and dragging to set channel priority. > > Sensitive word filtering is off by default. If you filled in a word list and never saved the switch, turn it on under Request checks after upgrading.  ## 中文  * **DeepSeek**：新增 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash（deepseek-flash）和 deepseek-v4-pro 的支持，后面可以加 -none、-low、-max，`@thinking` 和 `@effort` 也能用。新增 Search 和 Fetch，地址是 /v1/tools/search 和 /v1/tools/fetch。Claude Code 和 Codex 让 DeepSeek 去搜索、打开网页时，按次数计费，每 1000 次 0.75 美元。 * **通知**：新增按种类开关通知的功能，余额不足提醒默认开着。验证码、重置密码、绑定邮箱，看 SMTP 有没有配好。余额低于你设的数时提醒一次，充回去之后，下次再低才会再提醒。 * **渠道**：新增拖动调整渠道优先级的功能，在渠道列表打开「拖拽模式」。新增 New API 渠道查询上游余额的功能，也可以绑定海螺、Vidu 和 Veo。新增令牌「允许查询用户额度」，新建的默认开着，以前建的还是关的。 * **限流和计费**：修复分组限流算到一起的问题，某个分组自己设了限流，就按这个分组的次数算。修复 Vidu 收费和实际生成对不上的问题，按它实际生成的模型、时长和分辨率计费。修复 Claude 流式把上游报的 0 丢掉的问题，报了 0 就按 0 算。提示词全在缓存里时，按读缓存的价格计费。 * **报错**：修复请求内容读失败时看不出原因的问题。上游报错里，你填的地址、IP 和密钥会被藏起来，文档链接和邮箱还看得到。 * **其他**：新增 Sub2API 查询余额的功能。新增 AWS 的 Mantle（Messages API），已经在用的还是 InvokeModel。新增 Vertex 用 API Key 时填写项目 ID 的功能。任务插件这一版更快。 * **修复**：修复 Vertex 的 us、eu 访问地址错误的问题。修复容器里过载保护看错 CPU 和内存的问题，看的是容器自己的，连续大约 15 秒都过高才会拒绝请求。  ## English  * **DeepSeek**: Added deepseek-flash and deepseek-v4-pro. You can add -none, -low, or -max, and `@thinking` and `@effort` work too. Added Search and Fetch at /v1/tools/search and /v1/tools/fetch. When Claude Code or Codex has DeepSeek search or open a page, that is charged per request, $0.75 per 1,000. * **Notifications**: Added a switch for each kind of notification. The low balance reminder starts on. Verification codes, password resets, and email binding go out when SMTP is set up. You get one reminder when the balance goes below the number you set, and another the next time it does, after a top-up. * **Channels**: Added dragging to change channel priority. Turn on Drag mode in the channel list. Added upstream balance checks for New API channels, which can also bind Hailuo, Vidu, and Veo. Added Allow querying user quota on tokens. New tokens start with it on. Older tokens stay off. * **Rate limits and billing**: Fixed group rate limits being counted together. A group with its own limit is counted on its own. Fixed Vidu charges that did not match the video it generated. The charge uses the model, duration, and resolution it actually made. Fixed Claude streams dropping a 0 from the upstream. A reported 0 stays 0. When the whole prompt is cached, the charge is the cache-read price. * **Errors**: Fixed request failures that hid the real reason the body could not be read. If it is too large, the response is 413. In an upstream error, the address, IP, and key you entered are hidden. Documentation links and email addresses stay visible. * **Also**: Added balance checks for Sub2API channels. Added Mantle (Messages API) for AWS channels. Ones already in use stay on InvokeModel. Added a project ID field for Vertex API Key mode. Task plugins are faster in this release. * **Fixes**: Fixed wrong Vertex addresses for the us and eu regions. Fixed overload protection in containers reading the wrong CPU and memory. It uses the container's own, and turns requests away only after they stay too high for about 15 seconds.  ---  ## What's Changed * feat(ui): show newly added pricing groups at the top by @seefs001 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7782 * feat(channel): support Sub2API balance queries by @AdoreJc in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7749 * fix: disable sensitive word filtering by default and clear list by @seefs001 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7814 * fix(vertex): pass project_id in API Key mode to avoid 404 on official endpoints by @xiaoyuyu6420 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/6330 * fix(web): sort pricing models by input, output and per-request price by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7813 * fix(claude): preserve explicit zero usage and cache-only billing by @paopaoandlingyia in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7816 * fix(relay): mask only the custom upstream address in error messages by @RedwindA in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7822 * fix(playground): support the reasoning field in chat responses by @seefs001 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7831 * fix(dashboard): draw flow chart labels in the theme text color by @RedwindA in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7821 * fix(relaykit): emit summary on reasoning items announced without one by @rjchien728 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7833 * fix(middleware): 过载哨兵改用容器口径并加入连续性与新鲜度判定 by @ax2 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7317 * fix(relayconvert): 保留 Chat/Claude → Responses 转换中 input_image 的 detail 字段 by @sicnuyudidi in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7855 * fix(web): 补全日志页模型厂商规则（Cloudflare clef、NVIDIA 家族） by @xuu1998 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7840 * fix(web): apply theme before first paint to avoid light flash and Dark Reader misdetection by @RedwindA in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7571 * fix: align model card summary with status bar hours by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7847 * feat(notify): turn each kind of notification on or off by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7868 * feat(deepseek): support V4.1 Flash, the Search and Fetch APIs, and @ reasoning modifiers by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7867  ## New Contributors * @AdoreJc made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7749 * @xiaoyuyu6420 made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/6330 * @paopaoandlingyia made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7816 * @rjchien728 made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7833 * @sicnuyudidi made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7855 * @xuu1998 made their first contribution in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7840  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/compare/v1.0.0-rc.44...v1.0.0-rc.45
```

### 2026-10-16T11:46:21Z [**open-webui/open-webui**](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui)

```
 ### Added  - 🔐 **End-to-end encrypted chats.** Direct messages and private group channels can now hold a temporary end-to-end encrypted chat: a member picks Encrypted Chat in the channel menu, the other members see "Encrypted chat in progress" with a Join link, messages of up to 16 KiB are encrypted in the browser with MLS and only passed through the server without being saved, participants can verify each other by comparing codes and see when others are typing, and the chat is cleared when you leave or reload and ends when channel membership or access changes. Administrators turn it on under Admin Settings > General > Encrypted channels or with "ENABLE_ENCRYPTED_CHANNELS" and grant it with the new Encrypted Channels permission, off by default or set with "USER_PERMISSIONS_FEATURES_ENCRYPTED_CHANNELS", and it needs websocket connections with websocket server logging off and HTTPS in production. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7dc91de86c41866f4170602f074c39dbf1609377), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0b3812f2857b4c65fd1ab931f40de4f1264edc46), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b1e876896caee5bf30a010fb414a9249ec7f59a2), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4dedaaf6e2292c2c4fb48668eee1292cfd7de2d0) - 📡 **Much lighter streaming with Redis.** With Redis, a streaming reply is now stored under its own key and new text is appended to it instead of rewriting the whole reply into a shared hash on every update, and replies started on older instances are still read during an upgrade; with room channels on, each instance now only subscribes to the rooms of people connected to it, so Redis no longer sends it live updates for chats it does not serve. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f5a26f53a089eeb9683ead687a1fd3e9125132a6) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/362b01d0b2ba677858a5af85caf3dd8544c5e6df) [#32478](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32478) - 🔍 **Compare view options and undo.** The Compare revision view has a Comparison options menu to ignore whitespace, ignore emphasis markers or only show changes with the unchanged lines folded away, an Undo button that steps back through your edits in both panes in order, and changed lines that are now lined up by their matching text. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/d0750f6617acd3b57ce58ba1d543c37e6c793c8d), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a087aa1deb4d999b122f882335fd08af8f06e72c), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/10ce0d23651fc2c96fd0d276147cd48105e21b84), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3288fe010a5e8f8a59e1e5d2262957d175e56bde) - 📝 **Richer Markdown file previews.** Markdown files opened in the terminal file browser now render with the same renderer as chat replies, including math, footnotes, highlighted code blocks, alerts and Mermaid diagrams, as well as HTML blocks mixed with Markdown, laid out as a compact document, and task list checkboxes in an editable file can be ticked to save the change to the file. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c9a52208da9e8deeac6f2df7fcacbe519c2acb29) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7c3980338b72c5faa7e7f3a088deb477644fef40) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/15ac5163ea00a934ec0c68d2bb7f6bc5c655223e) [#32514](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32514) - 👀 **Live collaborators in shared chats.** The top bar of a chat now shows the pictures of the other people who have the same chat open at that moment, up to three and a count for the rest, with a Collaborators list that opens their profile cards. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4dedaaf6e2292c2c4fb48668eee1292cfd7de2d0), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8191ac30dd79b4a39130fe83ffc16035776be484), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8e9519240fcdcd568a9b951ba06e523c58e68350) - ✏️ **Edit and regenerate in shared chats with replies.** In a chat from a shared folder that allows replies, people who may edit messages can now edit a prompt to start a new branch and regenerate any reply, not only replies to their own messages, and others viewing the chat stay on the branch they had selected. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2a6e0da057d3b6eb6ba506346c4db788cab735f0), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/788a61180be76aef27761b051300d6eefa715947) - 🗄️ **Folder access in the folder editor.** Sharing a folder now happens through an Access button in the Edit Folder dialog instead of a Share item in the folder menu, the sharing mode can be set before anyone is added, and subfolders note that access granted to parent folders also applies to them. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f4557ca06b8331c8914bac9e8f54ce63b0445c21), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4a70253bc8586ad1d2a97241665b2060324bab37) - 🔎 **Workspace search.** The search window now searches your chats, chats in folders shared with you, chats shared with you, channel and direct messages and notes in one place, with filters for content type, a folder or channel, and whether something was created by you or shared with you, highlighted matches, a preview of each result with Ctrl/Cmd + Enter, Copy link, and channel results that open the thread scrolled to the matching message. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b4db56ae96651d926adb4aefc6ec1275f35c6b6f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ef28e99e587a2845a2852bb6ab8c182dc1588980), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1a1a81d8e09330900ac74e4bc97a48e1e0d52a8a) - 💭 **Comments in document previews.** Word, Excel, PowerPoint and PDF files opened in the terminal file browser now show their comments and replies, marked where they belong in the document and opened by hovering or tapping a marker, and older .xls spreadsheets can now be previewed too. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ba0b2169e1799e1915c6dbb071c56d54896467fb) - 🧹 **Clear Input Selections shortcut.** Clearing the model override, selected tools and filters and the web search, image generation and code interpreter toggles from the chat input is now a shortcut you can change in Settings > Shortcuts, still Escape by default. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9cf48f4fb0a8aef30010294912a3a5674850beaf) - 🙋 **Ask about a file from the file browser.** Selecting text in a file open in the terminal file browser now shows an "Ask or edit…" box that sends your request to the model with the file attached and the selection quoted, so it can answer or edit that file in the same terminal; unsaved edits can be saved before sending, and requests sent while a reply is running wait in the queue. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/af34515131d9efd41cfeae5c1ea50a7f7ed84443) - ☁️ **Google Drive access limited to picked files.** The Google Drive picker now asks only for access to the files a user picks instead of read access to their whole Drive, so self-hosted installs no longer need Google's restricted-scope verification and users no longer see the "Google hasn't verified this app" warning. [#32531](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32531) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ed5c2d7522c126b6163aad56762790025b31ec0b) [#29537](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/29537) - 📜 **Full text of long status lines.** Status lines above a reply, such as a token usage summary sent by a function, can now be clicked to show their full text, and with several status lines the history shows every entry in full. [#32532](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32532) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/291b57bec24ec670050b996ede45ea6dd56c7f9f) [#30056](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/30056) - 📏 **Skill read limits.** Administrators can set how many characters of a skill the model reads at once and how long the preview of longer instructions is, under Admin Settings > General with "Skill Read Limit" and "Skill Preview Length" or the "SKILL_CONTENT_MAX_CHARS" and "SKILL_PREVIEW_MAX_CHARS" environment variables, which default to 20000 and 5000. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/92e64c1d59cdd99b5f33a108186a1d368daab964) - 🔄 **General improvements.** Various improvements were implemented across the application to enhance performance, stability, and security. - 🌐 **Translation updates.** Translations for Portuguese (Brazil), Chinese (Traditional), Arabic, Bengali, Hebrew, Georgian, Punjabi, Slovak, Serbian, Urdu, Uzbek and Vietnamese were enhanced and expanded. [#32440](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32440), [#32482](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32482), [#32501](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32501)  ### Fixed  - 🧾 **API key header requests in the audit log.** Requests authenticated with an API key in the x-api-key header, as Anthropic SDKs send it, or in a header set with "CUSTOM_API_KEY_HEADER" now appear in the audit log under the key's owner, and failed attempts with a wrong key are logged without a user, where these requests were missing from the audit log. [#32443](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32443), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f7c8ad012ddcf458c39cdf8a96b41c410655196c) - 📚 **Knowledge sharing kept on rename.** A collaborator with write access who edits a shared knowledge base's name or description no longer turns off sharing they could not set themselves, such as Public. [#32450](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32450), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/08f6023427cdad6179b8fc5f656c99f3f0ad44f8), [#32449](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32449) - 🤝 **Prompt and tool sharing kept on save.** A collaborator with write access who saves a shared prompt or tool no longer makes it private for everyone; sharing they could not set themselves is now kept. [#32493](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32493), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5dff6489d2f6a6c33c49ca035e5384ff5dd29006), [#32492](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32492) - 📄 **PDF export of long notes and chats.** Long notes and chats now download as PDF, where Firefox failed with "Canvas exceeds max size" and Chrome saved a black PDF. [#32459](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32459), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f78e46ef44ecc1a7ea8a4951edc17e22bf1d5cad), [#18334](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/18334), [#26373](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/26373), [#19583](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/19583) - ⏩ **Continue Response with several models.** Continue Response now continues the reply it was clicked under, where in a chat with several models it could extend another model's reply. [#32456](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32456), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3c82fda4e813e00d33ca16a8ddd61596296a2066), [#32455](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32455) - 🏷️ **Tags of imported chats.** Imported chats now show their tags and the tags appear in tag suggestions, where tags missing from the account were not created on import. [#32454](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32454), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fd47370e851c3fcb55ba69aec39488aaec7f4b02), [#32453](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32453) - 🔖 **Tags of archived chats.** Searching the sidebar for a tag whose chats are all archived no longer deletes the tag, which lost its original name when the chats were unarchived. [#32452](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32452), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/58b821a4642480b2202320b45146bace72962f3b), [#32451](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32451) - ⌨️ **Input methods in channels.** Pressing Enter to confirm a word while typing with an input method, such as for Japanese, Chinese or Korean, no longer sends and clears a channel message before the word is in. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9d0aa791ad70ef2a7771c74955e2c9304021609e) - 🖱️ **Scroll error after leaving a chat.** Scrolling a chat right before opening Notes no longer logs a TypeError in the browser console. [#32458](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32458), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9245e31ccba8e464054297b1f3660ab3e7f77969), [#32457](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32457) - 📐 **Steady first turn in new chats.** The latest message and reply in a new chat now keep their real height when the chat gets saved and the message list reloads, so the view no longer jumps on the first turn. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/bbccff89c1cf318c21cd9eeb4228f98882a33736) - 💬 **Sending in channels.** Your own channel message now stays in place when the server confirms it instead of being redrawn, and the channel scrolls to your new message as you send it, where it could jump or keep the old scroll position. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/99457b373e6b81fe50df0d18a75d126706237841) - 📂 **Terminal folder in new chats.** In a new chat, files you attach are uploaded to the folder open in the terminal file browser and the file browser stays in that folder once the chat is created, where uploads could go to the default folder and the browser jumped back to it. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7ca62fdf93f6177e929cca20c712f3d958e1d935) - 📁 **Shared folder settings and access.** Editing a shared folder's models, system prompt or files no longer resets its sharing mode, and a subfolder in a folder shared with write access stays writable when it is also shared directly with read access. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f4557ca06b8331c8914bac9e8f54ce63b0445c21), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4a70253bc8586ad1d2a97241665b2060324bab37) - 🔑 **OAuth token refresh with client_secret_basic.** When "OAUTH_TOKEN_ENDPOINT_AUTH_METHOD" is client_secret_basic, token refresh now sends the client credentials in the Basic header like sign-in does, where providers that accept only Basic rejected the refresh and Open WebUI deleted the user's OAuth tokens, breaking model connections and tool servers that use them until the next sign-in. [#32507](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32507), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ae6793dd4f209455387bcc418a5c775eec0b16c3), [#32506](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32506) - 🛠️ **OAuth tools after sign-in.** An OAuth MCP tool now stays turned on after its sign-in redirect when the model has default tools, and links with ?tools= turn the listed tools on. [#32488](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32488), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/81722918384cb56608a2ab26ca03059b3a811758), [#32476](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32476) - 🔤 **Admin default font kept.** Saving Settings > Interface no longer stores the administrator's default font and image compression size as your own settings, so later changes to those defaults still reach you. [#32497](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32497), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/e979a1f9e2c86d60c7ee3ba48fb0dc42a3b65aa0), [#32496](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32496) - 🔊 **Read aloud with slow speech.** Read aloud no longer stops after the first sentence when the speech engine takes longer to produce the next one than the current one takes to play. [#32484](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32484), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7da8653e71cda807609d9ec64912cd319c0d6ccf), [#32446](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32446) - 🖼️ **Inline HTML previews on Per chat terminals.** Inline HTML previews in replies now find the file in the chat's own terminal workspace when the Open Terminal chat context is Per chat, where they showed "File not found". [#32485](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32485), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ec39df7da56f6947fde17a9be2c146251f891b26), [#32477](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32477) - 🧵 **Replies in channel threads.** A reply started in one channel thread no longer carries over to the next thread you open, where messages could quote a message from the other thread. [#32490](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32490), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0696bfdf276fe6331c6dfaf50c53676d08b1574a), [#32489](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32489) - 📑 **Scanned PDF previews.** Previewing a scanned PDF without a text layer in the knowledge base viewer no longer scrolls the page up and pushes the header and toolbar out of reach. [#32487](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32487), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1bc5648a7b67c82405f86ed4bd0506f9ae3eb739), [#32481](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32481) - ↩️ **Right-to-left lists.** Bullets, numbers and task checkboxes of Markdown lists now sit on the correct side for right-to-left text in replies and file previews. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6768f52aca633945292da08ff9450fd4dc62705f), [#32444](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32444) - 🔁 **Regenerate in cloned shared chats.** Regenerate now works in a chat you cloned or forked from someone else's share, where it failed with "You can only regenerate your own messages". [#32508](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32508), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ad3e5a6c425f7b4af8a66f2e8dc42a7625526f95), [#32505](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32505) - 🗑️ **Delete hidden for repliers in shared chats.** People replying in a shared chat no longer see a Delete button the server refuses, which made a deleted reply come back on reload; Delete shows to the chat's owner and to administrators when "ENABLE_ADMIN_CHAT_ACCESS" is on. [#32480](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32480), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/493b10630b0c19ee5a60f9de0fc5d997bb82bbb1), [#32479](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32479) - 🎛️ **Model default features can be turned off.** A model can now switch off a Default Feature, such as Code Interpreter, that the administrator turned on in Model Defaults, where it came back on when reopened and in new chats. [#32500](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32500), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a8dd8dbf987a3c7f4d6aa7c102e4e9cb158c4f5a), [#32498](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32498) - 🧰 **Built-in tools turned back on stay on.** A built-in tool such as Memory that is off in Model Defaults now stays on after a model turns it back on and is saved. [#32504](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32504), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9cc648cad8589686e617e4a64bea10134ff16288), [#32503](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32503) - 🏠 **Refresh with Landing Page Mode set to Chat.** Refreshing a new chat when Landing Page Mode is Chat no longer hides the message field and opens the model menu in the top left corner. [#32510](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32510), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5d4f16bcc9af1afac55dc0ffb4dced96041531a5), [#32509](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32509) - 🗜️ **Compaction during tool runs keeps sources out.** Context compaction during a long series of tool calls now summarizes your own message without the knowledge and file content added to it, and that content is given back to the model afterwards. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/597b00a5479ac0466b2f4d23a5e902ee0950091d), [#32491](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32491) - 🧩 **Built-in tools with a custom tools parameter.** A model with a custom parameter named tools, such as one that turns on a provider's native web search, now keeps its built-in tools, attached tools and file context, and its custom tools are sent alongside them. [#32530](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32530) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3ccea416b4e69ef688acdd879c8cb7cd31d5ab84) [#32528](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32528) - 🔌 **Tool servers back after an outage.** With Redis, tools from OpenAPI tool servers now come back once the servers are reachable again, where an empty list cached while every server was down kept them missing until a restart or a save in the tool server settings. [#32533](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32533) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/51a4d582706311502ae53a6c11876b87a49f5400) [#29573](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/29573) - 📥 **PDF links read as documents.** PDFs from sites that block unknown clients, such as Wikimedia, and arXiv links like arxiv.org/pdf/1706.03762 now go to document extraction, where the model got the raw PDF bytes. [#32523](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32523) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a5942da0a264b4e97a5d6a18adea7d4fcbfc275a) [#32524](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32524) [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/823670d9fa925255d3ebf28e55779b8be45a1c3a)  ### Changed  - 🎯 **Model skills now load their full instructions.** With the Skills builtin tool on, skills attached to a model in the model editor, which every new chat pre-selects, and skills picked from the chat input menu now send their full instructions with each message, where they were only listed by name and description for the model to load with view_skill; deployments that attached skills to models only to make them available should remove them from the model to avoid the extra prompt length, since every skill the user can read is listed for the model anyway. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5cf7c03332ad3fd68ddba276b94a87f8cea25c7a) - 📮 **Redis permissions for room channels.** With "WEBSOCKET_REDIS_ROOM_CHANNELS" on, the Redis user now needs the PUBLISH, SUBSCRIBE, UNSUBSCRIBE and PING permissions and access to the "&socketio" and "&socketio#\*" channels, while PSUBSCRIBE is no longer used; a missing permission is logged with how to fix it. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/362b01d0b2ba677858a5af85caf3dd8544c5e6df) 
```

### 2026-10-19T19:37:22Z [**mortbopet/Ripes**](https://github.com/mortbopet/Ripes)

```
### New features - **Qt 6**: Ripes has been migrated to Qt 6, and the macOS release is now a universal (Apple Silicon + Intel) build. - **Ripes in the browser**: a WebAssembly build of Ripes is available at [ripes.dk](https://ripes.dk). - **New processor models**:   - A 5-stage multicycle processor (with one or two memories), based on the work of Francisco Valero Gil.   - An ISA simulator "processor" model (`RV32_ISS`/`RV64_ISS`) for fast, non-pipelined execution. - **Theming**: Light/Dark/System color schemes with live theme switching. - **New memory-mapped I/O peripherals**: a raster screen, a real-time clock, and an edge-capture/latching D-pad. - **Command line mode**:   - ELF and C files can now be executed from the CLI.   - System input (stdin) handling for CLI execution.   - Cache simulation is now available in the CLI (`--cache-preset`). - **Assembler**:   - GNU-style `offset(base)` memory operands (e.g. `lw t0, 4(sp)`, `lw t0, (sp)`, `jalr ra, 4(t0)`, `c.lw a0, 4(a1)`, `c.lwsp a0, 4(sp)`).   - Labels may now start with a dot. - **Caches**: non-power-of-two associativities are now supported, and cache configuration now uses the term "sets" instead of "lines". - **UI**:   - Added a memory viewer widget to the edit and processor tabs; register values can be copied from the register widget and followed into memory (right click).   - Panning in the cache graphic view, a modernized sidebar, and persisted window geometry.   - Added a setting for enabling VSRTL VCD tracing.  ### Bug fixes - **Processor models**:   - `jalr` did not clear the least significant bit of the target address (#477).   - Register shift instructions (`sll`/`srl`/`sra`) used all bits of `rs2` instead of only the lower 5 (RV32) or 6 (RV64) bits (#398).   - Incorrect `mulh`/`mulhu`/`mulhsu` results on 64-bit processors.   - Compressed `c.j`/`c.jal` instructions jumping to the wrong address for offsets below -1024 (#472).   - Incorrect EX stage stall in the 5-stage pipeline (#444).   - Fixed RVC prime register encoding/decoding. - **Assembler**:   - `c.j`/`c.jal`/`c.beqz`/`c.bnez` encoded label targets as absolute rather than PC-relative addresses (#411).   - `sw rs2, SYMBOL(rs1)` with a symbolic offset was assembled as the `sw rs2, label, rt` pseudo-instruction (#467).   - Superfluous instruction operands are now rejected rather than silently ignored.   - Disabled ISA extensions of a processor were still accepted by the assembler.   - Hex/binary representations of edge-case signed immediates (e.g. `0xFFFFFFFF`) are now accepted.   - Division by zero in constant expressions crashed the assembler (#347).   - Parentheses were matched before comments were removed (#421). - **Caches**: fixed the random replacement policy, ensured a minimum of one way, and fixed numeric limits for way counts and presets. - **System calls**: fixed the `read` syscall character count being halved, the `O_CREAT` flag in `open`, read-only file operations, and added a stack-heap collision check to `brk`. - **UI and misc**:   - Auto-clocking was not stopped when a breakpoint was hit.   - A breakpoint at line 0 was also mirrored at line `num instructions`.   - I/O devices are now updated upon processor reset.   - Fixed a crash when hovering over the cache statistics chart.   - Fixed memory leaks in I/O simulation.   - Console input and clipboard usage in the WebAssembly build (#304).   - Source code highlighting with programs loaded at a non-zero start address. 
```

### 2026-10-20T07:12:17Z [**lollipopkit/flutter_server_box**](https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box)

```
### Added  - **Icon packs:** icons chosen apart from the theme, and themable icons in the Files window, looked up by file extension. - **Markdown preview:** Markdown files open rendered in the file editor, with an Edit button for the source. - **Reconnect button in the terminal,** for a connection that looks alive but no longer answers. - **System groups** beside system users, in the app and the Monitor panel. - **Screen readers:** the terminal can be read with a screen reader, the benchmark log is read as text, icon-only buttons have names, and the Monitor panel can be used with a keyboard and a screen reader. The panel's terminal reader mode is opt-in. - The app and the Monitor agent check that they speak the same protocol version, and say which one needs updating when they do not.  ### Changed  - Saved private keys are hidden by default in the editor and can be shown after app-lock authentication. - Agent: thinking levels follow the selected model, and favorites and the composer show which provider a model is from.  ### Fixed  - Remote desktop: KDE Plasma's built-in RDP server (krdp) no longer disconnects right after login, and xrdp logs on with the saved password instead of showing its own login screen. - Server details: cards with more than six rows (sensors, GPUs, batteries, BMC, custom commands, disk health) can show every row. - When the status script cannot be placed in `/tmp`, the fallback directory in the user's home now works. Memory that could not be read shows the reason instead of "100% of 1024 B". - Monitor: disks are read on machines whose language is not English. - A launch that fails shows why instead of opening no window, and an unreadable preferences file no longer stops the app from starting. On Windows and Linux, preferences are written atomically, so an interrupted write no longer damages them.  中文更新日志：https://serverbox.lollipopkit.com/docs/zh/changelog/  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box/compare/v1.0.1787...v1.0.1816 
```

```
## 云空调 CuteAC 0.4.3  修复多屏菜单栏弹窗首次打开时的位置偏移、跳动和裁切，优化小数温度显示。  - 从副屏菜单栏打开时，显示前就确定目标屏幕、锚点和内容尺寸，避免先出现在错误位置再跳回。 - 修复挂机、柜机切换及内容高度变化时的裁切；内容超过屏幕可用高度时可滚动查看，兼容系统滚动条设置。 - 温度如 `25.5°C` 的小数和小数点使用较小字号，单位保持在数字右上角；调整电源按钮与温度区域的对齐。 - 沿用同一 Developer ID 签名，保留现有 Home Assistant 绑定、多台空调切换和 Home ↔ CuteAC 状态同步。  下载 `CuteAC-0.4.3.zip`，解压后替换“应用程序”中的 `CuteAC.app`。需要 macOS 14 或更高版本；建议搭配 [Home 0.2.2](https://github.com/YunLeFun/home/releases/tag/macos-v0.2.2)。已使用 Developer ID 签名，尚未进行 Apple 公证。  沿用仓库统一版本规则，core/Vue npm 包和 Home 插件同步发布 0.4.3。本版还包含此前已合入 main 的网页改进：切换 2D/3D、挂机/柜机时保持页面位置，以及 3D 挂机右侧的能效标识。
```

### 2026-10-17T01:58:23Z [**tw93/Pake**](https://github.com/tw93/Pake)

```
<div align="center"> <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Pake/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.17.6</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog  1. With a hidden title bar on macOS, the logo and sidebar buttons of ChatGPT, Grok, Gemini, and DeepSeek now sit clear of the traffic lights, with the sidebar open or collapsed. 2. Built-in style tweaks now load only on the site they were written for, so an app you package no longer inherits rules meant for other sites, such as a hidden footer or flattened lists. 3. On macOS, pressing Enter to confirm a Chinese or Japanese input method candidate no longer sends a half-typed message in chat boxes like X and Grok. 4. Notifications on Windows now play the system sound, and stay quiet only when the page asks for a silent notification. 5. Linux deb and rpm packages now install one launcher instead of two with the same name. 6. The YouTube Music app has a new macOS-shaped icon. 7. The FAQ now notes that passkeys and Touch ID sign-in are not available in macOS apps, so use a password and a two-factor code.  ### 更新日志  1. macOS 隐藏标题栏时，ChatGPT、Grok、Gemini 和 DeepSeek 的标志和侧边栏按钮不再被左上角的红绿灯挡住，侧边栏展开和收起都一样。 2. 内置的网站样式微调只在对应的网站上生效，自己打包的应用不会再沾上为别的网站写的规则，比如页脚被隐藏或列表被压平。 3. macOS 上用中文或日文输入法按回车选词时，X、Grok 这类聊天框不会再把打了一半的内容发出去。 4. Windows 上的通知会播放系统提示音，只有网页要求静音时才不响。 5. Linux 的 deb 和 rpm 安装包只会装一个启动项，不再出现两个同名图标。 6. YouTube Music 应用换成了 macOS 样式的新图标。 7. FAQ 补充说明 macOS 应用用不了通行密钥和指纹登录，登录时改用密码加两步验证码。  Special thanks to @iamxtso for the report and to @houzixiashanxiedaima, @reg-chen, and @Totara-thib for the pull requests behind this release. ### 2026-10-15T22:08:07Z [**m1k1o/neko**](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko)
```

```
### New Features - **Replaced the Vue 2 web client with a React 19 and TypeScript client built with Vite**. It keeps the familiar layout and workflows: login and invite links, OAuth, video controls, fullscreen and Picture-in-Picture, clipboard, control handoff, microphone, emotes, chat, file transfer, settings, broadcast, member actions, and all 15 translations and URL parameters ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - Added a framework-free TypeScript client core, `client/core` (`@m1k1o/neko`), with authentication, room state and events, a pluggable stream transport, WebRTC, typed input channels, and the keyboard/mouse/touch overlay. It can be used independently of the React GUI or embedded in another framework ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - The client detects silent websocket connections, reconnects with backoff, and requests a replacement media peer when WebRTC fails. The stream can continue through a signalling outage, and playback resumes after reconnection ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - Added binary WebRTC data-channel input with websocket control-event fallback for transports without their own input channel. The client also handles IME and on-screen keyboard text as key events; Ctrl+V uses whichever clipboard was changed most recently ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - Chat now renders CommonMark and GitHub Flavored Markdown, including tables, lists, and nested quotes. Links are limited to HTTP, HTTPS, and mailto; HTML and images are not rendered. The new renderer replaces the hand-written parser and keeps hostile or oversized messages from stalling the chat ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)).  ### Fixes - Bound each OAuth authorization state to a short-lived, HttpOnly browser cookie, preventing a callback URL from creating a session in a browser that did not start the login. Concurrent login flows and explicit reverse-proxy redirects are supported ([#708](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/708)). - Fixed session and member IDs containing URL path characters. The `multiuser` and `noauth` providers now use sanitized IDs while preserving the display name; unsafe usernames submitted through `POST /api/members` are rejected ([#712](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/712)). - Fixed a server panic when video pipelines were configured without explicit video IDs, and when no video stream was available ([#711](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/711)). - Fixed slow WebRTC video timestamps under load, which could make browser buffers grow and audio/video drift. Generated pipelines also keep only the newest raw frame when an encoder falls behind, reducing input latency instead of accumulating stale frames ([#712](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/712)). - Fixed the client leaving a user at the disconnected screen when the server rejects a banned session, and hiding the microphone control when the browser does not support media capture ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - Internal websocket connections without an `Origin` header are now accepted, allowing trusted server-side websocket dials ([#710](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/710)).  ### Changes - The React client uses the v3 API only and works with `NEKO_LEGACY=false`. It no longer supports legacy v2-only behavior, including IP bans; kick removes the session, and the user may log in again ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - Added `chat.max_length` (default 512 characters; `0` disables the limit). Longer messages receive HTTP 400 through REST and are dropped over the websocket. Incoming websocket frames are limited to 1 MiB ([#712](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/712)). - Added the `neko_capture_streamsink_frames` metric to show when a video pipeline produces fewer frames than its configured frame rate ([#712](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/712)). - The default video pipeline no longer draws the mouse pointer into the stream; the client draws it without the stream delay. Set `capture.video.show_pointer` to `true` to restore the old behavior ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - Scroll sensitivity uses the v3 scale (-5 to 5), with the `?scroll` URL parameter mapped to it. File downloads are streamed instead of buffered in memory. The microphone turns off when the connection is re-established, and opening a second tab takes over the session ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). - The client uses the system font stack instead of Whitney and renders emoji as native characters rather than a sprite sheet ([#713](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/713)). 
```

```
### Features  - Conditionally include default IPv6 addr ([#721](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/721)) - Webui better uploaders ([#749](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/749)) - Support HEAD requests for embedded assets and health check ([#750](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/750)) - Render placeholders in custom 404 page ([#752](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/752)) - Preserve directory entries and metadata when zipping a directory ([#760](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/760))  ### Bug Fixes  - Liblzma dynamic linking issues on MacOS ([#712](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/712)) - Guard_root_contained walks up to existing ancestor for non-existent paths ([#716](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/716)) - Return response after forbid status ([#722](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/722)) - Require path separator boundary when stripping --path-prefix ([#725](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/725)) - Clamp range last-pos to EOF per RFC 9110 ([#738](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/738)) - Keep directories grouped on top when sorting in descending order ([#732](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/732)) - Enhance security ([#753](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/753)) - Close path ACL bypass via filesystem name aliases ([#754](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/754)) - Serve HTML/SVG/XML files inline again ([#759](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/759)) - Bind unix socket address only once ([#761](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/761)) - Don't set SO_REUSEADDR on Windows ([#762](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/762)) - Eliminate panics that could abort the server ([#764](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/764))  ### Refactor  - Handle unspecified ip addrs in check_addrs ([#708](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/708)) - Webui add css color variables ([#710](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/710)) - Replace async-zip with async-deflate-zip ([#720](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/720)) - Upgrade async-deflate-zip to v0.2.0 ([#723](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/723)) - Improve server port selection ([#748](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/748)) - Send title-case HTTP/1 response header names ([#751](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/751)) - Reduce release binary size ([#763](https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/issues/763))   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/sigoden/dufs/compare/v0.46.0...v0.47.0 
```

```
<details> <summary>正式版用户将获得 v2.11.3-preview ~ v2.11.4-preview 的所有改动, 点击展开查看</summary>  ✨新增 - 新增组件 `番剧动态稍后再看按钮`, 在动态页的番剧卡片上增加与原生功能一致的稍后再看按钮. (#5688, PR #5704 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `直播显示分区信息`, 在播放器标题栏显示当前直播间的分区. (PR #5705 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `直播自动切换最高画质`, 进入直播间会自动选择最高画质, 之后仍可手动切换. (#5638, PR #5706 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `仅搜索首页`, 首页只保留顶栏和中央搜索框, 并支持自定义背景. (PR #5659 by [Yimino](https://github.com/mintonight)) - 新增组件 `合集链接重定向`, 修正指向个人空间列表的合集链接路径和参数. (#5684, PR #5709 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `强制保留播放器控制栏`, 鼠标静止或移出播放器后仍保留细进度条等控制栏区域. (#5720, PR #5724 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - `弹幕合并器` 新增弹幕时停功能, 拖动进度后恢复时会写入偏移; 同时改进搜索、分 P 选择和管理操作. (#5647, #5650, PR #5701 by [XianYuDaXian](https://github.com/XianYuDaXian)) - 设置面板支持导入和导出单个组件的选项, 通用设置支持自定义导出文件名. (#5702, #5734, PR #5708, PR #5736 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - `快捷键扩展` 能够区分数字键盘、鼠标中键和侧键, 并新增屏蔽 B 站播放器原生快捷键的设置. (#5464) - `快捷键扩展 - 开关 CC 字幕` 支持配置字幕语言偏好, 并优先选择匹配的人工字幕. (#5685) - `视频快照` 适配更多页面, 调整按钮和预览样式, 并修复与 `视频预览放大` 的兼容问题. (PR #5707 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `自定义动态页内容宽度`, 自定义动态首页、动态详情、图文动态与专栏的正文宽度. (#5440, PR #5796 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `直播自动网页全屏`, 进入直播间时, 自动将播放器切换至网页全屏模式. (#965, PR #5748 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `启用直播截图`, 在播放器的音量按钮右边增加一个截图按钮. 装有 `快捷键扩展` 时支持键盘快捷键 <kbd>Ctrl</kbd>+<kbd>Alt</kbd>+<kbd>C</kbd>. (#5788, PR #5790 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `隐藏分区栏`, 隐藏主站首页及支持分区首页中 Banner 下方包含动态、热门、各分区及专栏、直播等入口的导航栏, 可自定义隐藏后保留的垂直间距. (#4075, PR #5783 by [ChiyukiKazama](https://github.com/ChiyukiKazama)) - 新增组件 `评论区评论排序`, 支持按点赞数、发布时间、用户等级对视频、番剧、动态、专栏等页面已加载的评论进行排序, 也可恢复默认顺序. (#3446, #5052, PR #5723 by [ChairKeter](https://github.com/ChairKeter)) - 新增组件 `自定义首页换一换数量`, 自定义首页“换一换”按钮每次请求的推荐条目数量, 默认 15 条, 广告等推荐条目也计入数量. (PR #5785 by [ChiyukiKazama](https://github.com/ChiyukiKazama)) - 新增组件 `新视频永远从头开始`, 点击首页视频卡片时移除 `t` 参数, 使新视频始终从头开始播放, 不受预览时长影响, 不影响已经看过的视频从上次离开位置继续. (#4766, PR #5716 by [Earchaut](https://github.com/Earchaut)) - 新增组件 `自定义弹幕切换行为`, 自定义使用快捷键或点击播放器弹幕按钮切换弹幕时, 在哪些状态之间循环; `快捷键扩展` 切换弹幕时显示对应状态提示. (#4743, #5777, #5782, PR #5787 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `滚轮调节音量`, 在播放器画面上滚动滚轮调节音量, 并可自定义每一格滚轮改变的音量. (#718, #4283, PR #5797 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 新增组件 `推荐视频打开方式`, 控制视频播放页右侧推荐栏的链接在当前标签页或新标签页打开, 并可显示反向操作图标. (#5036, PR #5738 by [RhoPaper](https://github.com/RhoPaper)) - `下载视频` 支持在合集输入源中区分多个子合集. (#5227, PR #5781 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 文件名格式新增 `user` 变量, 可使用 UP 主昵称. (#2134, #4658, PR #5771 by [WzLYVg387U](https://github.com/WzLYVg387U)) - `视频预览放大` 支持搜索页视频卡片. (#5759, PR #5764 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - `动态过滤` 的关键词列表支持折叠, 输入框移至列表上方, 新添加的关键词显示在最前面. (#5794, PR #5746 by [Acetab](https://github.com/Acetab))  🐛修复 - 修复 `直播弹幕助手 (+1 & 收藏)` 在弹幕悬停和全屏兼容模式下的 +1 发送问题. (PR #5728 by [oooolcepsed](https://github.com/cccccccccooool)) - 修复 `楼中楼回复“UP主觉得很赞”显示` 在深色 / 夜间模式下的样式问题. (PR #5718 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `视频快照` 图片跨域加载失败时预览不可用且错误提示不明确的问题. (PR #5737 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55))  </details>  🐛修复 - 修复 `删除广告` 在首页推荐流可能空白的问题. (PR #5804 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `动态分组过滤` 将已删除分组中的 UP 主持续过滤掉的问题. (#5812, PR #5813 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `隐藏首页轮播图` 导致推荐卡片错位的问题. (PR #5814 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `深色模式` 对 blackboard / 登录页面的适配. (PR #5829 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `快捷键扩展` 在原生长按加速调整速度时同时触发音量调整的问题. * [Milestone](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/milestones/1.30.2)  This is a second security and bug-fixing release in the continuation of [1.30.x](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/releases/tag/1.30.1).  Note that our rolling-release channel (*edge*) is recommended for faster security patches.  Bug fixes highlights 🐛: * Fix PHP 8.5+ deprecation warning with SQLite * Block `data:` URI scheme in some cases  Performance highlights 🏎️: * Reduce memory consumption of export  This release has been made by @Alkarex, @Inverle and newcomers @abp002, @Arslan-TR, @Emredinho61, @HawkForge, @lordmarrik, @spa77k, @tomac4t, @tpq2030  Full [changelog](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/blob/1.30.2/CHANGELOG.md):  * Security 	* SimplePie sanitizer: block `data:` URI scheme while keeping `data:image` for images [#9417](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9417) 	* Mitigate some 404 situations [#9435](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9435) 	* Do not log the Fever API key on authentication failure [#9468](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9468) 	* Fix local DNS leak when fetching feeds through a proxy [#9451](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9451) * Bug fixes 	* WebSub fix POST, fix warning, better message [#9420](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9420) 	* Fix PHP 8.5+ deprecation of `sqliteCreateFunction` for SQLite [#9421](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9421) 	* Preserve explicit sort overrides when searching [#9467](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9467) 	* Fix wrong order of args in extension compatibility check [#9445](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9445) * Performance 	* Reduce memory consumption of export [#9350](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9350) * Features 	* Better doc and log proxy cases [#9436](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9436) * Extensions 	* Update registerHook docs with newer PHP 8.1+ syntax for callables [#9443](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9443), [Extensions#548](https://github.com/FreshRSS/Extensions/pull/548) * UI 	* Disallow user page navigation while refreshing feeds [#9446](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9446) 	* Various UI and style improvements [#9426](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9426), [#9441](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9441) * I18n 	* Improve German [#9439](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9439) 	* Improve Polish [#9458](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9458) 	* Improve Turkish [#9440](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9440) * Misc. 	* Update to `GT\CssXPath` 1.6.0 [#9411](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9411) 	* CI/CD: Fix login to Docker Hub [#9433](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9433) 	* Fix SVGO zero percent filtering in CI [#9422](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9422) 	* Update dev dependencies [#9425](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9425)
```

### 2026-10-20T01:08:39Z [**miniflux/v2**](https://github.com/miniflux/v2)

```
 ### New features  - Added Gotify push notification support. - Added Steam scraper rules. - Added validation with helpful error messages when saving rewrite rules.  ### Performance improvements  - Reduced database storage used by enclosure and entry indexes. - Reduced memory used by internal data structures.  ### Security  - Blocked reserved IP address ranges to further protect against server-side request forgery. - Hardened WebAuthn redirects against open-redirect attacks, including redirect URLs containing surrounding whitespace. - Prevented per-feed proxy URLs from bypassing private-network restrictions by default. **Set `FETCHER_ALLOW_PRIVATE_FEED_PROXY=1` to retain access to private per-feed proxies.** - Enforced `DISABLE_LOCAL_AUTH` for API password authentication and password changes; API clients must use an API key when local authentication is disabled. - Rejected Google Reader authentication tokens when no password is configured. - Restricted embedded iframe URLs to web schemes and tightened YouTube hostname validation.  ### Bug fixes  - Added timeouts to the ACME challenge listener. - Fixed response compression negotiation to consistently prefer the server's configured encoding order. - Improved Karakeep errors when saving an entry fails by reporting the HTTP status. - Returned to the first unread page when marking entries as read leaves the current feed or category page empty. - Restored support for proxies configured through `HTTP_PROXY` or `HTTPS_PROXY`, including across redirects. - Returned a validation error instead of crashing when creating or importing feeds for users without a category. - Preserved the requested redirect after passkey login. - Increased the Google Reader item-ID limit to 10,000. - Returned a bad-request response instead of crashing on POST requests to static asset paths. - Improved accessibility by adding descriptive names to documentation links. - Improved error logging and responses for failed database existence checks and shared-entry lookups.  ### Localization  - Updated Polish (`pl_PL`) translations.  ### Documentation  - Clarified the Unix socket permissions used with `LISTEN_ADDR`.  ### Dependencies  - Updated several Go modules and GitHub Actions dependencies.  ---  As always, thank you to all contributors who helped improve Miniflux in this release. 
```

### 2026-10-14T09:52:45Z [**fatedier/frp**](https://github.com/fatedier/frp)

```
## Improvements  * Building frp from source now requires Go 1.26.0 or later.  ## Fixes  * Updated dependencies to fix SSH tunnel gateway channel deadlocks and panics caused by malformed challenges from NTLM-authenticated HTTP proxies. * Fixed VirtualNet routing issues during reconnection and shutdown, and excessive retries after repeated connection failures. * Health checks now reset the failure count after success, so `healthCheck.maxFailed` applies to consecutive failures. * Fixed control connections getting stuck after message write failures, which could block reconnection. * Fixed incorrect daily traffic statistics around daylight-saving transitions and skipped local midnights. * Fixed early-data loss in HTTP virtual-host CONNECT tunnels. Requests with a positive content length or transfer encoding now return HTTP 400. * Fixed work connections remaining open when server-side bandwidth limiting is enabled for TCP, HTTP, and UDP proxies. 
```
